### [1] [Hydra: Phase-Aware Workload Characterization of LLM Inference across Edge SoC Generations, Backends, and Quantization Levels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25053)
*Amir Taherin,Sana Taghipour Anvari,Charles Amante,Yixiao Chen,Ruben Noroian,Zlatan Feric,Nicolas Bohm Agostini,Pu Zhao,José Cano,Bin Ren,Yanzhi Wang,David Kaeli*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出了Hydra框架，用于边缘设备LLM推理的多维度性能表征，揭示了延迟、内存流量和能效之间的复杂关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘LLM部署受多种因素影响，传统仅关注模型大小和精度的评估方法无法揭示推理后端、硬件平台、内存流量和功耗管理对延迟和效率的影响。

Method: 开发了Hydra框架，通过统一的per-prompt时序模式对HuggingFace Transformers和llama.cpp进行插桩，将时间记录与硬件遥测数据融合，实现对prefill和decode阶段性能、资源利用率和能效的多维度表征。

Result: 在三代边缘SoC（AGX Xavier、Orin、Thor）、13个指令微调LLM、5种执行格式上收集了约107K条记录。发现：聚合延迟掩盖了关键部署效应，后端结构改变延迟引入位置，量化减少内存流量和能耗但不能单调预测功耗，SoC代际改变资源利用率和效率的解释方式。

Conclusion: Hydra通过连接阶段级时序与系统资源利用及能效指标，实现了可重现的、阶段感知的边缘LLM推理表征，为边缘部署优化提供了新洞察。

Abstract: Edge LLM deployment is shaped by more than model size and precision: inference backend, hardware platform, memory traffic, and power management all affect latency and efficiency. We present Hydra, a common-schema, phase-aware workload characterization framework for LLM inference on edge SoCs. Hydra instruments HuggingFace Transformers and llama.cpp with a shared per-prompt timing schema and fuses those records with hardware telemetry, enabling a multi-dimensional characterization of performance, system-resource utilization, and efficiency across prefill and decode phases. Using Hydra, we evaluate three consecutive edge System-on-Chip (SoC) generations (AGX Xavier, AGX Orin, and AGX Thor), 13 instruction-tuned LLMs from seven families, five execution formats, and consider input/output-length sensitivity. The resulting artifact contains roughly 107K per-prompt records and is publicly released with Hydra. Our analysis shows that aggregate latency alone hides key deployment effects: backend structure changes where latency is introduced, quantization reduces memory traffic and energy but does not predict power monotonically, and SoC generation changes how utilization and efficiency should be interpreted. By connecting phase-level timing with system-resource utilization and efficiency metrics, Hydra enables reproducible, phase-aware characterization of edge LLM inference. Hydra's source code and the collected per-prompt trace corpus are available open-source at: https://github.com/amirtaherin/hydra

</details>


### [2] [FLINT: Efficiently Leveraging High Bandwidth Flash for Capacity-Scalable LLM Inference Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25062)
*Geraldo F. Oliveira,Arash Tavakkol,Xiangyu Zhu,Ahmet Caner Yüzügüler,Vamanan Arulchelvan,Lukas Cavigelli,Renzo Andri,Mohammad Sadrosadati,Jia Xinglei,Onur Mutlu,Zhou Ke,Shai Bergman,Ji Zhang*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: FLINT是一种面向大语言模型推理的工作负载驱动型HBF(3D堆叠NAND闪存)架构，通过硬件突发缓冲控制器、幻影平面刷新机制和只读FTL三项创新，解决了现有方案在预取策略、刷新操作和闪存管理方面的关键瓶颈，实现了高效的大容量近加速器存储。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM推理 increasingly受限于加速器内存容量而非计算吞吐量，尤其在单加速器和小节点推理系统中限制更严重。HBF作为新兴3D堆叠NAND闪存技术可提供多太字节近加速器容量，是存储LLM权重的理想容量层。但现有基于HBF的方案面临三大采用挑战：依赖粗粒度静态预取、将NAND闪存管理任务暴露在关键推理路径上、以及错失针对工作负载行为优化闪存管理机制的机会。

Method: FLINT引入三项核心机制：(1)硬件突发缓冲控制器，动态合并和流水线化HBF读取操作，旨在利用现有NAND闪存缓冲区同时维持高HBF带宽；(2)幻影平面刷新机制，通过低成本资源复制将刷新相关NAND闪存操作移出读取前台，从而将刷新从关键推理路径中移除；(3)只读FTL，用紧凑表格替代SSD级别的任意写入支持，将逻辑权重突发转换为物理HBF位置。

Result: FLINT成功设计了一个将HBF作为内存容量层与HBM集成的有效HBF底层架构，解决了三大挑战：通过动态合并流水线化读取克服静态预取限制，通过幻影平面刷新消除关键路径上的刷新开销，通过只读FTL简化了闪存管理并专门针对LLM推理工作负载进行优化。

Conclusion: FLINT为容量可扩展的LLM推理提供了一个工作负载驱动的HBF底层架构，有效解决了HBF技术在LLM推理场景中的关键应用障碍，实现了将HBF集成到加速器内存层次结构的目标，为大模型部署提供了可行的容量扩展方案。

Abstract: LLM inference is increasingly constrained by accelerator memory capacity rather than compute throughput. This constraint is especially acute in single-accelerator and small-node inference systems, where limited on-package memory capacity restricts the size of deployable models. HBF is an emerging 3D-stacked NAND flash technology that provides multi-terabyte near-accelerator capacity, making it a promising capacity tier for storing LLM weights. However, existing HBF-based proposals face three adoption challenges: they (1) rely on coarse-grained static prefetching for LLM weights aiming to hide the microsecond-level read latency of the NAND flash device while maximizing HBF's read throughput, (2) expose NAND flash management tasks (e.g., refresh operations) to the accelerator-visible critical inference path, and (3) miss optimization opportunities to specialize and optimize the flash-management mechanisms to the workload behavior.
  Our goal is to design an efficient HBF substrate that integrates HBF as a memory-capacity tier alongside HBM while addressing these three challenges. To this end, we propose FLINT, a workload-driven HBF substrate for capacity-scalable LLM inference. FLINT introduces three mechanisms: (1) a hardware burst-buffer controller that dynamically coalesces and pipelines HBF reads aiming to utilize existing NAND flash buffers while sustaining high HBF bandwidth, (2) a phantom-plane refresh mechanism, which removes refresh from the critical inference path by moving refresh-related NAND flash operations outside the read foreground back via low-cost resource duplication, and (3) a read-only FTL, which replaces SSD-class support for arbitrary writes with a compact table that translates logical weight bursts to physical HBF locations.

</details>


### [3] [An Open-Source Benchmark Suite of 3D-IC Testcases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25155)
*Rohan Soni,Jooyeon Jeong,Alexander Graening,Anthony Foo,Richard Chen,Puneet Gupta*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个开源的3D-IC基准测试套件，基于CATCH框架中的芯粒案例研究，为3D异构集成物理设计研究提供了可复用的评估平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试套件仅适用于2D布局布线算法，无法捕捉TSV、混合键合和芯粒架构等新兴3D异构集成技术的物理设计挑战，且缺乏公开可访问且可扩展的3D基准测试集来支持可重现的算法评估。

Method: 基于CATCH开源框架中的代表性芯粒案例研究，构建开源3D-IC基准测试套件；定义涵盖计算、存储、I/O、模拟和基板组件的可复用虚拟芯粒模型；将异构裸片集成、裸片间互连及技术相关设计约束等关键物理设计特性编码到测试用例中。

Result: 成功构建并公开发布了开源3D-IC基准测试套件，提供可复用的虚拟芯粒模型，能够捕获3D系统的关键物理设计特征，支持多种3D物理设计问题的可重现评估。

Conclusion: 该基准测试套件为3D异构集成领域建立了统一的评估平台，有望通过促进可重现的研究评估，加速该领域的社区研究进展。

Abstract: The physical design community has benefited from standardized, publicly available benchmark suites, which have enabled reproducible evaluation and driven significant advances in 2D place-and-route algorithms over the past three decades. However, the emergence of 3D heterogeneous integration technologies, including through-silicon vias (TSVs), hybrid bonding, and chiplet-based architectures, has introduced new physical design challenges that are not captured by existing planar benchmarks. Although several 3D-IC design examples have been reported, publicly accessible and scalable benchmark suites that enable reproducible evaluation across different 3D physical design problems remain limited. In this paper, we present an open-source suite of 3D-IC benchmark testcases derived from representative chiplet-based case studies in CATCH, an open-source framework for estimating the cost of heterogeneous integration architectures. The proposed benchmark suite provides reusable virtual chiplet models covering compute, memory, I/O, analog, and substrate components. Each testcase captures essential physical design characteristics of 3D systems, including heterogeneous die integration, inter-die connectivity, and technology-dependent design constraints. By publicly releasing these benchmarks, we aim to establish a common evaluation platform and accelerate community-wide research progress in 3D heterogeneous integration.

</details>


### [4] [BOOSTEDSOSA: Accelerated Inferencing for Low Variance Stochastic Online Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25346)
*Adam H. Ross,Riccardo Revalor,Aryan Singh,Ayush Jain,Debjit Pal*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: BOOSTEDSOSA是一种双FPGA机器学习辅助调度架构，通过预测作业运行时间显著降低预测误差（MAE降低高达71.88%），相比用户估计提高调度准确性，同时实现17倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高性能计算系统中的异构调度面临重大挑战。随机在线调度加速器（SOSAs）虽然提供了有前景的解决方案，但其有效性因依赖用户提供的运行时间估计而受到损害。这些估计引入了显著的方差（平均MAE达数百核心日），导致竞争比率边界随运行时变异性增加而增大，削弱了随机在线调度算法的竞争力。

Method: 提出BOOSTEDSOSA双FPGA机器学习辅助调度架构，集成机器学习预测器用于预测期望处理时间，并采用新颖的时间感知训练策略。该预测器仅使用提交时可用的调度器参数来估计作业运行时间，使其能够在现有HPC系统中使用。

Result: 使用Argonne领导计算设施、MIT Supercloud和UIUC Blue Waters工作负载数据集的历史真实HPC作业数据进行验证。预测器相比用户运行时间估计降低MAE高达63.85%；加性训练策略相比静态模型降低MAE高达71.88%。端到端实现平均17倍加速（相比AVX优化的软件基线），处理速度高达1,711作业/秒。

Conclusion: BOOSTEDSOSA成功解决了SOSAs对用户运行时间估计依赖的关键限制，通过机器学习预测器显著提高了调度准确性，同时保持与现有HPC系统的兼容性，并实现了显著的性能加速，为随机在线调度在高性能计算环境中的实际应用提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Heterogeneous scheduling in stochastic, online envi- ronments, such as high-performance computing (HPC) systems, presents a significant challenge. Stochastic Online Scheduling Accelerators (SOSAs) offer a promising solution, but their effectiveness is compromised by a reliance on runtime estimates provided by users. These estimates introduce substantial vari- ance into the scheduling process (mean MAE in hundreds of Core-Days), thereby weakening the competitiveness of Stochastic Online Scheduling algorithms as their competitive-ratio bound increases with runtime variability.
  To address this limitation, we introduce BOOSTEDSOSA, a dual-FPGA ML-assisted Scheduling architecture that integrates a Machine Learning predictor for expected processing times, with a novel temporal-aware training policy. The predictor estimates job runtimes using only scheduler parameters available at submission time, enabling its use in existing HPC systems. Using historical real-world HPC job data (from the Argonne Leadership Comput- ing Facility, MIT Supercloud and UIUC Blue Waters workload datasets), we show that the predictor reduces MAE by up to 63.85% compared to user runtime estimates, and the additive training policy reduces MAE by up to 71.88% compared to a static model. End-to-end, BOOSTEDSOSA achieves an average 17x speedup over an AVX-optimized software baseline and processes up to 1,711 jobs/seconds

</details>


### [5] [APT: Accelerating Diffusion Transformers via Attention Probability-Guided Pruning and Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25380)
*Sungyeob Yoo,Seeyeon Kim,Joonyong Park,Seunghee Han,Joo-Young Kim*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出了APT，一个软硬件协同设计的扩散Transformer加速器，通过注意力概率引导的自适应双阈值算法和专用硬件架构，在高分辨率图像生成任务上相比NVIDIA A100实现最高8.16倍加速和14.98倍能效提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散Transformer(DiTs)虽已成为高分辨率图像/视频生成的最先进模型，但其自注意力机制的计算开销随输出分辨率呈四次方增长，导致严重延迟。现有的稀疏化和量化技术在高分辨率DiTs场景下效果有限，无法有效降低计算成本。

Method: 1. 算法层面：提出注意力概率引导的自适应双阈值(APDT)方法，动态进行元素选择和精度分配；2. 引入时间步感知FlashAttention(TAFA)，利用时间相似性预测注意力概率；3. 架构层面：协同设计专用加速器，支持不规则稀疏性和双精度执行，包含动态掩码管理、地址转换、双精度计算单元和分块数据流。

Result: 在PixArt-α、Stable Diffusion 3和FLUX等SOTA DiT模型上评估，相比NVIDIA A100实现最高8.16倍加速和14.98倍能效提升；相比现有扩散模型加速器EXION实现最高3.01倍加速和2.04倍能效提升。

Conclusion: 通过软硬件协同设计，APT成功解决了高分辨率DiTs的计算瓶颈，证明了注意力概率作为统一重要性度量指标在优化扩散模型计算方面的有效性，为高分辨率生成AI应用提供了高效的加速方案。

Abstract: Recent advances in generative AI have significantly increased the demand for high-resolution image and video generation, positioning diffusion models as a core technology. Among them, Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) have emerged as the state-of-the-art (SOTA) models due to their scalability and output quality. However, self-attention in DiTs incurs significant computational overhead, leading to excessively long latency as the complexity grows with the fourth power of the output resolution. While prior works have attempted to mitigate this cost using sparsity and quantization techniques, they fall short of effectively reducing the computational cost in high-resolution DiTs.
  In this paper, we present APT, a software-hardware co-designed accelerator for high-resolution DiTs. APT leverages attention probabilities as a unified importance metric to jointly optimize computation through fine-grained pruning and adaptive precision scaling. At the algorithm level, we propose Attention Probability-guided Adaptive Dual Thresholding (APDT), which dynamically performs element selection and precision assignment using dual thresholds. To ensure compatibility with memory-efficient FlashAttention, we introduce Timestep-Aware FlashAttention (TAFA), which predicts attention probabilities across timesteps by exploiting temporal similarity. At the architecture level, we co-design a specialized accelerator that efficiently supports irregular sparsity and dual-precision execution, featuring dynamic mask management, address translation, dual-precision compute units, and a tile-based dataflow. Finally, we evaluate APT on SOTA DiT models, including PixArt-$α$, Stable Diffusion 3, and FLUX. APT achieves up to 8.16$\times$ speedup and 14.98$\times$ higher energy efficiency over NVIDIA A100, and up to 3.01$\times$ speedup and 2.04$\times$ higher energy efficiency over EXION, a SOTA diffusion model accelerator.

</details>


### [6] [Syn2Logic: End-to-End Neuromorphic Design Automation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25536)
*Artur Podobas*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了电子神经形态设计自动化（eNDA）框架Syn2Logic，该框架通过自定义DSL和编译器将计算神经科学模型直接转换为可综合的RTL硬件，无需编写HDL代码。应用该框架实现了三个突破：最快的C. elegans加速器、性能超越传统求解器的神经形态数独求解器，以及在MNIST数据集上达到560万FPS/Watt的FPGA加速器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经科学研究与EDA传统流程之间存在鸿沟，神经科学家难以将其模型直接实现为硬件加速器。现有方法需要大量的硬件描述语言编程，限制了神经形态硬件的开发效率和创新能力。

Method: 提出电子神经形态设计自动化（eNDA）流程框架Syn2Logic，该框架包含：1）自定义领域特定语言（DSL）供神经科学家建模神经行为；2）编译器将模型描述直接转换为可综合的RTL硬件代码；3）完整的自动化设计流程，无需编写任何HDL代码。

Result: 成功应用eNDA流程实现了三个应用案例：1）创建了超越现有最先进模拟器速度的C. elegans加速器；2）开发了超越CP-SAT和SCIP求解器性能的神经形态数独求解器（在TOP1465谜题上）；3）在小型FPGA上实现了560万FPS/Watt的MNIST加速器，在速度和能效上均超越现有神经形态架构。

Conclusion: eNDA流程有效填补了计算神经科学建模与传统EDA流程之间的空白，使神经科学家能够专注于模型开发而无需具备硬件设计专业知识。实验结果表明该框架能够生成高性能、高能效的神经形态硬件加速器，证明了eNDA方法的实用价值和推广潜力。

Abstract: In this work, we propose a view on electronic Neuromorphic Design Automation (eNDA), which we see as a design automation flow that bridges computational neuroscience modeling with traditional Electronic Design Automation (EDA) flow. We introduce the term, give examples of how it can be implemented, and design a prototype implementation: Syn2Logic. Syn2Logic is an entire eNDA framework, that allows neuroscientists to model neural behavior using a custom DSL and a compiler that takes the same model description down to synthesizable RTL hardware. We end the paper by applying the eNDA-flow through Syn2Logic to show how to -- without writing a single line of hardware description language (HDL) code-- (i) generate what we believe is the fastest C. elegans accelerator that runs significantly faster than state-of-the-art simulators, (ii) create (to the best of our knowledge) the fastest, most generic neuromorphic sudoku solver that outperforms CP-SAT and SCIP on TOP1465 puzzles, and (iii) create a 5.6 million FPS/Watt accelerator on a tiny FPGA that outperforms existing neuromorphic architectures in terms of speed and energy-efficiency on the MNIST dataset.

</details>


### [7] [Integrated Hardware Annealing based on Langevin Dynamics for Ising Machines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26100)
*Yongchao Liu,Lianlong Sun,Michael Huang,Hui Wu*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于朗之万动力学的伊辛机硬件退火算法，通过随机噪声扰动避免局部最优，在65nm CMOS工艺下实现86.5%的基态到达概率，解质量提升97.5%，求解时间减少50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 伊辛机在求解组合优化问题时容易因复杂的能量景观陷入局部最优，硬件退火算法通过概率性方法缓解此问题，但现有方法在解质量和求解效率方面仍有提升空间。

Method: 提出基于朗之万动力学的硬件退火算法，引入随机噪声进行随机扰动；完成理论分析、系统级设计和详细电路设计；采用65nm CMOS工艺进行芯片级仿真验证。

Result: 所提算法以86.5%的概率引导系统到达基态，解质量显著提升97.5%；与现有硬件退火方法相比，解质量更优且求解时间减少50%。

Conclusion: 基于朗之万动力学的硬件退火算法有效解决了伊辛机的局部最优陷阱问题，在解质量和计算效率方面均显著优于现有方法，为伊辛机优化提供了有效的硬件实现方案。

Abstract: Ising machines are non-von Neumann machines designed to solve combinatorial optimization problems (COP) by searching for the ground state, or the lowest energy configuration, within the Ising model. However, Ising machines often face the challenges of getting trapped in local minima due to the complex energy landscapes. Hardware annealing algorithms help mitigate this issue by using a probabilistic approach to steer the system toward the ground state. In this paper, we present a hardware annealing algorithm for Ising machines based on Langevin dynamics, a stochastic perturbation by random noise. Theoretical analysis, system-level design, and detailed circuit design are carried out. We evaluate the performance of the algorithm through chip-level simulation using a standard 65-nm CMOS technology to demonstrate the algorithm's efficacy. The results show that the proposed hardware annealing algorithm effectively guides the system to reach the ground state with a probability of 86.5%, significantly improving the solution quality by 97.5%. Further, we compare the algorithm with state-of-the-art hardware annealing methods through behavioral-level simulations, highlighting its improved solution quality alongside a 50% reduction in time-to-solution.

</details>

### [8] [Dynamic Influence-Weighted Distillation for Single-IMU Activity Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24904)
*Bingxuan Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出动态影响加权（DIW）方法，利用训练时四个IMU传感器辅助，提升测试时仅使用右臂单IMU的活动识别性能，宏F1提升6.68-7.66个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多部位惯性传感器能提升活动识别准确率，但部署时要求佩戴所有传感器会增加负担。研究者希望利用训练时多个传感器的信息，改进测试时仅使用单传感器的模型性能。

Method: 提出动态影响加权（DIW）方法，通过冻结的四IMU教师模型提供逻辑值和特征目标。DIW在独立折内训练参与者上测试单步候选更新，为逻辑值损失和特征损失分配样本级门控权重，实现动态知识蒸馏。

Result: 在WEAR数据集上，宏F1从监督学习的0.561820和固定权重知识蒸馏的0.571623提升至0.638451，分别提升7.66和6.68个百分点。在19个标签中18个、22个留出参与者中21个上超越监督学习方法。

Conclusion: DIW成功将训练时的多位置传感器信息转化为更强的单IMU模型，在不改变部署传感方式或学生模型前向计算图的前提下，显著提升了活动识别性能。

Abstract: Inertial sensors at multiple body locations can improve activity recognition, but requiring every sensor at inference increases the deployment burden. We study whether four synchronized IMUs available during training can improve a student that uses only the right-arm IMU during fitting and inference. A frozen four-IMU teacher provides logit and feature targets. Fixed-weight knowledge distillation applies each target with the same strength to every fitting sample, although the student may not benefit equally from them. We introduce dynamic influence weighting (DIW), which tests a one-step candidate update on separate fold-internal training participants. DIW then assigns separate sample-wise gates to the logit and feature losses. On WEAR, we evaluate 19 labels and 68,298 complete windows from 22 participants using subject-disjoint five-fold cross-validation. Pooled out-of-fold macro-F1 is 0.561820 for Supervised and 0.571623 for Fixed-weight KD. DIW reaches 0.638451, gains of 7.66 and 6.68 percentage points, respectively. It exceeds Supervised for 18 of 19 labels and 21 of 22 held-out participants. All three routes retain the same 80,915-parameter right-arm student at inference. Under this protocol, DIW converts training-only multi-position information into a stronger single-IMU model without changing deployed sensing or the student forward graph.

</details>


### [9] [GreenLeaf Law Embed Tiny: A Compact Embedding Model for Legal Domain Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24936)
*Surya Saka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了GreenLeaf Law Embed Tiny，一个0.6B参数的法律领域嵌入模型，通过蒸馏和领域微调，在MLEB和MTEB(Law, v1)基准上分别达到75.11%和64.38%的性能，并支持多种量化部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了在资源受限的环境中实现高效的法律领域检索，同时保持竞争力性能，作者提出了一个小型化的专用嵌入模型。研究动机在于证明领域特定训练和高质量数据能够提升专用领域应用的性能。

Method: 采用两阶段训练流程：首先从大型教师模型蒸馏知识到紧凑的学生架构，然后应用领域特定微调和硬负样本挖掘。构建了包含340万查询-段落对的数据集（其中15万为跨法律管辖区的人工精选样本）。推理架构支持BF16、INT8和二值化等多种量化级别。

Result: GreenLeaf-Tiny在Massive Legal Embedding Benchmark (MLEB)上达到75.11%，在MTEB(Law, v1)上达到64.38%，在1B参数以下的模型中表现出竞争力。模型支持多种量化级别，适用于资源受限环境的部署。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，结合高质量数据的领域特定训练可以有效提升专用领域应用的性能。小型化模型通过精心设计的蒸馏和微调流程，能够在保持轻量化的同时实现具有竞争力的法律检索效果。

Abstract: We present GreenLeaf Law Embed Tiny, a 0.6B parameter embedding model for legal domain retrieval. GreenLeaf-Tiny achieves 75.11% on the Massive Legal Embedding Benchmark (MLEB) and 64.38% on MTEB(Law, v1),demonstrating competitive performance among models under 1B parameters. Our approach combines a two-stage training pipeline that first distills knowledge from a larger teacher model into a compact student architecture, then applies domain-specific fine-tuning with hard negative mining; a carefully curated dataset of 3.4 million query-passage pairs, including 150,000 human-curated samples across diverse legal jurisdictions; and an efficient inference architecture supporting multiple quantization levels (BF16, INT8, binary) enabling deployment in resource-constrained environments. We provide detailed analysis of our training methodology, architectural choices, and comprehensive evaluation across legal retrieval tasks. Our results demonstrate that domain-specific training with high-quality data can improve performance for specialized domain applications

</details>


### [10] [Multi-Modal Anomaly Detection: A Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24937)
*Xudong Mou,Zexin Wu,Chuan Luo,Shiru Chen,Xudong Liu,Chunming Hu,Renyu Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从假设驱动的视角综述了多模态异常检测(MMAD)领域，将现有方法分为正常性假设和异常性假设两种范式，并探讨了基础模型对该领域的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态异常检测在安全与可靠性关键应用中日益重要，但现有文献分散于不同领域和模态组合，且现有综述通常按架构而非异常定义与分离方式进行分类，缺乏统一的组织框架。

Method: 从假设驱动视角组织研究：(1)正式定义问题并识别五个内在特征；(2)将方法分为两类范式：正常性假设方法（通过表征学习、跨模态对齐和知识增强建模规律性）和异常性假设方法（通过粗粒度、结构和语义异常注入强化决策边界）；(3)研究基础模型如何通过可扩展预训练、灵活跨模态迁移和推理能力重塑该领域。

Result: 整理了各领域代表性基准和评估协议，识别了MMAD的核心挑战和内在特性，建立了基于假设驱动的统一分类框架，揭示了基础模型在该领域的变革性影响。

Conclusion: 强调了构建鲁棒、自适应且可解释的MMAD系统面临的开放性问题和未来方向，为该领域提供了系统性的研究路线图。

Abstract: Multi-Modal Anomaly Detection (MMAD) detects rare abnormal events from heterogeneous data sources and is increasingly used in safety- and reliability-critical applications such as industrial inspection and cybersecurity. Yet the literature is fragmented across domains and modality combinations, and existing surveys usually group methods by architecture rather than by how abnormality is defined and separated in multi-modal settings. We survey MMAD from an assumption-driven perspective. We formalize the problem, identify five intrinsic characteristics underlying its core challenges, and organize prior work into two complementary paradigms. The first, normality-assumption methods, models regularity via representation learning, cross-modal alignment, and knowledge enhancement. The second, anomaly-assumption methods, sharpens decision boundaries through coarse-grained, structural, and semantic anomaly injection. We also investigate how foundation models are reshaping MMAD through scalable pretraining, flexible cross-modal transfer, and emerging reasoning capabilities. Finally, we compile representative benchmarks and evaluation protocols across domains and highlight open problems and future directions for robust, adaptive, and interpretable MMAD systems.

</details>


### [11] [ExFold: Unified Expert Folding for Training-Free MoE Prefill-Decode Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24938)
*Juntong Wu,Yifei Liu,Junyi Chen,Siqi Fan,Chaoran Feng,Minghao Li,Liujie Zhang,Weihang Chen,Li Yuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ExFold是一种无需训练的统一专家折叠框架，通过将排除专家的贡献投影到保留专家上，同时加速MoE模型的预填充和解码阶段，在保持约99%质量的同时实现最高1.41倍TTFT和2.45倍TPOT加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE模型的服务存在两个具有根本不同瓶颈的推理阶段：预填充受限于逐token专家计算，解码受限于批次激活专家集的内存流量。现有训练无关加速方法仅优化单一资源代理（逐token执行的专家或批次激活的专家），要么丢弃排除专家的贡献，要么仅隐式近似，无法同时优化两个阶段。

Method: 提出ExFold框架，将预填充和解码统一为有预算的输出近似问题：仅执行阶段特定的约束专家集，同时使用校准的标量投影器将预算排除专家的贡献投影到保留专家上。观察到许多专家输出在方向上对齐但幅度不同，因此在无标签数据上校准成对标量投影矩阵。预填充加速为token级Top-K折叠，解码加速为批次级专家池折叠，两者共享同一折叠机制。

Result: 在vLLM中实现即插即用的ExFold插件，配备轻量级专家折叠CUDA内核，实现最高1.41倍TTFT（Time To First Token）和2.45倍TPOT（Time Per Output Token）加速，同时保持约99%的原始平均质量。

Conclusion: ExFold成功统一了MoE预填充和解码的加速问题，通过共享的专家折叠机制有效恢复排除专家的贡献，为MoE模型低延迟服务提供了一种高效、无需训练的解决方案。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models scale capacity for strong quality while keeping per-token compute bounded through sparse expert activation. Yet low-latency MoE serving is increasingly challenging, because it spans two inference phases with fundamentally different bottlenecks: prefill is dominated by token-wise expert computation, whereas decode is constrained by memory traffic from the batch-wise activated expert set. However, existing training-free acceleration methods optimize only a single resource proxy, either the experts each token executes or the experts a batch activates, and either discard the excluded experts' contribution or leave it only implicitly approximated. In this paper, we propose ExFold, a unified training-free expert-folding framework for jointly accelerating MoE prefill and decode. ExFold casts both prefill and decode as one budgeted output-approximation problem: execute only a phase-specific constrained expert set while projecting the contribution of budget-excluded experts onto retained experts using calibrated scalar projectors. Motivated by the observation that many expert outputs are directionally aligned but differ in magnitude, ExFold calibrates a pairwise scalar-projector matrix on unlabeled data and uses it at inference time to fold excluded expert contributions into retained experts. Under this view, prefill acceleration becomes token-level Top-K folding, and decode acceleration becomes batch-level expert-pool folding. The two phases differ only in how retained experts are selected, while excluded contributions are recovered by one shared folding mechanism. We implement ExFold as a plug-and-play plugin in vLLM, with a lightweight expert-folding CUDA kernel, delivering up to 1.41x TTFT and 2.45x TPOT speedups while retaining about 99% of the original average quality.

</details>


### [12] [When Does Frequency Decomposition Benefit Physics-Informed Neural Networks? A Preliminary Ablation Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24940)
*Shubham Rai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出双分支谱门控架构(DBSG-PINN)，发现频率分解对频谱复杂的多尺度PDE问题显著提升精度(最高降59.2%误差)，但对平滑单尺度问题收益有限，揭示了谱技术并非普适有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PINNs存在谱偏置问题(低频成分学习更快)，现有改进技术(傅里叶特征嵌入、正弦激活)缺乏对不同频谱区域实际收益的系统性验证，研究旨在明确频率分解技术对哪类PDE问题真正有效。

Method: 提出双分支谱门控架构，通过自适应门控将低频和高频成分分离到不同子网络；在5个一维基准PDE上执行受控消融实验，覆盖从平滑单尺度到振荡多尺度问题。

Result: 频率分解对频谱复杂问题最有效(多模态波问题降59.2%相对L2误差)，平滑PDE收益甚微；1D Wave问题表现不如固定组合变体；门控优势与目标解的频谱丰富度正相关，多尺度问题优势最大。

Conclusion: 谱技术有效性依赖PDE频谱特性，非普适有效；本研究基于单随机种子，为探索性研究，需更多种子和基准测试验证模式普适性，并提出需进一步研究的问题。

Abstract: Partial differential equations (PDEs) often have high-frequency and multi-scale features that neural networks struggle to approximate. Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) build the governing equations directly into training, but suffer from spectral bias: they learn low-frequency components faster than high-frequency ones. Techniques such as Fourier feature embeddings and sinusoidal activations address this, but most studies assume they help across the board without checking which spectral regimes actually benefit. We introduce a dual-branch, spectrally-gated architecture (DBSG-PINN) that splits low- and high-frequency components into separate subnetworks joined by an adaptive gate, and use it to run a partially controlled ablation of frequency decomposition and spectral routing. We test this on five one-dimensional benchmark PDEs, ranging from smooth, single-scale problems to oscillatory, multi-scale ones. Frequency decomposition helps most on the spectrally complex benchmarks, cutting relative $L_2$ error by up to $59.2\%$ on a multimodal wave problem, but gives little benefit on smoother PDEs. On one benchmark (1D Wave), it performs substantially worse than a simpler fixed-combination variant. The gate's benefit scales with how spectrally rich the target solution is: the full model's advantage over the ablations is largest on multi-scale benchmarks and smallest (or negative) on single-scale ones, consistent with the gate exploiting frequency structure rather than acting as noise,though we do not directly visualize or quantify its spatial activations in this study. All results come from a single training seed across five 1D benchmarks, so we present this as an exploratory study meant to raise questions rather than answer them, and outline the additional seeds and benchmarks needed to test whether the pattern holds.

</details>


### [13] [FAMPWQ: Fisher Information-based Adaptive Mixed Precision Weight Quantization for Effective LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24945)
*Gongwei Lee,Ji Liu,Juncheng Jia,Ji Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FAMPWQ方法，利用Fisher信息度量层间量化敏感度，并通过强化学习实现自适应混合精度权重量化，显著提升LLM在资源受限设备上的推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在多领域取得显著成就，但资源需求过高限制了在资源受限设备上的部署；传统量化方法采用统一位宽或简单启发式敏感度评估，导致性能严重下降。

Method: 1. 提出基于Fisher信息的度量指标，测量各层对量化的敏感度；2. 设计强化学习位宽分配器，根据Fisher信息敏感度指标生成自适应位宽分配策略，实现层级自适应权重量化。

Result: 在7个模型和5个基准测试上验证，FAMPWQ在PPL（降低达3.39）、准确率（提升达6.87%）和LLM-as-a-judge对比（胜率达76%）方面显著优于7种基线方法。

Conclusion: FAMPWQ通过Fisher信息驱动的自适应混合精度量化，有效解决了传统量化方法的性能下降问题，实现了LLM在商用GPU上的高效推理。

Abstract: Recent years have witnessed remarkable achievements of Large Language Models (LLMs) in multiple domains, while the excessive resource requirements of LLMs hinder the deployment on resource-constrained devices. Although model quantization stands out as an effective approach, conventional quantization approaches typically incur severe performance degradation due to uniform bit-width or simple heuristic sensitivity evaluation. In this paper, we propose a novel Fisher information-based Adaptive Mixed Precision Weight Quantization approach, i.e., FAMPWQ, which performs layer-adaptive weight quantization for effective LLM inference on commodity GPUs. First, we propose a system model with a novel Fisher information metric to measure the layer-wise sensitivity to quantization. Second, we propose a reinforcement learning-based bit-width allocator in FAMPWQ, which generates an adaptive bit-width allocation strategy based on the Fisher information sensitivity metric. Extensive experiments on 7 models and 5 benchmarks demonstrate that FAMPWQ significantly outperforms 7 baseline approaches in terms of PPL (up to 3.39 smaller), accuracy (up to 6.87% higher), and LLM-as-a-judge comparison (up to 76% win rate).

</details>


### [14] [MacroAgent: Regularity-Aware Macro Legalization with LLM-Agent-Designed Contour Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24946)
*Jiaxi Jiang,Xufeng Yao,Yuxuan Zhao,Yuntao Lu,Peiyu Liao,Zuodong Zhang,Yibo Lin,Bei Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MacroAgent框架，利用大语言模型发现宏单元合法化的启发式算法，实现2-8倍布局规整度提升、5%线长减少和更好的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代VLSI设计中宏单元占据核心区域，其位置显著影响最终QoR。现有宏单元合法化方法存在鲁棒性不足、计算成本高、忽略宏单元间规整性等问题，亟需一种能够兼顾规整性与效率的解决方案。

Method: 四阶段框架：聚类、轮廓生成、模板匹配和簇间细化。创新性地利用LLM发现多个有效的启发式规整感知轮廓算法，生成鲁棒且有效的宏单元合法化算法方案。

Result: 在TILOS和Chipyard基准测试上：布局规整度提升2-8倍，全局布线后线长减少3-5%（拥塞度相当），鲁棒性显著提升。端到端评估显示：相比DREAMPlace基线，布线后线长降低2.9%，TNS改善68.3%；集成到Innovus流程中线长降低1.8%。

Conclusion: MacroAgent框架成功生成鲁棒有效的宏单元合法化算法，规整性改进转化为实际PPA收益，在可接受运行时间内实现了优于现有方法性能，验证了LLM辅助算法发现在EDA领域的有效性。

Abstract: Macros constitute a large part of the core area in modern very large-scale integration (VLSI) designs. Moreover, macro positions have a significant impact on the final quality of result (QoR), and macro legalization is typically the final step in determining the macro positions. However, existing approaches related to macro legalization either lack robustness or incur substantial computational costs or neglect the regularity between macros. To address these limitations, we introduce MacroAgent. The novel framework is a four-stage approach: clustering, contour generation, template matching, and inter-cluster refinement. We propose leveraging Large Language Models (LLMs) to discover multiple, effective heuristic regularity-aware contour algorithms. This framework successfully generates robust and effective algorithmic solutions for macro legalization. Compared with state-of-the-art macro legalization works, experimental results on TILOS and Chipyard benchmarks demonstrate a 2 to 8 fold improvement in layout regularity, a 3% to 5% reduction in routed wirelength with comparable congestion after global routing, and significantly better robustness with an acceptable runtime. Furthermore, end-to-end evaluation through Cadence Innovus place-and-route confirms that the regularity improvements translate into tangible PPA gains, including 2.9% lower routed wirelength and 68.3% TNS improvement over the DREAMPlace macro legalization baseline; it also achieves 1.8% lower routed wirelength when integrated into the Innovus macro placement flow.

</details>


### [15] [CAT-GS: Balanced Multimodal Learning via Calibrated Gating and Fusion Surgery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24947)
*Mahir Shahriar Tamim,Sharjil Khan,Md. Samiul Alim,Tanvir Ahmed Khan,Shafin Rahman,Nabeel Mohammed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CAT-GS优化控制器，通过动态校准和融合干预解决多模态训练中的模态不平衡、不稳定门控和融合干扰三大失效问题，在多个基准上提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端多模态神经网络训练存在三种耦合的失效模式导致学习退化：(i)模态不平衡——某分支主导梯度优化；(ii)不稳定门控——噪声置信度诱导模态选择混乱；(iii)融合干扰——模态特定梯度在共享融合层产生冲突。这些不稳定的神经动力学问题亟需解决。

Method: 提出CAT-GS（校准自适应阈值门控与融合手术）神经动力学优化控制器，在反向传播过程中无需修改模型架构、融合模块或任务损失即可工作。核心技术包括：(1)通过温度缩放和EMA平滑校准教师导出的可靠性；(2)使用边际阈值策略在热身dropout、弱模态优先化和弱偏向混合间切换以稳定神经动力学；(3)通过梯度预算重归一化稳定激进门控下的梯度幅度；(4)应用融合专用PCGrad减少主共享瓶颈处的破坏性跨模态干扰。

Result: 在音频-视觉多模态基准（CREMA-D、AV-MNIST、VGGSound）、三模态场景（UR-FUNNY）、受控合成数据（CG-MNIST）及跨域基准（AVE、CMU-MOSI）上验证。CAT-GS相比OGM-GE、G²D、UMT等强基线在所有设置上改善或匹配融合多模态准确率，并产生更平滑的门控行为和更少的冲突融合梯度。

Conclusion: CAT-GS通过神经动力学视角统一解决了多模态训练中的三大耦合失效问题，无需修改模型结构即可有效提升融合性能和训练稳定性，为智能计算应用提供了一种实用的优化控制方案。

Abstract: End-to-end training of multimodal neural networks often exhibits unstable neural dynamics characterized by three coupled failure modes that degrade learning: (i) modality imbalance, where one branch dominates gradient-based optimization; (ii) unstable gating, where noisy confidence cues induce erratic modality selection; and (iii) fusion interference, where modality-specific gradients conflict at the shared fusion layer. We propose CAT-GS (Calibrated, Adaptive, Thresholded Gating with Fusion Surgery), a neural dynamics-based optimization controller for intelligent computing applications. CAT-GS operates during backpropagation without modifying model architectures, fusion modules, or task losses. Through calibration of teacher-derived reliability via temperature scaling and EMA smoothing, CAT-GS stabilizes neural dynamics using a margin-thresholded policy to switch between warm-up dropout, weak-modality prioritization, and weak-biased blending, stabilizes gradient magnitudes under aggressive gating via capped gradient-budget renormalization, and applies fusion-only PCGrad to reduce destructive cross-modal interference at the primary shared bottleneck. We evaluate CAT-GS on audio--visual multimodal pattern recognition benchmarks (CREMA-D, AV-MNIST, and VGGSound), a tri-modal setting (UR-FUNNY), controlled synthetic data (CG-MNIST), and additional cross-domain benchmarks (AVE and CMU-MOSI). CAT-GS improves or matches fused multimodal accuracy against strong imbalance-aware baselines (including OGM-GE, G$^2$D, and UMT) across settings, and yields smoother gating behavior with fewer conflicting fusion gradients.

</details>


### [16] [Beyond Scaling: Self-Evolving LLM Agents for Hardware Kernel Optimization via an Experience-Driven Workflow and Experience Graph Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25570)
*Siyuan Chen,Runlin Hou,Shenxiu Wu,Yansong Sun,Junming Cao,Yiyu Zhang,Shudi Shao,Junhao Qiu,Zhichao Lu,Qingfu Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出KOPE框架，通过经验图记忆和主动上下文管理实现硬件内核优化的经验积累与复用，在相同条件下性能提升1.54倍，通过率提升至84.6%，token消耗减少92%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硬件内核优化需要反复编译、测试、分析和修改。虽然LLM代理可以自动化部分流程，但现有方法无法从已完成的优化运行中学习——代理很少保留决策、执行反馈及后续使用该证据的决策；而保留所有历史轨迹又因上下文限制而不可行。

Method: 提出KOPE框架：1）经验图记忆(Experience Graph Memory)记录优化轨迹、正确性和性能反馈，保留决策顺序、观察结果和替代分支；2）主动上下文管理与注入(Active Context Management and Injection)在固定token预算下检索相关经验。

Result: 在GLM-5.2设置下：几何平均加速比是CANNBot的1.54倍；主动上下文管理将通过率从60.0%提升至84.6%，正域几何均值从0.0382提升至0.0661，token消耗从15.9B降至1.113B；经验图记忆将全套通过率从55.2%提升至84.6%，有效时序比较上获得1.43倍几何平均加速。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，在基础模型保持固定的情况下，通过外部经验可以实现持续优化，验证了经验驱动的硬件内核优化方法的有效性。

Abstract: Hardware kernel optimization requires repeated compilation, correctness testing, profiling, and revision. LLM agents can automate parts of this process, and stronger foundation models, longer context windows, and longer execution horizons have improved optimization within individual tasks. These advances alone do not enable an agent to learn from completed optimization runs. Existing kernel-optimization agents seldom preserve a decision, its observed execution feedback, and the later decisions that use that evidence. Retaining every prior trajectory is also impractical because an expanding history competes with the current task for context. We present KOPE, an experience-driven framework for hardware kernel optimization. KOPE records optimization trajectories with correctness and performance feedback in Experience Graph Memory, then uses Active Context Management and Injection to retrieve relevant experience under a fixed token budget. The graph retains decision order, observed outcomes, and alternative branches, allowing evidence collected on the target hardware to inform later optimization steps and tasks. Under the same GLM-5.2 setting, the geometric mean of KOPE's per-operator speedups is $1.54\times$ that of CANNBot, the strongest competing baseline. In a complete 53-operator ablation, Active Context Management and Injection raises pass rate from 60.0\% to 84.6\%, increases the evaluator-reported positive-field geometric mean from 0.0382 to 0.0661, and reduces optimization token consumption from 15.9B to 1.113B tokens relative to passive agent-led context construction. Enabling Experience Graph Memory raises full-suite pass rate from 55.2\% to 84.6\% and yields a $1.43\times$ geometric-mean speedup on valid timing comparisons. These results support continual optimization through external experience while the foundation model remains fixed.

</details>


### [17] [Demystifying Reinforcement Learning Post-Training of Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24949)
*Donovan Clay,Saket Gollapudi,Sankar Harilal,Min Jang,Jacob Morrison,Sewoong Oh,Natasha Jaques*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文解析了大语言模型强化学习后训练算法的内部机制，通过控制实验揭示了奖励信号粒度、提示分布多样性和模型规模等因素如何影响训练效果，为NLP研究者提供了理解和应用RL的理论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管强化学习后训练在提升大语言模型的推理、数学和编程能力方面表现出色，但对许多研究者而言，其背后的经典强化学习原理仍是一个"黑盒"。本文旨在解构RL后训练算法，阐明各步骤的内在机制，帮助NLP社区理解并有效应用这一技术工具。

Method: 在受控的简化环境中隔离可验证奖励强化学习的机制，使用策略输出分布的熵作为分析视角，对比预训练、监督微调(SFT)和强化学习后训练各阶段对模型确定性分布的影响，系统考察基座模型的先验分布、奖励信号粒度、提示分布多样性和模型规模等因素的交互作用。

Result: 研究发现："虚假奖励"的影响取决于后训练时使用的提示分布；RL后训练的成功取决于基座模型是否已为期望行为分配足够的概率质量，这与经典强化学习中的探索概念相关联；各训练阶段对模型确定性分布的塑造方式存在显著差异，这些选择之间的交互作用共同决定了后训练的成败。

Conclusion: 本文为NLP社区提供了一份RL后训练的入门指南，揭示了算法背后的原理和关键影响因素，帮助研究者在将强化学习作为工具时能够做出更明智的设计选择，推动该技术在语言模型能力提升中的有效应用。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) post-training has emerged as a powerful framework for enhancing the capabilities of large language models (LLMs), enabling impressive reasoning, math, and coding capabilities. Yet for many researchers and practitioners, the principles behind classical RL remain a "black box". In this work, we deconstruct the RL post-training algorithm, investigating each step to clarify what is actually happening beneath the surface. By isolating the mechanics of RL with Verifiable Rewards in a controlled and simplified environment, we examine how RL outcomes are shaped by the base model's prior distribution, the granularity of the reward signal, the diversity of the prompt distribution, and model scale. We use the entropy of the policy's output distribution as a lens to compare the distributions learned through pretraining, SFT, and RL post-training, revealing how each stage shapes model certainty. Our investigation sheds light on how these choices interact to affect post-training success. For example, we show that the effect of so-called 'spurious rewards' depends on the prompt distribution used for post-training. We also provide insight into why the success of RL post-training depends on whether the base model already places sufficient probability mass on the desired behavior, linking it to the classical concept of exploration in RL. Ultimately, we provide this primer as a resource to those in the NLP community wishing to incorporate RL as a tool in their toolbox.

</details>


### [18] [Resilient Decentralized Wireless Federated Learning via Gradient Tracking with AdamW](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25535)
*Nguyen Van Thieu,Ti Ti Nguyen,Ons Aouedi,Vu Nguyen Ha,Symeon Chatzinotas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了QEF-GT-AdamW算法，一种面向无线通信网络的通信高效且抗中断的去中心化学习方法，通过梯度跟踪、AdamW优化和双流有偏量化等技术，在带宽、功率和时隙受限的不可靠无线环境中实现了更好的收敛性能和精度-通信权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无线物联网边缘网络中的去中心化学习面临两大挑战：异构本地数据（非IID）和受限的无线通信链路（严格的空时预算、衰落信道和丢包）。现有去中心化优化方案在这些约束条件下往往产生大量通信开销并导致性能退化，需要一种能够同时应对数据异构性和通信不可靠性的解决方案。

Method: 提出QEF-GT-AdamW算法，集成了三个关键技术：（1）梯度跟踪技术以缓解非IID数据的影响；（2）基于AdamW的自适应优化以提高训练稳定性；（3）双流有偏量化配合误差反馈机制，减少模型和跟踪信息交换的通信负载。针对不可靠广播通信，采用本地回退策略处理数据包接收失败情况。论文显式建模了带宽、发射功率、空时约束和衰落信道对去中心化学习性能的影响。

Result: 在异构MNIST和CIFAR-10数据集上的实验结果表明，QEF-GT-AdamW相比代表性去中心化学习基线方法具有更好的鲁棒性和收敛性能，在有限无线资源条件下实现了良好的精度-通信权衡，能够在压缩和不可靠无线通信环境下稳定工作。

Conclusion: QEF-GT-AdamW算法成功解决了无线通信环境下去中心化学习的核心挑战，通过理论分析和实验验证证明了其在压缩和不可靠通信条件下的收敛性保证，为资源受限的无线物联网边缘网络提供了一种实用的去中心化学习解决方案。

Abstract: Wireless Internet-of-Things (IoT) edge networks require decentralized learning (DecL) methods that can operate reliably under both heterogeneous local data and communication-constrained wireless links. However, existing decentralized optimization schemes often incur substantial communication overhead and degraded performance when transmissions are constrained by strict airtime budgets, fading channels, and packet losses. This paper proposes QEF-GT-AdamW, a communication-efficient and outage-resilient algorithm for DecL over wireless communication (WCom) networks. The proposed method combines gradient tracking to mitigate the effect of non-IID data, AdamW-based adaptive optimization to improve training stability, and dual-stream biased quantization with error feedback to reduce communication payloads for both model and tracking exchanges. To address unreliable broadcast communication, the proposed framework further employs a local fallback strategy when scheduled packets are not successfully received. We explicitly model the effect of bandwidth, transmit power, airtime constraints, and fading channels on DecL performance, and establish convergence guarantees for the proposed algorithm under compressed and unreliable wireless communication. Experimental results on heterogeneous MNIST and CIFAR-10 settings show that QEF-GT-AdamW consistently improves robustness and convergence performance over representative DecL baselines while achieving favorable accuracy-communication trade-offs under limited wireless resources.

</details>


### [19] [AFDBench: A Reasoning-First AI Scientist for NationalWeather Service Forecast Discussions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24954)
*Manmeet Singh,Somnath Luitel,Prabhjot Singh,Manraaj Banga,Naveen Sudharsan,Josh Durkee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了AFDBench，首个用于评估生成式气象推理的基准，并使用GRPO强化学习方法显著提升了LLM在专业气象文本生成方面的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在生成高影响力的气象文本时会产生数值幻觉，这对气象传播构成风险。现有开源LLM在专业气象写作风格和数据保真度方面表现不佳，无法满足专业气象预报的严格要求。

Method: 1) 构建AFDBench基准，包含7,732份专家撰写的NWS讨论文档及配对的AI气象预报数据；
2) 提出三个评估指标：Met-Align（数值准确性）、Style-Align（专业语域遵循度）、Input-Grounding（源数据保真度）；
3) 应用Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO)强化学习方法，结合领域特定奖励（温度准确性、天气形势正确性、格式合规性）。

Result: 在1,033个来自两个未见NWS办公室的保留样本上，GRPO将Style-Align从0.318提升至0.619（接近翻倍），Input-Grounding从0.881提升至0.940，成功使7B参数模型学会专业气象学家写作风格并忠实解读AI气象数据。

Conclusion: 研究表明强化学习能有效提升LLM在专业气象文本生成任务中的表现，AFDBench为评估生成式气象推理提供了首个标准化基准，为构建可靠的AI气象预报系统奠定了基础。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) hallucinate numerical values when generating high-stakes meteorological text, posing risks for weather communication. We present AFDBench, an AI meteorologist that generates professional Area Forecast Discussions (AFDs) by reasoning through structured AI weather forecast data from Google's WeatherNext 2. We introduce AFDBench, the first benchmark for evaluating generative meteorological reasoning, comprising 7,732 expert written discussions from 13 National Weather Service (NWS) offices paired with real AI weather forecast inputs, and three complementary metrics: Met-Align (numerical accuracy), Style-Align (professional dialect adherence), and Input-Grounding (fidelity to source weather data). Zero-shot evaluations reveal that open-source LLMs achieve low Style-Align (~0.33) and moderate Input-Grounding (~0.88), failing to write in the professional NWS register or faithfully use their input data. We apply Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) with domain-specific rewards targeting temperature accuracy, synoptic correctness, and format compliance. On 1,033 held-out samples from two unseen NWS offices, GRPO nearly doubles Style-Align from 0.318 to 0.619 and improves Input-Grounding from 0.881 to 0.940, demonstrating that reinforcement learning teaches a 7B-parameter model to write like a professional meteorologist and faithfully interpret AI weather data.

</details>


### [20] [Cooperative Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning for Adaptive Aggregation in Semi-Supervised Federated Learning with non-IID Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25794)
*Rene Glitza,Luca Becker,Rainer Martin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出pFedMARL方法，利用多智能体强化学习（MARL）动态调整联邦学习聚合策略，有效应对非独立同分布（non-IID）数据异构性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习在处理异构、非独立同分布（non-IID）客户端数据分布时面临挑战，导致全局模型性能次优且存在偏差。现有方法难以动态适应复杂的数据分布变化。

Method: 采用基于TD3（Twin Delayed Deep Deterministic Policy Gradient）的多智能体强化学习方法。服务器端智能体动态调整客户端贡献以优化全局模型鲁棒性，客户端智能体平衡全局与局部更新实现有效个性化，且无需预训练。

Result: 在训练半监督音频频谱Transformer时，pFedMARL在多种non-IID场景及存在对抗性客户端情况下，性能匹配或超越FedAvg、Ditto和本地训练方法。实验证明该方法提升了准确性、鲁棒性和公平性。

Conclusion: pFedMARL通过动态适应聚合策略有效解决了联邦学习中的数据异构性问题，在真实部署场景中具有实用价值，为非IID环境下的联邦学习提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) enables distributed training of machine learning models while preserving data privacy. However, FL struggles with heterogeneous, non-IID client data distributions, resulting in sub-optimal and biased global models. In this paper, we propose pFedMARL, a novel approach leveraging Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning (MARL) with Twin Delayed Deep Deterministic Policy Gradient (TD3) to dynamically adapt aggregation strategies in FL settings. Our method employs a server-side agent adjusting client contributions to optimize global model robustness and client-side agents balancing global and local updates to personalize models effectively without pre-training. We demonstrate superior performance of pFedMARL for training a semi-supervised audio spectrogram transformer, matching or outperforming FedAvg, Ditto, and local training approaches across multiple non-IID scenarios and in the presence of adversarial clients. Our results indicate that pFedMARL actively improves accuracy, robustness, and fairness, making it suitable for real-world deployments.

</details>


### [21] [Why and When Neural Networks Improve Local Approximation in Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24963)
*Chengkuo Bian,Pengcheng Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 神经网络代理在无导数优化中的效果矛盾可通过三个关键因素解释：角色（辅助vs替代）、半径（模型可靠范围）和空间（基础方法剩余优化能力）。实验表明，辅助型代理将求解成功率从67提升至84，而替代型降至65。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献中神经网络代理在无导数优化中的效果相互矛盾：同一模型家族对某些求解器能减少评估次数，对另一些却无效果甚至更差。这种矛盾现象缺乏理论解释，无法确定何时何地学习到的局部模型真正有效。

Method: 提出三个决定性因素框架：(1)角色因素：代理作为候选提议者（辅助）还是梯度替代者；(2)半径因素：模型仅在有限邻域内可靠，误差随邻域变化；(3)空间因素：代理仅能加速基础方法仍有能力取得的进展。形式化半径感知的局部泛化概念，关联经典的全线性条件，在固定代理类、训练流程和基础方法下逐一验证各因素。

Result: 在117个基准实例上：辅助型代理将高精度求解实例从67增至84，替代型降至65；移除训练损失中的梯度项使代理接受率从0.703降至0.148；1000组配对比较显示无噪声阈值，仅基础方法提前停止。相同因素限制增益：基于模型的信赖域求解器（空间小）从88降至86，插值软件保持103领先；蒙特卡洛库存模型中修复接受接口价值10.40成本单位，而代理本身为0.00。

Conclusion: 代理效果矛盾源于忽视了角色、半径和空间三因素，而非训练曲线报告的拟合精度。辅助而非替代的代理设计、考虑半径可靠性的局部泛化、以及评估基础方法剩余优化空间，是成功应用神经网络代理的关键原则。

Abstract: Published experience with neural surrogates in derivative-free optimisation is contradictory: the same family of models that cuts the evaluation count of one solver leaves another unchanged, or makes it worse. We show that the contradiction dissolves once three factors are stated, and that these, rather than the fit accuracy a training curve reports, are what delimit when a learned local model pays. Role: a surrogate that proposes candidates the true objective must still approve helps, while one that replaces a gradient the solver depends on hurts. Radius: a model fitted to an optimisation path is reliable only inside a bounded neighbourhood, and its error neither vanishes as that neighbourhood shrinks nor survives its growth. Room: a surrogate can only accelerate progress the base method is still able to make. We formalise radius-aware local generalisation, relate it to the classical fully linear condition, and test each factor with the surrogate class, training pipeline and base method held fixed. Over 117 benchmark instances safeguarded assistance raises the instances solved to high accuracy from 67 to 84 while gradient replacement lowers them to 65; removing the gradient term from the training loss cuts surrogate acceptance from 0.703 to 0.148; and 1000 paired comparisons over ten noise levels show no noise threshold, only a base method that stops early. The same factors bound the gain: a model-based trust-region solver, which leaves little room, drops from 88 to 86 when the identical surrogate is attached, and released interpolation software stays ahead at 103, and on a Monte-Carlo inventory model repairing the acceptance interface is worth 10.40 cost units against 0.00 for the surrogate.

</details>


### [22] [Physics-Informed Error Field Learning: A Post-Training Optimization Framework for Physics-Informed Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24970)
*Jiuyun Sun,Yong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PIEFL框架，通过引入辅助误差网络学习当前解与精确解之间的误差场，在主网络达到满意精度后进行误差校正，避免了后期优化中收益递减的问题，在相同计算预算下获得了更高的求解精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PINNs在后期优化阶段存在参数更新收益递减问题：继续优化往往只能带来微小的精度提升，却显著增加计算成本，传统方法持续优化整个解空间效率低下。

Method: 提出物理信息误差场学习（PIEFL）框架：1）主网络达到满意精度后引入辅助误差网络；2）在物理约束下推导误差控制方程；3）将学习目标从解场转移到误差场；4）学习当前近似与精确解的偏差；5）将误差校正与主预测结合以提高精度。该方法无需修改主网络架构，可作为通用的训练后优化策略。

Result: 在代表性偏微分方程上的数值实验表明：PIEFL在相同计算预算下实现了更高的求解精度，避免了持续优化整个解空间的低效问题，有效提升了PINNs性能。

Conclusion: PIEFL框架成功解决了PINNs后期优化收益递减的问题，通过聚焦计算资源修正已有预测误差而非持续优化整个解空间，显著提升了求解效率与精度。该方法具有良好的兼容性，可作为现有PINN模型的后训练优化策略，为PINNs的实际应用提供了有效改进方案。

Abstract: Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) have emerged as an important class of numerical methods for solving partial differential equations (PDEs). However, during the late-stage optimization process, further parameter updates often yield diminishing accuracy improvements while increasing computational costs. To address this issue, this paper proposes a Physics-Informed Error Field Learning (PIEFL) framework for PINNs. Unlike conventional approaches that continuously approximate the solution field using a single network, PIEFL introduces an auxiliary error network after the primary network achieves satisfactory accuracy and shifts the learning objective from the solution field to the error field. By deriving error control equations under physical constraints, the error network learns the discrepancy between the current approximation and the exact solution, and the learned error correction is combined with the primary prediction to improve solution accuracy. The proposed framework avoids continuous optimization of the entire solution space and focuses computational resources on correcting existing prediction errors. Moreover, PIEFL requires no modification to the primary network architecture, making it compatible with existing PINN models and applicable as a general post-training optimization strategy. Numerical experiments on representative PDEs demonstrate that PIEFL achieves higher solution accuracy under the same computational budget, validating its effectiveness in improving the performance of PINNs.

</details>


### [23] [Resource-Efficient Pruning for Transformer via Low-Rank Importance Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24973)
*Peng Liu,Huibing Zeng,Yiqun Zhang,Yang Yi,Jigang Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了REP-LIE方法，通过利用LoRA低秩矩阵的梯度进行重要性估计，实现了在微调过程中资源高效的模型剪枝，无需全梯度计算和全参数优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于Transformer的大规模预训练语言模型虽然强大，但其高昂的计算和内存成本严重阻碍了在资源受限环境中的部署。传统剪枝方法通常依赖全梯度的重要性估计，且需要在剪枝前对模型进行微调才能获得满意性能，这一过程往往导致不可承受的资源消耗。

Method: 提出REP-LIE方法，利用LoRA低秩矩阵的梯度来估计权重重要性，避免了全梯度计算的需求。为解决重要性估计中的固有随机性问题，引入稳定性分数作为迭代剪枝不重要模型参数的依据。剪枝后的模型通过轻量级更新进行微调，无需全参数优化过程。

Result: 在中等规模编码器模型和大规模生成模型（LLaMA-7B和Mistral-7B）上进行了广泛实验，结果表明REP-LIE与现有方法相比仍能取得具有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: REP-LIE成功实现了在微调过程中的资源高效剪枝，显著降低了资源消耗，同时保持了与现有方法相当的性能，为大规模语言模型在资源受限环境中的部署提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: With the rapid development of large-scale pre-trained language models based on Transformer architectures, their high computational and memory costs have become a major obstacle to deployment, especially in resource-constrained environments. Traditional pruning methods typically depend on full gradient-based importance estimation, and they necessitate prior finetuning of the model to achieve satisfactory performance. This process often results in intolerable resource consumption. This paper proposes REP-LIE, a new approach to enable resource-efficient pruning during the process of finetuning. REP-LIE leverages the gradients of LoRA low-rank matrices to estimate the importance of weights without requiring full gradient computation. To address the inherent randomness in importance estimation, a stability score is introduced, serving as the basis for iterative pruning of unimportant model parameters. The pruned model is further finetuned through lightweight updates, eliminating the need for full-parameter optimization in the process of finetuning. Extensive experiments on both medium-scale encoder models and large-scale generative models (LLaMA-7B and Mistral-7B) demonstrate that REP-LIE still achieves competitive performance compared to existing approaches.

</details>

*Rahul Jain,Pierre Trepagnier,Rick Gentile,Joey Botero,Alexia Schulz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于FSQ量化层的自回归Transformer干扰抑制方法，在数字电视OFDM干扰环境下实现了比传统方法和先前AI方法更低的延迟和更好的抑制性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于AI的结构化干扰抑制方法因能联合考虑目标信号(SOI)和混合信号而优于传统方法。先前工作已探索了自回归Transformer模型，但需要在保持低延迟的同时进一步提升干扰抑制性能。

Method: 在已有自回归Transformer模型基础上引入有限标量量化(FSQ)tokenizer层，旨在提升干扰抑制性能同时保持低延迟。同时探索其他推理优化技术以在不显著损失精度的情况下加速推理。以数字调制射频信号为目标信号，数字电视OFDM信号作为结构化干扰进行实验验证。

Result: 实现了低延迟且相比传统技术和先前AI方法具有更好的干扰抑制效果。通过语音质量感知评估(PESQ)等音频指标验证了AI方法的优势。

Conclusion: 基于FSQ的Transformer干扰抑制方法在低延迟和高性能方面具有优势，适用于多种操作场景，为实际应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: AI-based structured interference rejection has grown more popular because deep learning approaches can outperform traditional methods by jointly considering the signal of interest (SOI) and the signal mixture (SOI plus interference). This work builds on a previous AI-enabled approach utilizing autoregressive transformer-based models by adding a Finite Scalar Quantization (FSQ) tokenizer layer which aims to improve the interference rejection performance while keeping overall latency to a minimum. Additionally, we experiment with other inference optimization techniques with the goal of speeding up inference without much accuracy loss. We explore this space with an experiment where the SOI is a digitally modulated radio frequency (RF) signal and the structured interference is a digital television signal, an extremely common type of Orthogonal Frequency-Division Multiplexing (OFDM) transmission. Our results achieve low latency and increased interference rejection over traditional techniques and prior work with other AI-enabled methods. We demonstrate the benefits of the AI-enabled approaches via audio metrics such as Perceptual Evaluation of Speech Quality (PESQ). Additionally, we explore a variety of applications and detail how our interference rejection algorithm may be used in operationally-relevant scenarios.

</details>


### [25] [MSR-IVA: Masked Structural Residual Independent Vector Analysis for State-Aware Fusion of Structural MRI and Dynamic Functional Network Connectivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24978)
*Victor Solomon,Zening Fu,Rafal Angryk,Vince D. Calhoun,Jingyu Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了MSR-IVA框架，通过共享结构表示与状态特定残差适应相结合，解决了多模态融合中结构分解一致性与状态特异性之间的矛盾，在ADNI数据集上显著提升了源耦合性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在sMRI和dFNC的多模态融合中，传统方法存在两难困境：对每个动态状态分别应用IVA会产生不相关的结构分解，而强制相同分解又会抑制状态特定的关联性。此外，并非所有受试者都表达每个动态状态，这增加了建模的复杂性。

Method: 提出掩码结构残差独立向量分析（MSR-IVA）框架，采用状态感知策略：1）建立共享的结构潜在表示；2）引入状态特定的残差适应机制；3）设计掩码机制处理不完全状态表达问题。该方法在保持结构源对应关系的同时允许状态特定的适应。

Result: 在ADNI队列上的实验表明：相比独立成对IVA基线，MSR-IVA将匹配源耦合提升6.5%，未匹配依赖降低15.7%。在表达双状态的受试者中，MSR-IVA的平均跨状态结构源相关性达到0.9177，而无共享方法仅为0.2978，证明了该方法在保持源对应的同时实现了受控的结构共享。

Conclusion: MSR-IVA成功平衡了结构表示的一致性与状态特异性需求，通过残差适应机制在保持源对应关系的同时允许状态特定适应，为多模态脑影像融合提供了有效解决方案，特别适用于动态状态表达不完整的实际场景。

Abstract: Multimodal fusion of structural MRI (sMRI) and dynamic functional network connectivity (dFNC) can reveal how brain structure relates to changing functional states. When the same structural latent representation is coupled with multiple states, applying independent vector analysis (IVA) separately to each state can produce unrelated structural decompositions, while forcing identical decompositions may suppress state-specific relationships. In addition, not every subject expresses every dynamic state. We propose masked structural residual IVA (MSR-IVA), a state-aware framework that combines a shared structural representation with state-specific residual adaptations and masks for incomplete state expression. On an Alzheimer's Disease Neuroimaging Initiative cohort, MSR-IVA improved matched source coupling by 6.5% and reduced unmatched dependence by 15.7% relative to the independent pairwise IVA baseline. Among subjects expressing both states, mean absolute cross-state structural source correlation was 0.9177 for MSR-IVA versus 0.2978 for no sharing, demonstrating controlled structural sharing that preserves source correspondence while allowing state-specific adaptation.

</details>


### [26] [D$^3$-MOPD: Adaptive Dynamic Domain ScheDuling for Efficient Multi-Teacher Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24987)
*Zechen Sun,Zhiwei Zhang,Fei Zhao,Juntao Li,Mu Chuan,Huayu Deng,Guojian Zhan,Wenliang Chen,Yao Hu,Min Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出D³-MOPD方法，通过动态调度多教师蒸馏中的域采样比例，显著提升训练效率和学生模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多教师策略蒸馏（MOPD）方法在训练前固定各域数据混合比例，忽视了不同域收敛速度差异显著的特点：快速收敛的域浪费计算资源，慢速收敛的域训练不足。

Method: 提出D³-MOPD（动态域调度），一种零开销调度器。利用训练过程中已产生的逐域逆向KL散度信号在线调整域采样比例。异步运行的监视器追踪各域KL轨迹，估计剩余空间和改进速率，动态调整采样比例而不改变核心训练循环。

Result: 在Qwen3.6-35B-A3B学生模型蒸馏四个领域专家教师的实验中：D³-MOPD缩小了97%的学生-教师性能差距（基线MOPD为63%），以约3倍减少的rollout步数达到相同峰值性能，在7个基准中的3个上超越专家教师。

Conclusion: D³-MOPD自然扩展到任意数量的域，域数量越多，调度器可利用的收敛模式多样性越强，预期收益越大。该方法有效解决了多域收敛不均衡问题，显著提升了知识蒸馏的效率和效果。

Abstract: Multi-teacher on-policy distillation (MOPD) distills several domain-expert teachers into a single student by minimizing per-domain reverse-KL divergence on the student's own rollouts. Existing approaches typically fix the per-domain data mixture before training, overlooking the fact that different domains converge at substantially different rates: some plateau early while others continue to improve throughout the training budget. A fixed mixture therefore wastes compute on fast-converging domains and undertrains slower-converging ones. To address this, we propose D$^3$-MOPD (Dynamic Domain ScheDuling for MOPD), a zero-overhead scheduler that repurposes the per-domain reverse-KL signal already produced during training to adapt the domain mixture online. Running asynchronously outside the training process, an off-process watcher periodically tracks each domain's KL trajectory, estimates remaining headroom and current improvement rate, and accordingly adjusts the domain sampling ratios without altering the core training loop. Our D$^3$-MOPD scales naturally to arbitrary numbers of domains, and the expected benefit grows as more domains introduce more diverse convergence patterns for the scheduler to exploit. On a Qwen3.6-35B-A3B student distilled from four domain-expert teachers, D$^3$-MOPD closes 97% of the average student-to-teacher performance gap, compared with 63% for vanilla MOPD, reaches the same peak performance with an approximately 3$\times$ reduction in rollout steps, and surpasses the specialist teachers on three of seven benchmarks.

</details>


### [27] [Rollout-Decoded Reconstruction for Long-Horizon Prediction in Latent World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25017)
*Rishi Shah,Rishav Shrestha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Rollout-Decoded Reconstruction (RDR)方法，通过在训练过程中让解码器处理模型的自由运行rollout潜在变量并惩罚重建误差，解决了潜在世界模型中解码器训练与部署不一致的问题。在Kuramoto-Sivashinsky方程上，RDR将有效预测时间提升了1.80倍（从3.87提升至6.97时间单位），且不增加任何参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜在世界模型存在训练-部署不一致问题：解码器在训练时处理的是锚定于观测的潜在变量，但在评估/部署时却要处理模型自由运行产生的潜在变量（距最后一次观测数百步之后），这种分布偏移导致性能下降。

Method: 提出Rollout-Decoded Reconstruction (RDR)方法：在训练过程中完全模拟评估时的自由运行方式，对rollout过程中的每个潜在变量进行解码，并计算其与真实值的重建误差作为损失项。该损失项不增加模型参数，仅增加训练时计算开销，权重为零时退化为标准目标函数。

Result: 在混沌Kuramoto-Sivashinsky方程任务上，RDR将有效预测时间（首次达到归一化误差0.5的时间）从3.87±0.23提升至6.97±0.42时间单位，提升1.80倍，模型参数量保持193,568不变。该改进在未参与选择的种子上得到确认，并在10个预注册配置中的10个都保持优势（提升倍数1.71-2.50×）。潜在宽度越大优势越明显。

Conclusion: RDR是一个简单有效的方法，通过单一损失项使训练过程与评估部署保持一致，显著提升了混沌系统预测的有效时间。该方法无需增加参数，易于实现（单标志A/B测试），为解决潜在世界模型的训练-部署分布偏移问题提供了新思路。

Abstract: A latent world model trains its decoder on latents anchored to observations, then deploys it on the model's own free-running rollout, hundreds of steps past the last observation. Rollout-Decoded Reconstruction (RDR) closes this gap with a single loss term that free-runs the model during training exactly as evaluation will, decodes every rollout latent, and penalizes reconstruction error against ground truth. The term adds no parameters, costs training-time compute only, and reduces to the standard objective at weight zero, so every comparison in this paper is a one-flag A/B. On the chaotic Kuramoto-Sivashinsky equation, RDR raises valid prediction time (the time to first crossing of normalized error 0.5) from $3.87 \pm 0.23$ to $6.97 \pm 0.42$ time units at an identical 193,568 parameters, a $1.80\times$ improvement confirmed on seeds never used in selection and holding in 10 of 10 preregistered configurations at ratios of 1.71-2.50$\times$. The results come from a single system; a sweep in which the advantage grows with latent width is descriptive, and control experiments on two classic tasks are preliminary.

</details>


### [28] [On the Representational Geometry of Dynamic Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25034)
*Richard F. M. Lim,Ruriko Yoshida*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从几何角度揭示了标准神经网络在动态规划任务上无法泛化到更长输入的本质原因：DP的三种表示（DAG最短路径、热带多项式、扩展牛顿多面体）构成了同构半环，但其结构冗余和组合限制阻碍了长度泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准神经网络架构在动态规划（DP）目标任务上往往无法泛化到更长的输入序列。作者希望从几何视角深入理解这一泛化困难的根本原因，探究DP结构中哪些性质导致了这种局限性。

Method: 作者建立了有限min-plus DP的三种等价表示形式：DAG上的最短路径、热带多项式、以及编码决策边界的扩展牛顿多面体。证明了这三种表示在形式多项式和计算函数两个层面上构成同构半环，并通过这些连接刻画了所有结构冗余。作者从几何角度研究了长度泛化问题：长度T时的决策边界是否能决定T+1时的边界。

Result: 发现了两个结构性的负面结果：（1）半环中两种本征降维方法（将变量设置为各恒等元素）既不是单射的，也不总是封闭在DP内部；（2）串联和并联组合无法从更小的子DAG构建所有DAG拓扑结构，即使所有终端操作也无法捕获所有DP组合方式。这些发现从理论上解释了神经网络在DP任务上泛化困难的结构性障碍。

Conclusion: DP问题的内在几何结构存在根本性的限制：决策边界在相邻长度间不能自然延续，标准组合操作无法完整刻画DP结构。这些结构性障碍从理论上解释了神经网络在DP任务上泛化到更长输入的困难，为设计更具泛化能力的架构提供了重要指导。

Abstract: Standard neural architectures often fail to generalize to longer inputs for dynamic programming (DP) targets. We investigate what makes this hard geometrically. Every finite min-plus DP is a shortest path on a DAG, which is equivalently a tropical polynomial whose extended Newton polyhedron encodes the decision boundary of which path wins. We prove these three descriptions (graph, polynomial, polyhedron) form isomorphic semirings at two levels --- formal polynomials and their computed functions --- connected by operations that characterize all structural redundancies. We then address the length-generalization question geometrically: does the decision boundary at length $T$ decide the boundary at $T+1$? We present two structural negatives. The semiring's two native ways to reduce dimension (setting a variable to each identity) are neither injective nor always closed within the DP. Series and parallel composition fail to construct all DAG topologies from smaller sub-DAGs, and even all terminal-only operations do not capture all DP compositions.

</details>


### [29] [DeMMO: Longitudinal and Cross-Disease Modelling of Digital Mobility Outcomes via Multi-Task Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25073)
*Menghui Zhou,Zhipeng Yuan,Vitaveska Lanfranchi,Po Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DeMMO框架，首次实现跨疾病、跨临床结果的纵向多变量数字移动性指标（DMO）联合建模，在Mobilise-D大规模多中心数据集上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DMO研究多局限于单一疾病单次访问，缺乏对多变量DMO与多种临床结果关系的纵向演化建模；现有时序多任务框架无法跨疾病联合建模多个预测结果，特别是在疾病队列不共享参与者的情况下。

Method: 提出DeMMO框架：用纵向DMO系数矩阵表示每个疾病-结果目标；结合时序正则化与稳定及访问特异性特征选择；核心技术贡献是自动跨疾病跨结果关系学习机制，直接从纵向映射中学习有符号关系，实现无配对参与者情况下的选择性信息共享。

Result: 在Mobilise-D大规模多中心数据集（24个真实世界DMO，5次访问，多种运动受限疾病）上，与9个线性、纵向和深度回归基线相比，DeMMO取得最佳整体和结果特异性预测性能，对最强基线有显著提升；稳定性选择识别出可靠的纵向DMO模式。

Conclusion: DeMMO为纵向、多疾病、多结果学习提供了可解释的解决方案，支持后续临床验证和疾病监测；代码和实验结果已开源。

Abstract: Digital mobility outcomes (DMOs) derived from wearable sensors characterise mobility in daily life and offer a promising means of monitoring disease progression. Yet most DMO studies examine one disease at one visit; they do not model how multivariate DMO relationships with multiple clinical outcomes evolve jointly across diseases. Technically, existing temporal multi-task frameworks can model progression within an individual disease, but they do not jointly model multiple prediction outcomes across diseases, particularly when disease cohorts do not share participants. To address these gaps, we propose DeMMO, an interpretable framework for longitudinal, multi-disease, and multi-outcome learning. DeMMO represents each disease-outcome objective by a longitudinal DMO coefficient matrix and combines temporal regularisation with stable and visit-specific feature selection. Its central technical contribution is an automatic cross-disease and cross-outcome relation-learning mechanism that learns signed relations directly from these longitudinal mappings, enabling selective information sharing without paired participants. We evaluate DeMMO on the recently released, large-scale, multicentre Mobilise-D dataset, which provides a new opportunity to study 24 harmonised real-world DMOs over five visits across multiple mobility-limiting conditions. Against nine strong linear, longitudinal, and deep-regression baselines, DeMMO achieves the best overall and outcome-specific prediction performance, with significant improvements over the strongest baselines. Stability selection further identifies reliable longitudinal DMO patterns for subsequent clinical validation and disease monitoring. The implementation code and experimental results are available at https://github.com/menghui-zhou/DeMMO.

</details>


### [30] [NVExplain: Explaining Time Series Forecasting with Latent Trajectory Analysis and Structure-Preserving Surrogates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25080)
*Muyan Anna Li,Manikandan Ravikiran,Aditi Gautam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种模型无关的时间序列预测可解释性框架，通过语义流量化信息演化，构建滞后期-预测期归因矩阵，提供具有时间连贯性的解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列预测模型在高风险场景中被广泛使用，但现有的事后解释方法通常忽略时间依赖性，且无法提供针对不同预测时间范围的解释，导致预测结果难以理解。

Method: 将预测建模为潜在轨迹，引入语义流（semantic flow）量化信息在模型内部表示中的时间演化过程；通过聚合语义流构建滞后期-预测期归因矩阵；生成保持结构的扰动并拟合稀疏局部代理模型，生成人类可读且时间连贯的解释。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上评估了方法的忠实度和稳定性。语义流变体在忠实度上达到与标准事后基线竞争或优越的性能，同时计算效率显著更高。稳定性分析表明解释具有鲁棒性，并识别出解释应用需谨慎的场景。

Conclusion: 该框架成功解决了时间序列预测中的时间依赖性和时间范围特定解释问题，为高风险场景中的预测模型提供了有效、高效且鲁棒的解释方法。

Abstract: Time series forecasting models are widely used in high-stakes settings, yet their predictions remain difficult to interpret because existing post-hoc methods often ignore temporal dependence and fail to provide horizon-specific explanations. We propose a model-agnostic explainability framework that explains forecasting predictions by attributing each forecast horizon to temporally relevant historical lags. The framework models forecasting as a latent trajectory and introduces semantic flow to quantify how information evolves across time in the model's internal representations. By aggregating semantic flow, it constructs a lag-horizon attribution matrix that captures horizon-resolved temporal influence. To improve explainability, we further generate structure-preserving perturbations and fit sparse local surrogate models, producing human-readable and temporally coherent explanations. We evaluate the method using faithfulness and stability diagnostics across multiple benchmark datasets. Results show that the semantic-flow variant achieves competitive or superior faithfulness compared to standard post-hoc baselines, while being substantially more computationally efficient. Stability analysis further demonstrates that the explanations are robust and identifies regimes where interpretation should be applied with caution.

</details>


### [31] [The Frame Kernel Method for Multiscale Operator Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25084)
*Branden Frieden,Ryan Whitehead,M. Keith Ballard,Robert M. Kirby,Varun Shankar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种原生多尺度算子学习方法，用于多尺度偏微分方程的代理建模，在挑战性问题上比流行的神经算子显著更准确。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的神经算子在处理固有地具有多尺度特性的偏微分方程时精度不足，且缺乏对输出函数的多尺度分解能力。

Method: 提出一种新颖的多尺度核框架函数逼近技术，将算子学习问题转化为学习输出函数的框架系数作为输入函数框架系数的函数，适用于张量积网格和点云。

Result: 证明了插值定理、误差估计和数值收敛率；在挑战性问题上的准确性显著优于流行的神经算子，同时允许在泛化时进行事后多尺度分解。

Conclusion: 该多尺度框架核方法为多尺度PDE的代理建模提供了一种更准确且具备多尺度分解能力的有效方法。

Abstract: We present a natively multiscale operator learning method for the surrogate modeling of (numerical solvers for) multiscale partial differential equations (PDEs). The primary novelty of our method lies in a novel multiscale kernel frame function approximation technique. Leveraging this new kernel frame technique, we cast the operator learning problem as one of learning frame coefficients of output functions as a function of frame coefficients of input functions. The generalization step then automatically allows for a multiscale decomposition of the output functions. Our method is applicable to both tensor-product grids and point clouds. We present interpolation proofs, error estimates, and numerical convergence rates for our frame approximation. We the demonstrate the applicability of our method for the surrogate modeling of inherently multiscale PDEs. The new multiscale frame kernel method is significantly more accurate than popular neural operators on challenging problems from the literature, while simultaneously admitting an a posteriori multiscale decomposition upon generalization.

</details>

*Morteza Sarafyazd*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出von-Neumann状态空间Transformer（VN-SST），通过低秩指令库和状态空间记忆实现高效神经解码，在三个运动皮层基准测试中显著提升数据效率，尤其在数据稀缺场景下表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 皮层计算具有显著的低维特性，现有Transformer前馈块对所有token应用相同算子，效率低下。受冯·诺依曼架构启发，作者提出将解码过程分解为控制器解码指令和执行特定token算子两个步骤，以实现更高效的计算。

Method: 构建von-Neumann状态空间Transformer（VN-SST），其前馈块为低秩指令库：包含共享基础算子和少量学习到的低秩指令，每个token通过从状态空间记忆的低维投影读取代码来合成实际使用的权重矩阵，模仿低维动力学如何路由皮层计算。

Result: 在三个运动皮层神经解码基准测试中，VN-SST比现代Transformer具有更高的数据效率，在数据最稀缺的基准测试中以大幅优势获胜，在其他两个基准测试中领先，并且能将更长的上下文转化为准确率提升而非下降。在两个小型文本基准测试中同样表现出更高的参数效率。

Conclusion: VN-SST通过将程序容量作为控制通道而非精度杠杆，实现了对大规模指令库的压缩（每token仅需几位），证明了该机制在神经解码和语言建模任务中的通用性和有效性。

Abstract: Cortical computation is strikingly low-dimensional: a handful of latent variables, carried in a neural population's activity, steer the higher-dimensional responses of individual neurons. Our aim is sample efficiency-models that decode well from limited data and at small parameter budgets. In a standard Transformer layer, the feed-forward block applies the same operator to every token. We suggest a von-Neumann inspired hypothesis of efficient computation as an alternative for neural decoding: a controller decodes an instruction and then executes a token-specific operator; the usual realization-a soft mixture of experts-only blends their outputs, not operators. We introduce a von-Neumann State-Space Transformer (VN-SST), a memory-augmented Transformer whose feed-forward block is a low-rank instruction bank: a shared base operator plus a small set of learned low-rank instructions, from which a per-token code synthesizes the weight matrix actually used at that token. The code is read from a low- dimensional projection of a carried state-space memory, so a slow latent trajectory acts as an instruction pointer-mirroring how low-dimensional dynamics may route cortical computation. On three motor-cortex neural-decoding benchmarks, VN-SST is far more data-efficient than a modern Transformer, each jointly predicting spikes and decoding behavior. This model wins by a wide margin on the scarcest benchmark, leads on the other two, and turns longer context into rising rather than falling accuracy. We evaluated that the network compresses a large instruction bank to a few bits per token, so program capacity acts as a control channel, not an accuracy lever. The same model is also more parameter-efficient on two small text benchmarks used for language modeling (LLMs), suggesting a generic mechanism.

</details>

*Molka Chkir,Syed Muhammad Danish,Jos Höll,Arghavan Asad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了注意力机制对LLM解码阶段能耗的影响，发现GQA配合滑动窗口注意力能显著降低能耗增长，批处理可减少高达87%的每token能耗和请求延迟。
