Abstract: Forecasting a stochastic dynamical system rarely means a single number: one wants several observables---future state, threshold event, regime label---each with its own likelihood. Standard multi-task recipes balance per-task losses, tuned or learned. We instead compose the observables' likelihoods in per-task free-routed last-layer beliefs on a shared backbone; this absorbs unit-dependent loss scaling into likelihood parameters learned in the same gradient pass. Stochastic dynamics supply what static benchmarks cannot: computable ground truth for the predictive variance. Results land where theory puts them: on the well-specified, homoscedastic Ornstein--Uhlenbeck process the learned predictive law recovers the analytic kernel and correctly specified baselines tie. On heteroscedastic systems (stochastic Lorenz-63, real air-quality data) the belief's input-dependent variance separates: best single-run NLL on the state and regime tasks, calibration matched only by arms whose NLL it beats, at a fraction of the tuned grids' cost. On the real series the state margin holds across five rolling origins.

</details>


### [103] [DualOPSD: Adaptive Privileged Teachers for On-Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26019)
*Yutong Chen,Guangfu Guo,Zhichao Xu,Kunpeng Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DualOPSD框架，通过非对称交替更新教师和学生模型，在Qwen3-8B上相比OPSD在AIME/HMMT数学推理任务上提升10-24分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原有OPSD方法中的特权教师在训练过程中保持固定，无法适应学生模型分布和输出风格的变化，导致监督不够灵活。

Method: 提出DualOPSD非对称交替框架：(1)学生首先从特权教师学习；(2)教师随后在同一学生轨迹上向更新后的学生分布移动。这种更新使后续监督能够响应学习者的变化，且无需额外的rollout。

Result: 在Qwen3-8B非思考模式下，DualOPSD相比OPSD在AIME 2024、AIME 2025和HMMT 2025上分别提升23.61、13.89和10.00个avg@12百分点。1.7B和4B规模实验显示精度提升依赖于模型规模。所有规模下DualOPSD都减少了截断现象，4B诊断实验显示师生双向KL散度更低。

Conclusion: DualOPSD通过动态适应教师模型实现了比固定教师OPSD更好的性能，证明了在自蒸馏过程中让教师适应学生分布变化的有效性。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) uses a privileged copy of the student model to provide dense supervision without an external teacher. OPSD keeps this privileged teacher fixed, even though the student distribution and output style change during training. We propose DualOPSD, an asymmetric alternating framework that adapts both policies. The student first learns from the privileged teacher. The teacher then moves toward the updated student distribution on the same student trajectory. This update makes later supervision responsive to the learner and does not require another rollout. On Qwen3-8B in non-thinking mode, DualOPSD improves avg@12 over OPSD by 23.61, 13.89, and 10.00 points on AIME 2024, AIME 2025, and HMMT 2025. Results at 1.7B and 4B show that the accuracy gain depends on model scale. Across all three scales, DualOPSD reduces truncation. The 4B diagnostic also shows lower KL in both directions between the teacher and student.

</details>


### [104] [Quantum-Inspired Modeling of Driving Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25907)
*Mohammad Elayan,Omid Armantalab,Wissam Kontar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种受量子力学启发的驾驶员行为表示方法，将连续性、概率性、情境依赖性、历史依赖性等特性统一起来，能够自动发现驾驶行为模式并支持实际应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 驾驶员行为具有异质性、情境依赖性和时变性，这些特性塑造了我们观察到的交通现象。然而，现有模型通常预先固定行为变量的交互方式，无法捕捉这些复杂特性，导致行为差异被吸收为噪声；而足够灵活的模型又往往失去可解释性。

Method: 引入一种受量子力学启发的驾驶员行为表示方法，将每个驾驶员编码为演化的密度矩阵（density matrix），提供行为不确定性、时间演化和情境依赖性变化的统一表示。该方法结合了连续性、概率性、情境依赖性、历史依赖性等通常被分开处理的特性，并从数据中学习行为变量之间的交互关系。在I-24 MOTION数据集上进行无监督训练。

Result: 框架自动恢复了三种可解释的驾驶模式：自由流、过渡态和拥堵态。这些模式捕捉了数据的完整行为范围以及驾驶员在不同条件下的平滑过渡。该表示能够重现已知的宏观现象，与基本图对齐并再现磁滞回线。此外，该框架能为经典跟驰模型提供情境依赖参数，并为自动驾驶车辆提供周围驾驶员的实时行为读取和短时运动预测。

Conclusion: 该框架指向了本质上可解释且可信赖的交通模型，为交通建模提供了新方向。研究已发布开源工具包，涵盖数据处理、训练、推理和分析全过程。

Abstract: Driver behavior is heterogeneous, context-dependent, and changes over time, and these properties shape the traffic phenomena we observe. Most models, however, fix in advance which behavioral variables interact and how. Behavior outside that form is absorbed as noise, while models flexible enough to capture it tend to lose interpretability. We introduce a quantum-inspired representation of driver behavior that combines properties usually treated separately or in part: it is continuous, probabilistic, context-dependent, history-dependent, and represents interactions among behavioral variables as learned from data. Each driver is encoded as an evolving density matrix, providing a unified representation of behavioral uncertainty, temporal evolution, and context-dependent behavioral variation. Trained without supervision on the I-24 MOTION dataset, the framework recovers three interpretable driving profiles representing three regimes: free flow, transition, and congestion. The profiles capture the behavioral range of the data and the smooth transitions drivers make between regimes as conditions change. The same representation also reproduces known macroscopic phenomena, aligning with the fundamental diagram and reproducing hysteresis loops. We also show how the representation supports practical use: it supplies context-dependent parameters to classical car-following models, and gives an autonomous vehicle a live behavioral read of the surrounding drivers with a short-horizon forecast of their motion. The framework points toward models of traffic that are interpretable and trustworthy by construction. We release an open-source toolkit on GitHub (https://github.com/mselayan/quantum-driver-representation) spanning data processing, training, inference, and analysis.

</details>


### [105] [How Much Rank Does LoRA Need? Rank-Error Bounds for Transformer Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26052)
*Gerard Conangla Planes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为Transformer注意力机制中LoRA更新的秩选择提供了理论框架，推导了在给定秩下可实现的最小期望KL误差的上下界，揭示了近似误差与目标更新的下游加权尾部能量之间的量化关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LoRA方法中秩的选择通常依赖经验调参，缺乏理论指导。本文旨在建立任务相关的理论框架，为Transformer注意力机制中LoRA秩的选择提供理论依据，实现从经验选择到理论指导的转变。

Method: 固定预训练注意力头、目标注意力函数和下游任务输入分布，推导秩为r的query LoRA更新所能达到的最小期望KL误差的上下界。关键技术包括：(1) 当目标注意力概率远离零时证明下界正比于ψ(||d||₂)；(2) 证明无条件上界min{||d||₂²/4, √2||d||₂}；(3) 在可实现性、几何和矩条件下建立基于下游加权尾部能量的界限；(4) 分析softmax饱和对秩需求的影响；(5) 扩展到多头LoRA和联合query/key更新的场景。

Result: 主要理论结果包括：(1) 当目标注意力概率有界远离零时，KL误差下界正比于ψ(||d||₂)，其中d为候选与目标注意力分数之差，ψ(t)=min{t²,t}；(2) 无条件上界为min{||d||₂²/4, √2||d||₂}；(3) 在显式可实现性、几何和矩条件下，最优秩r误差介于ψ(√T_r)的显式倍数与min{T_r/4, √(2T_r)}之间，其中T_r为目标更新的下游加权尾部能量；(4) 构造了softmax饱和使得匹配注意力函数所需秩严格小于匹配有限logits所需秩的显式族；(5) 揭示了秩共享和query/key分解约束的影响。

Conclusion: 本文建立了Transformer注意力机制中LoRA近似误差的首个任务相关理论框架，提供了与下游任务分布相关的谱界限。结果表明近似误差取决于目标更新的下游加权尾部能量，softmax饱和可降低实际所需秩。该理论为实践中LoRA秩的选择提供了明确的指导原则，并推广到了多头和联合更新等复杂场景。

Abstract: Choosing the rank of a low-rank adaptation (LoRA) update is usually an empirical task. In this paper, we provide a task-dependent theory of the approximation error achievable at each LoRA rank for Transformer attention. We fix a pretrained attention head, a target attention function, and a distribution over inputs from the downstream task, and bound the smallest expected Kullback--Leibler (KL) error achievable by a rank-$r$ query LoRA update. When target attention probabilities are bounded away from zero, we prove a lower bound of the error proportional to $ψ(\|d\|_2)$, where $d$ is the difference between candidate and target attention scores and $ψ(t)=\min\{t^2,t\}$. We also prove an unconditional upper bound $\min\{\|d\|_2^2/4,\sqrt2\|d\|_2\}$. Under explicit realizability, geometry, and moment conditions, we then bound the best rank-$r$ error between an explicit multiple of $ψ(\sqrt{T_r})$ and $\min\{T_r/4,\sqrt{2T_r}\}$, where $T_r$ is the downstream-weighted tail energy of the target update. We also provide target-Fisher bounds when candidate scores remain within a fixed range of the target scores, and an unrestricted lower bound when a subset of tokens carries most of the probability mass. These spectral bounds describe finite-score approximation. We then construct explicit families in which softmax saturation makes the rank required to match the attention function strictly smaller than the rank required to match the finite logits. Finally, we extend the analysis to fused multi-head LoRA and joint query/key updates, exposing the effects of rank sharing and query/key factorization constraints.

</details>


### [106] [ICON Decomposition: Multivariate Concept-Level Explanations of Deep Representations for Model Auditing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26083)
*Roshan Prakash Rane,Marco Simnacher,Manuel Pfeuffer,Marc-Andre Schulz,Nys Tjade Siegel,Maximilian Dreyer,Frederik Pahde,Wojciech Samek,Sonja Greven,Kerstin Ritter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了ICON分解方法，通过控制其他概念和结果的效应，准确量化深度神经网络中各概念对网络层的解释程度，有效识别模型真实依赖的概念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于概念的可解释性方法在检测捷径学习时，孤立地评估每个概念，容易将概念间的相关性误判为模型使用的证据，导致对模型依赖概念的错误识别。

Method: 提出ICON分解方法，在控制所有其他概念和输出结果的影响后，量化每个概念对网络层方差的解释程度，从而分离出模型真正依赖的概念，并量化未被任何已提供概念解释的表示部分。

Result: 在具有真实标签的合成数据上，ICON比七种基线方法更准确地恢复概念重要性；在皮肤病变和脑成像模型上，成功隔离模型真正依赖的概念，量化未解释的表示，并通过重训练和分布外测试验证了稀疏解释的有效性。

Conclusion: ICON分解能够更准确地识别深度神经网络中模型真正依赖的概念，为解决捷径学习问题提供了有效的可解释性工具，有助于提高模型的可信度和泛化能力。

Abstract: Deep neural networks often exploit spurious associations in their training data, a failure known as shortcut learning. Concept-based explainability methods screen for shortcuts by testing whether concepts such as a patient's sex or scanner settings can be decoded from a network layer. Because each concept is evaluated in isolation, these methods can mistake correlations between concepts as evidence that the model uses them. We introduce ICON decomposition, which instead quantifies how much of a layer's variance each concept explains after accounting for all other concepts and the outcome. On synthetic data with known ground truth, ICON recovers concept importance more accurately than seven alternative baseline methods. On skin-lesion and brain-imaging models, it isolates the concepts on which a model genuinely relies, quantifies the representation unexplained by any of the supplied concepts, and yields sparse explanations that we validate by retraining and out-of-distribution testing.

</details>


### [107] [When Pruning Meets Interpretability: Preserving Sparse Autoencoder Robustness in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25941)
*Suchit Gupte,Xueru Zhang,Mohammad Mahdi Khalili*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了剪枝对稀疏自编码器(SAE)行为的影响，揭示了幅度剪枝因忽略激活几何特性而导致SAE功能退化，而激活感知方法(如Wanda和SparseGPT)更稳健。发现中间层对剪枝最敏感，并提出了分层稀疏分配策略以提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏自编码器(SAE)被广泛用于解释大语言模型(LLM)的内部表示，但其在模型压缩后的可靠性尚未被充分理解。研究者希望系统性地探究剪枝如何影响SAE的行为，以填补这一研究空白。

Method: 1. 理论分析：提出扰动能量（协方差加权范数）作为剪枝影响SAE的关键指标；2. 对比分析：比较幅度剪枝与激活感知方法(Wanda、SparseGPT)的表现差异；3. 结构分析：探究不同网络层对剪枝的敏感性差异；4. 提出解决方案：设计分层稀疏分配策略，针对不同层的敏感性差异进行优化。

Result: 1. 理论发现：剪枝对固定SAE的影响由扰动能量决定；2. 方法比较：幅度剪枝因忽略激活几何特性而扭曲表示空间并降低SAE功能，激活感知方法隐式控制扰动能量，显著更稳健；3. 结构脆弱性：中间层对剪枝的敏感性显著高于早期层和晚期层；4. 策略验证：分层稀疏分配策略在相同平均剪枝稀疏度下实现了更低的困惑度；5. 在四种模型架构上的实验验证了理论发现。

Conclusion: 本研究为剪枝后SAE的鲁棒性提供了理论框架和实践指导。通过扰动能量视角揭示了不同剪枝方法的本质差异，发现了中间层的结构脆弱性，并提出了有效的分层稀疏分配策略，为大语言模型的压缩与可解释性研究提供了重要见解。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) are widely used to interpret the internal representations of large language models (LLMs), yet their reliability under post-hoc model compression remains poorly understood. We present a systematic study of how pruning affects SAE behavior and theoretically show that, for a fixed SAE, its impact is governed by perturbation energy, a covariance-weighted norm. This perspective exposes a key limitation of magnitude pruning: by ignoring activation geometry, it distorts the learned representation space and degrades SAE functionality. Activation-aware methods such as Wanda and SparseGPT, in contrast, implicitly control perturbation energy and are therefore substantially more robust at preserving SAE behavior. We further reveal a consistent structural vulnerability across all pruning methods: middle layers are significantly more sensitive to pruning than early or late layers. Guided by this insight, we propose a layer-wise sparsity allocation strategy, achieving lower perplexity under the same average pruning sparsity. Experiments across four model architectures validate our theoretical findings. Code is publicly available at https://github.com/osu-srml/sae-robustness-under-pruning/tree/main.

</details>


### [108] [TraceML: An Empirical Analysis of Human-Agent Planning in Machine Learning Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26086)
*Jiarui Yan,Weiwei Sun,Sijie Li,Wenhan Li,Yiming Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TraceML数据集揭示AI智能体在机器学习竞赛中表现不如人类的核心原因：人类采用多阶段迭代开发策略，而AI陷入狭窄循环，缺乏规划与回溯能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于结果的基准测试仅评估最终提交，忽略了开发过程，无法诊断AI智能体在长期机器学习开发任务中表现不佳的根本原因。研究者希望通过细粒度的轨迹对比分析，揭示人类与智能体在开发过程中的行为差异。

Method: 构建TraceML数据集，包含4,465条人类Kaggle竞赛轨迹（134个竞赛）以及430条人类与207条智能体配对轨迹。每个代码版本标注了分数、时间戳、动作类型、意图、编辑规模和分数影响。采用统一的版本级模式对人类和智能体轨迹进行对比分析，并设计了基于人类实践蒸馏的规划提示进行干预实验。

Result: 发现专家开发者交替进行数据处理、验证、模型修改和集成，并能重访之前搁置的方案；而Codex智能体困于重新加权集成和调参循环，MLEvolve仅原地变异模型，两者均缺乏人类的转型率和重开已弃工作的能力。规划提示虽能改善部分行为并提升分数，但努力轮廓仍呈智能体特征，仅能弥合可归结为指令的部分差距。

Conclusion: 基于结果的评估掩盖了AI智能体与人类在开发行为上的本质差异。过程级分析揭示了智能体缺乏策略性规划和回溯能力，当前指令微调方法只能解决部分问题。TraceML为未来智能体研究提供了细粒度诊断工具和基准。

Abstract: Large language models write correct code for isolated problems but remain far weaker at autonomous machine-learning development, where an agent must revise data pipelines, models, and validation over hours of feedback, and on most competitions still finishes below strong human competitors. Outcome-based benchmarks record this gap but not its cause, because they grade the final submission and discard the development process behind it. We introduce TraceML, which pairs human and agent work on the same competitions under one version-level schema: 4,465 human Kaggle trajectories across 134 competitions, seven of which are also worked by two agent scaffolds, giving 430 paired human and 207 agent trajectories. Every code version carries its score, its timestamp, and labels for the action taken, its intent, the edit size, and the score effect. Read this way, the gap becomes concrete. Experts alternate data work, validation, model changes, and ensembling, and return to approaches they had set aside. Each agent scaffold instead collapses into a narrow loop: Codex spends its steps re-weighting ensembles and tuning submissions, MLEvolve mutates its model in place, and neither pivots at the human rate nor reopens abandoned work. A short planning prompt distilled from human practice moves the behaviors it names toward the human profile and lifts scores, but the effort profile stays agent-shaped: instruction closes only the part of the gap that reduces to instructions. We release the corpus, the schema, the labelers, and the extraction pipeline at https://huggingface.co/datasets/jerryyan/TraceML.

</details>


### [109] [Spectral Allocation: Why Muon Outperforms Adam, and How to Improve Muon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25990)
*Xiaodong Wu,Wenyi Yu,Chao Zhang,Philip Woodland*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通过谱分析揭示了不同优化器性能差异的机制，并提出SAMuon优化器，在124M到1B参数模型上比AdamW和Muon减少13.3%-24.0%的训练token需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 正交优化器（如Muon）在大语言模型预训练中显著优于Adam，但其机制尚未被充分理解。作者希望通过谱分析来揭示这种性能差异的根本原因。

Method: 在真实训练轨迹的检查点上，将动量缓冲区分解为奇异方向，在留出数据上估计每个方向的最优步长。基于发现的各向异性谱分布特征（头部不稳定且需小步长，主体稳定且可大步长），提出了SAMuon和SAMuon-lite两种变体，分别使用低秩随机SVD和秩一幂迭代实现谱感知优化。

Result: 发现谱分布在批次、训练阶段间稳定且在不同优化器和模型规模间一致。SAMuon变体在所有评估的模型规模和批量大小配置上都优于调优后的AdamW和Muon基线。SAMuon比Muon减少13.3%-24.0%的训练token，SAMuon-lite保持大部分增益且几乎无额外开销。

Conclusion: 谱分析方法成功解释了优化器性能差异的机制，验证了谱感知优化的有效性。SAMuon在保持Muon渐近收敛率的同时，有效利用了谱分布特征，显著提升了训练效率。

Abstract: Orthogonal optimisers such as Muon can substantially accelerate large language model pretraining relative to Adam, yet the mechanism remains incompletely understood. We investigate this through an out-of-sample spectral probing analysis of Transformer loss landscapes. At checkpoints along real training trajectories, we decompose each momentum buffer into its singular directions and estimate the loss-optimal step size along each direction on held-out data. The resulting spectral profile is anisotropic yet stable across batches and training stages, and consistent across the optimisers and model scales: a volatile head operating at the Edge-of-Stability supports a much smaller step size than the tolerant bulk, which permits substantially larger steps. This profile provides a unified spectral allocation account of why Muon outperforms Adam, which outperforms SGD. It also exposes a limitation of Muon's uniform scaling: it still underutilises the bulk. Guided by this finding, we introduce Spectral-Aware Muon (SAMuon), which holds the head at the Muon scale and amplifies the bulk using a static spectral prior. We provide two variants: the complete SAMuon follows the measured profile using a low-rank randomised SVD and the simplified SAMuon-lite uses a two-level approximation via rank-one power iteration. Neither method adds persistent optimiser state or notable extra FLOPs beyond Muon at scale, and the idealised exact-whitening versions of both retain Muon's asymptotic convergence rate under standard assumptions. Across "modded-nanogpt" models from 124M to 1B parameters, both variants outperform tuned AdamW and Muon (Scion implementation) baselines in all evaluated model-scale and batch-size configurations. SAMuon requires 13.3% to 24.0% fewer training tokens to reach the same validation loss as Muon, while SAMuon-lite retains most of this gain with near-zero wall-clock overhead.

</details>


### [110] [Robust CurveMoE: Multi-Norm Adversarial Defense for Mixture-of-Experts Models via Mode Connectivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26043)
*Xu Zhang,Ren Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Robust CurveMoE是一个高效的混合专家框架，通过连接不同范数扰动的专用模型来防御多范数对抗攻击，显著提升了联合准确率并降低了训练成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多范数对抗防御方法在单一参数配置下优化竞争性鲁棒性目标，导致训练成本高昂且鲁棒性权衡不佳。

Method: 提出Robust CurveMoE框架：1）通过低损失路径连接不同范数的专用模型；2）从鲁棒性约束的曲线位置派生专家，仅对关键层进行选择性专家化，其余参数共享；3）引入基于梯度的贡献引导部分更新策略来降低曲线构建成本。

Result: 在CIFAR-100和ImageNet-100数据集上，Robust CurveMoE在干净样本准确率、特定范数准确率和联合准确率上均优于MSD和ERMC基线方法，联合准确率分别提升2.37%和2.13%。

Conclusion: Robust CurveMoE有效解决了多范数对抗防御中的训练效率和鲁棒性权衡问题，为构建高效的多范数鲁棒模型提供了新思路。

Abstract: Multi-norm adversarial defense aims to protect neural networks against perturbations defined by different norm constraints, but existing methods typically optimize competing robustness objectives within a single parameter configuration, leading to substantial training cost and unfavorable robustness trade-offs. We propose Robust CurveMoE, an efficient mixture-of-experts framework that connects models specialized for different perturbation norms through a low-loss path and exploits the complementary robustness profiles of models along this path. Robust CurveMoE derives clean and norm-specialized experts from robustness-constrained curve locations and selectively expertizes only influential layers, while sharing the remaining parameters across routing paths. To further reduce curve-construction cost, we introduce contribution-guided partial updating, which selects influential curve parameters using initialization-based gradient scores. We also theoretically bound the objective gap between partial and full curve optimization. Experiments on CIFAR-100 and ImageNet-100 with WideResNet and Vision Transformer architectures show that Robust CurveMoE consistently improves clean, norm-specific, and Union accuracy over MSD and ERMC. In particular, it improves Union accuracy by 2.37 and 2.13 percentage points over the strongest baseline on CIFAR-100 and ImageNet-100, respectively. Extensive ablations further validate the effectiveness of partial updating, selective expertization, and robustness-constrained expert selection.

</details>


### [111] [Group-Shared Low-Rank Approximation for Mobile-Efficient Pointwise Convolutions in Large-Kernel CNNs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26069)
*Hao Luo,Yiting Yang,Wenyi Zhao,Man Jiang,Zhijun Lin,Ghulam Mohiuddin,Ting Jiang,Kunming Luo,Zihao Zhang,Qingsen Yan,Guoqing Wang,Wei Dong,Peng Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对大卷积核CNN中逐点卷积参数占比过大（>87%）导致的边缘部署瓶颈，提出基于SVD的通道组共享低秩近似方法（CGS），通过组间共享投影矩阵显著降低存储成本，实现高性能视觉模型在资源受限边缘设备上的可行部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大卷积核CNN虽通过扩大感受野提升性能，但其参数二次方增长严重阻碍存储高效的边缘部署。现有高效架构专注于压缩深度可分离卷积，却忽视了关键问题：逐点卷积在模型中占据主要参数量（如RepLKNet-31B中>87%），成为资源受限边缘设备（如4-12GB RAM智能手机）部署的主要瓶颈，导致高昂存储成本和严重的内存加载约束。

Method: 提出通道组共享低秩近似（CGS），一种基于奇异值分解（SVD）的参数共享策略。CGS构建与SVD分解同构的结构化低秩范式，包含：跨通道组共享的高参数成本下/上投影矩阵，以及通道组特定的低参数成本可扩展对角矩阵。这种组共享设计在保持模型表达能力的同时实现显著参数压缩。

Result: 在大卷积核CNN模型（RepLKNet、ConvNeXt、SLaK）上的广泛实验表明，增强CGS的模型在竞争性性能和大幅降低存储成本之间取得经验性优良平衡。方法有效缓解存储约束，减少加载时的内存带宽压力和模型加载延迟。

Conclusion: CGS通过解决逐点卷积参数冗余问题，使预训练大卷积核CNN模型能够在边缘设备上可行部署，成功弥合高性能视觉模型与实际边缘应用之间的差距，为大卷积核CNN在资源受限环境中的实际应用提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Large-kernel Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs) deliver remarkable performance in vision tasks by significantly expanding receptive fields, yet their quadratic parameter growth critically impedes storage-efficient edge deployment. While existing efficient architectures adopt parameter-efficient depthwise separable convolution backbones that leverage techniques like low-rank approximation and weight sharing to compress depthwise convolutions, we identify a critical oversight: pointwise convolutions dominate parameter volume (>87% in models like RepLKNet-31B) and constitute the primary deployment bottleneck on resource-constrained edge devices. This results in prohibitive storage costs and severe memory-loading constraints on resource-limited devices (e.g., smartphones with 4-12 GB Random Access Memory (RAM)). To overcome this, we propose Channel Group-Shared (CGS) low-rank approximation, a novel Singular Value Decomposition (SVD)-based parameter-sharing strategy. CGS constructs a structured low-rank paradigm isomorphic to SVD decomposition, comprising shared (high-parameter-cost) down/up-projection matrices across channel groups within a layer and channel-group-specific (low-parameter-cost) scalable diagonal matrices. This group-sharing design achieves significant parameter reduction. Extensive experiments demonstrate that large-kernel CNNs (RepLKNet, ConvNeXt, SLaK) enhanced with CGS strike an empirically favorable balance between competitive performance and substantially reduced storage costs. Crucially, by alleviating storage constraints, reducing memory bandwidth pressure during loading, and minimizing model loading latency, CGS enables the feasible deployment of pre-trained large-kernel CNN models on edge devices, thereby bridging the gap between high-performance vision models and practical edge deployment.

</details>


### [112] [Agentic Autoresearch for Cell-Edge Power Control: Radically Redefining the Researcher's Role](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26093)
*Ahmad Khan,Akram Bin Sediq,Sara Azadegi Naeini,Raviraj S. Adve*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文展示了AI编程代理能够完全自主设计无线资源管理的机器学习算法，在26小时内自动完成了81次实验，以约600倍更低的推理成本达到了参考方法99.5%的性能，并发现了具有理论保证的算法结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的无线资源管理机器学习算法设计需要大量人工干预，包括架构设计、损失函数定义和训练流程制定，这些都是手工指定的。本研究旨在证明这一设计层面可以完全移交给自主代理来完成。

Method: 采用autoresearch协议，让AI编码代理编辑训练脚本、运行固定预算实验，根据单一不可变指标保留或丢弃更改。授予代理对架构族、输入表示、输出参数化、损失函数和任务采样法则的控制权，目标是解决多小区网络中的总和最小百分位功率控制问题。使用哈希固定的评估器、强制推理合约和预注册的伪造者来确保结果可信。

Result: 在26小时内进行了81次无人值守实验，代理在固定成本推理传递中达到了收敛的次最大化参考方法的99.5%性能，推理成本降低约600倍，从第一个可用架构开始缩小了94%的性能差距。单一参数集适用于所有网络规模和百分位目标。代理发现了可证明的结构而非调优的常数：所发现的输出参数化在训练权重的所有值下都能在最小百分位处精确重现最大最小最优分配。

Conclusion: 研究结果证明了AI代理完全自主设计机器学习算法的可行性，不仅能达到接近最优的性能，还能发现具有理论保证的算法结构，为无线资源管理领域的自动化算法设计开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Designing machine learning algorithms for wireless resource management is labour-intensive: the architecture, the loss function and the training recipe are all specified by hand. We demonstrate that this design layer can be surrendered to an autonomous agent in its entirety. We adopt the autoresearch protocol, in which an AI coding agent edits a training script, runs a fixed-budget experiment, and retains or discards the change according to a single immutable metric. We grant the agent authority over the architecture family, the input representation, the output parameterization, the loss function and the task-sampling law, and set it a target chosen for its difficulty: sum-least-percentile-rate power control across a multicell network. The formulation targets cell-edge throughput and is non-convex, non-smooth and strongly NP-hard away from its max-min vertex. Safeguards render the results trustworthy: a hash-pinned evaluator, an enforced inference contract and a pre-registered falsifier per experiment. In eighty-one unattended experiments over twenty-six hours, the agent reached $99.5\%$ of a converged minorization-maximization reference in one fixed-cost inference pass, at roughly $600\times$ lower inference cost, closing $94\%$ of the gap from its first working architecture, with one parameter set serving every network size and percentile target. It recovered provable structure rather than tuned constants: the output parameterization it discovered reproduces the exact max-min-optimal allocation at the minimum percentile, for every value of the trained weights.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [113] [VLM-based automatic multi-granularity graph representation of building layouts for design informatics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24886)
*Song Guo,Zhuoshi Chen,Maosu Li,Weimin Zhuang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于视觉语言模型（VLM）的自动图结构构建方法，用于从建筑平面图图像中提取多层次图表示（LoGs），实现了对公共建筑布局知识的结构化提取与任务自适应表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建筑平面图图像蕴含丰富的功能空间关系知识，对设计检索、知识推理和建筑信息模型（BIM）增强具有重要价值。然而，自动构建面向任务的图表示仍然面临挑战，缺乏有效的多粒度表示方法和自动化构建技术。

Method: 首先定义了公共建筑布局的多粒度图层次（Level-of-Graphs, LoGs），然后开发了基于视觉语言模型（VLM）的自动化图构建流程，包括节点识别、边推理、文本解析和图粗化四个步骤。以全球147个学术图书馆平面图为案例进行系统验证。

Result: VLM生成的图与人工标注图具有高度一致性（节点匹配率≥92%，每层平面图三层次图生成耗时509.3秒）。中粒度图在节点级区域预测任务中表现最优（Macro F1=0.647，复杂度仅为细粒度的65%），粗粒度图在图级布局质量评估中最有效（Spearman相关系数=0.610，复杂度仅为细粒度的16%）。

Conclusion: 该方法实现了从平面图图像中可扩展、免标注的结构化布局信息提取，将设计图像转化为知识表示，推进了设计信息学发展，增强了建筑全生命周期的设计信息利用能力。

Abstract: Architectural floorplan images encode rich relational knowledge among functional spaces, which underpins design retrieval, knowledge-based reasoning, and BIM enrichment through the building lifecycle. However, it remains challenging to automatically construct task-adaptive graph representations for public buildings. To address this gap, we first define a multi-granularity Level-of-Graphs (LoGs) for public building layouts. Methodologically, we present a Vision-Language Model (VLM)-based automatic LoG construction through node identification, edge inference, text parsing, and graph coarsening. VLM-generated representations are systematically evaluated and tested in real-world tasks, using 147 academic library floorplans worldwide as a case study. Experiments showed VLM-generated graphs were broadly consistent with human-labeled graphs (matched node ratio >= 92%; 509.3 s per floor plan for three-LoG graph generation). Meso-grained graphs yield the best node-level zone prediction (Macro F1 = 0.647, at 65% of fine-grained complexity), while coarse-grained graphs are most effective for graph-level layout quality evaluation (Spearman's \r{ho} = 0.610, at 16% of fine-grained complexity). By enabling scalable, annotation-free extraction of structured layout information from floorplan images, this study advances design informatics by converting plan images into knowledge representations, thereby enhancing the utilization of design information across the building life cycle.

</details>


### [114] [SIMGUIDE: Procedurally Grounded Multi-Context Representations for Personalized Agent Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24888)
*Chirag Shah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 用户表示格式而非内容是个性化AI代理的关键设计变量。SIMGUIDE通过结构化的Sim块和过程化示例显著优于传统RAG方法，在GPT-4o上提升7.9个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有个性化AI代理将用户视为扁平化单一实体，无法处理用户在不同生活情境中的冲突性优先级。核心问题不在于缺乏用户信息，而在于用户表示格式决定了代理能否有效利用这些信息。

Method: 提出SIMGUIDE方法，将用户上下文结构化为类型化的领域特定块（Sims），并用过去决策的过程化示例约束每个块。构建SIMBENCH基准测试套件，包含47个偏好条件规划任务，测试代理在不同用户上下文激活下的表现。

Result: 仅声明式Sim约束无法超越RAG，但过程化基础Sim在GPT-4o上优于RAG（+7.9偏好遵循点，p=0.013），在Claude Sonnet 4.5上也表现更优。参数级别上，任务匹配的LoRA微调比未适应基础模型提高12.8 ROUGE-L分数，按Sim类型而非用户身份路由适配器进一步提升7.3分。

Conclusion: 表示格式而非表示内容是一阶设计变量。结构化的用户表示格式对个性化AI代理性能具有决定性影响。

Abstract: Personalized AI agents overwhelmingly treat users as single entities: a flat profile concatenated into a prompt. This fails when the same person holds different priorities across life contexts -- and fails catastrophically when those priorities conflict. The core problem is not that agents lack information about users; it is that the format of user representations determines whether an agent can act on that information at all. We introduce SIMGUIDE, a method that structures user context into typed, domain-specific blocks called Sims and grounds each constraint with procedural examples drawn from past decisions. To evaluate this, we construct SIMBENCH, a diagnostic suite of 47 preference-conditioned planning tasks where the correct plan depends on which user context is active -- a property no existing benchmark tests. Declarative Sim constraints alone do not outperform retrieval-based personalization (RAG). Procedurally grounded Sims outperform RAG on GPT-4o (+7.9 Preference Adherence points, $p = 0.013$), and this advantage replicates on 100 $τ$-bench tasks across both GPT-4o and Claude Sonnet~4.5 ($p \leq 0.023$). At the parametric level, the same principle holds: training distribution dominates whether parametric adaptation succeeds at all. Task-matched LoRA fine-tuning improves generation quality by 12.8 ROUGE-L points over the unadapted base model, and routing adapters by Sim type rather than user identity adds a further 7.3 points, robust to 28% routing error. Representation format -- not representation content -- is the first-order design variable.

</details>


### [115] [Reliable LLM-Powered Decision Engines for Large-Scale Supply Chain Operations: Architecture, Safety, and Performance Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24889)
*Nirmal Kumar Jingar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种融合大语言模型与数学优化的混合决策引擎（LLM-DE），通过结合语义推理与形式化约束，在保证安全性的前提下实现了更智能、可扩展的供应链决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大规模供应链具有高度不确定性和易受干扰的特点，传统的基于规则或纯优化系统难以提供及时且具有韧性的决策。而异构数据源（如交易需求信号、非结构化中断报告）的增多为智能系统提供了机遇。

Method: 提出LLM-DE混合架构，将大语言模型的语义推理能力与数学优化、概率预测以及安全约束决策过滤相结合，构建端到端的决策流程，涵盖需求预测、库存优化、运输路径规划和中断缓解等任务。

Result: 实验验证表明，基于语言的推理与优化及形式约束相结合，能够生成更智能、更安全、更具可扩展性的供应链决策，在不确定性环境下表现优异。

Conclusion: 本研究为下一代智能供应链基础设施提供了新的架构设计、完整的算法流程和基于数学构建的操作决策系统理论框架，具有重要的理论和实践意义。

Abstract: Current large-scale supply chains are highly uncertain, dynamic, and disruption prone that are challenging to serve up timely and resilient decisions through traditional rule-based and optimization-only systems. The increasing supply of heterogeneous data sources, such as transactional demand signals and unstructured disruption report, presents a chance of intelligent systems, which could reason, adapt and optimize at the same time. A hybrid architecture that combines large language models (LLMs) with mathematical optimization, probabilistic forecasting, and safety-constrained decision filtering is proposed in this paper as a performance of a Decision Engine, which is called LLM-Powered Decision Engine (LLM-DE). In comparison to purely data-driven or heuristic solutions, LLM-DE integrates semantic reasoning with LLM with a set of performance and safety guarantees that allow safe decision-making in large-scale supply chain processes. The suggested framework enables the end-to-end decision making such as demand forecasting, inventory optimization, and transportation routing and disruption mitigation. The findings affirm that language-based reasoning combined with optimization and formal constraints can be used to come up with not only smarter but also safer and more scalable supply chain decisions. This research provides a new architecture, a complete pipeline of algorithm, and a formulation based on mathematical constructs of the operational decision systems incorporating LLM. The proposed model offers a pragmatic and theoretical basis of the next-generation intelligent supply chain infrastructures that can be implemented to work dependably in the face of uncertainty and massive complexity.

</details>


### [116] [AI-Powered Mental Health Chatbots in Africa: A Systematic Review and Culturally Adaptive Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24890)
*Matshepo Lebese,Pitso Tsibolane*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了52项AI心理健康聊天机器人研究，发现其在提升可及性和缓解症状方面具有潜力，但在非洲语境下存在文化适应不足、语言局限和基础设施障碍，并提出了文化适应性数字心理健康框架(CADMH)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非洲心理健康服务面临基础设施不足、社会偏见和专业人员严重短缺等挑战，而AI聊天机器人作为低成本、易获取的心理支持工具在全球兴起，亟需评估其在非洲语境下的适用性与局限性。

Method: 对2017至2025年间发表的52项实证研究进行系统性综述，批判性分析这些AI聊天bot在文化、语言和基础设施层面对非洲语境的相关性与适应性。

Result: 研究发现AI聊天机器人能提升心理健康服务可及性、减轻焦虑抑郁症状并扩大心理社会支持范围，但大多数系统仍基于西方模式和英语设计，缺乏非洲本土化适应、包容性和可持续性，存在明显的研究与实践空白。

Conclusion: 作者提出了文化适应性数字心理健康(CADMH)框架，整合非洲文化价值观、多语言设计、移动优先优化和伦理保障措施，为AI聊天机器人在非洲医疗保健中的整合应用提供研究、实践和政策指导。

Abstract: Mental health challenges in Africa remain under-addressed due to inadequate infrastructure, stigma, and a chronic shortage of professionals. Artificial Intelligence (AI)-powered chatbots are emerging globally as low-cost, accessible tools that can offer psychological support. This paper presents a systematic review of 52 empirical studies published between 2017 and 2025, critically analysing their cultural, linguistic, and infrastructural relevance to African contexts. The findings demonstrate the potential of AI chatbots to improve accessibility, reduce symptoms of anxiety and depression, and expand psychosocial support, yet reveal limited African-specific adaptation. Most systems remain rooted in Western models and English-language designs, leaving critical gaps in local relevance, inclusivity, and sustainability. Based on the findings, the authors develop a Culturally Adaptive Digital Mental Health (CADMH) framework that integrates African cultural values, multilingual design, mobile-first optimisation, and ethical safeguards. The study highlights opportunities and barriers for integrating AI chatbots into African healthcare, offering guidance for research, practice, and policy.

</details>


### [117] [Measurement-Budget Allocation in Quantum Learning with Finite-Shot Generalization Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24891)
*Ferhat Ozgur Catak*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究了近量子硬件上有限测量预算下训练状态数量与每状态采样次数的最优分配问题，推导出分布无关的泛化界并给出了保守的最优分配方案，通过PennyLane仿真验证了理论结果的正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在近期量子硬件上估计Born概率需要重复的电路执行。在固定的测量预算B下，量子学习实验必须决定使用多少个不同的训练状态n，以及每个状态分配多少次采样S。这个权衡问题对于量子分类器的设计至关重要。

Method: 针对具有固定或独立选择测量算符M的二分类量子分类器（理想得分为Tr(Mρ)），证明了分布无关的泛化界，该界分离了有限样本和有限采样的贡献。样本项标度为√(d/n)，采样项标度为√((log n)/S)。在约束B=nS下，推导出保守的闭式代理函数，给出最优分配规则：n* = 2√(2dB/log(2B/δ))，S* = B/n*。

Result: 理论结果表明，代理函数与精确最小化器具有相同的渐近标度，产生最坏情况速率B^(-1/4)。通过PennyLane仿真验证，在2量子比特和4量子比特变分量子电路上测试九个合成二元分类基准，所有配置下单侧经验泛化间隙均低于理论上界。该保证适用于全类二元量子测量，因而是保守的。

Conclusion: 为近期量子学习系统的有限采样评估和实验前规划提供了保守的统计指导，补充了硬件级调度和电路设计考虑。将保证扩展到完全自适应的含噪训练仍是一个开放问题。

Abstract: On near-term quantum hardware, estimating a Born probability requires repeated circuit executions. A quantum learning experiment with a fixed measurement budget $B$ must therefore decide how many distinct training states $n$ to use and how many shots $S$ to allocate to each state. We study this tradeoff for binary quantum classifiers with fixed or independently selected measurement operators $M$, where the ideal score is $\Tr(Mρ)$. We prove a distribution-free generalization bound that separates the finite-sample and finite-shot contributions. The sample term scales as $\sqrt{d/n}$, while the shot term scales as $\sqrt{(\log n)/S}$; under the constraint $B=nS$, these two terms move in opposite directions. Minimising a conservative closed-form surrogate of the bound gives the allocation rule $\nstar = 2\sqrt{2dB/\log(2B/δ)}$ and $\Sstar = B/\nstar$. This surrogate has the same asymptotic scaling as the exact minimizer and yields a worst-case rate of $B^{-1/4}$. The guarantee is intentionally conservative, since it applies to the full class of binary quantum measurements. We complement the theory with PennyLane simulations using 2-qubit and 4-qubit variational quantum circuits on nine synthetic binary classification benchmarks. In all tested configurations, the one-sided empirical generalization gap remains below the theoretical bound. The result provides a conservative statistical guideline for allocating measurement budgets in finite-shot evaluation and pre-experimental planning for near-term quantum learning systems, complementing hardware-level scheduling and circuit-design considerations. Extending the guarantee to fully adaptive shot-noisy training remains an open problem.

</details>


### [118] [Natural Language Input, Semantic Track Representation, and LLM Inference: Making the Maritime Information Exchange Model Tractable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24892)
*Frederick Roth*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用大语言模型（LLM）使海上信息交换模型（MIEM）和Rich Semantic Track模型变得实用的架构，通过将自然语言输入转换为语义断言记录（SARs），消除了操作员学习形式本体论语言的障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语义跟踪模型在国防和执法领域的采用障碍在于操作员必须学习形式本体论语言并手动将观察结果编码为类型化逻辑断言，这限制了其实际应用。

Method: 提出一种架构：操作员用自然语言提供观察结果；LLM将其转换为类型化的语义断言记录（SARs），即捕获n元关系的命名案例框架；知识图谱累积这些SARs；第二个LLM在图上执行推理、异常检测和假设排序。

Result: 通过两个详细示例（9/11时代预攻击指标场景和海上货物检查场景）展示了从自然语言输入到SAR表示再到推理输出的完整流程，证明该架构使Track Model和MIEM能够立即部署。

Conclusion: 该架构使Track Model和MIEM能够利用现有技术立即部署，建立了防止该方法被私有化垄断的现有技术基础，并将该方法建立在将语义跟踪表示与神经流形几何相联系的理论框架之上。

Abstract: We describe a practical architecture for making the Maritime Information Exchange Model (MIEM) and the broader Rich Semantic Track model tractable using current large language model (LLM) technology. The barrier to adoption of semantic track models in defense and law enforcement has been the requirement that operators learn formal ontology languages and manually encode observations as typed logical assertions. We propose eliminating this barrier entirely: operators contribute observations in natural language; an LLM translates these into typed Semantic Assertion Records (SARs), which are named case frames that capture n-ary relations in a single compact structure; a knowledge graph accumulates the SARs; and a second LLM pass performs inference, anomaly detection, and hypothesis ranking over the graph. We work through two detailed examples (a 9/11-era pre-attack indicator scenario and a maritime cargo inspection scenario) showing the full pipeline from natural language input to SAR representation to inference output. We argue that this architecture makes the Track Model and MIEM immediately deployable with current technology, establishes prior art against proprietary enclosure of the approach, and grounds the method in a theoretical framework connecting semantic track representations to neural manifold geometry.

</details>


### [119] [Poisoning Agentic Alpha: Adversarial Vulnerabilities Across Roles and Architectures in Multi-Agent Trading Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24069)
*CheolWon Na,Hao Ni,Lukasz Szpruch,Zhangyang Wang,Dhagash Mehta,Saurabh Nagrecha,Alejandro Lopez-Lira,Chanyeol Choi,Yongjae Lee,Jee-Hyong Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了基于大语言模型的多智能体交易系统对抗攻击问题，揭示了四种功能角色（分析师、研究员、交易员、风险管理）在四种通信拓扑结构下均无内在鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的多智能体交易系统正快速从研究原型转向控制真实资产的实战部署，其跨智能体通信机制既是有效性来源也是安全弱点——被污染的信号可在传播中影响最终决策并导致实际经济损失。现有攻击假设拥有系统内部特权访问，而本文关注更具现实可达性的威胁模型（攻击者仅能接触源数据和提示词），降低了攻击门槛。

Method: 提出角色特异性对抗者威胁模型；沿角色轴将交易流水线分解为四个功能角色（分析师、研究员、交易员、风险管理），并为每个角色匹配适配其接口的攻击；沿结构轴评估四种通信拓扑在数据级和智能体级攻击下的表现，引入对抗信号保持得分（APS）作为事后分析工具；在五种资产、两种骨干模型、两个目标方向上进行实验。

Result: 实验揭示核心发现：没有任何架构具有内在鲁棒性；通过APS指标分析了不同设计为何具有不同鲁棒性；系统刻画了对抗信号如何进入多智能体交易系统以及能在多大程度上向最终决策传播。

Conclusion: 研究发现为未来设计更安全、更鲁棒的智能体交易系统提供了重要洞见，强调了在系统设计阶段即需考虑对抗防御，而非依赖单一架构保障安全性。

Abstract: LLM-based multi-agent trading systems, in which specialized agents collaborate through structured communication to produce trading decisions, are moving rapidly from research prototypes to live deployments that control real assets. The same inter-agent communication that makes them effective also exposes them: a corrupted signal can propagate to the final decision and translate into realized financial loss. Unlike prior attacks that presume privileged access to system internals, we restrict the adversary to what is practically reachable---the source data and prompts agents consume---yielding a low-barrier, and thus democratized threat model instantiated as role-specific adversaries.
  We present the first systematic empirical study in the financial domain to characterize how an adversarial signal enters a multi-agent trading system and how far it survives toward the decision. Along the role axis, we decompose a widely-used trading pipeline into four functional roles---Analyst, Researcher, Trader, and Risk Manager---and pair each with an attack matched to its interface. Along the structural axis, we evaluate four communication topologies under data- and agent-level attacks, using the Adversarial Signal Preservation Score (APS) as a post-hoc lens on why some designs are more robust than others. We conduct experiments across five assets, two backbones, and two target directions. A central finding is that no architecture is inherently robust. These findings provide insights for the future design of safer and more robust agentic trading systems.

</details>


### [120] [Same-Player Verification for Account Consistency in Counter-Strike 2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24893)
*Xuchen Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究针对CS2游戏中的账号身份一致性问题，提出了基于比赛录像行为轨迹的"同一玩家验证"方法，通过低级别操作行为特征训练模型，ROC AUC达到0.931，结合历史数据聚合可提升至0.986。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在竞技FPS游戏（如CS2）中，账号完整性审查需要判断账号近期行为是否与历史操作者一致，这涉及临时替打、代练、高技能玩家使用低段位账号等场景。传统人工审查需要将当前比赛与多场历史比赛进行对比，效率低下，亟需自动化解决方案。

Method: 研究将审查任务形式化为"同一玩家验证"问题：（1）基于CS2游戏理解，从比赛录像（demo）中提取单玩家的行为轨迹，构建demo-player行为指纹，涵盖准星控制、移动-急停-射击协调、经济/购买、战斗/交战和时间节奏五个维度；（2）从1330个CS2 demo中提取13300个观测样本，从8840万候选对中采样663,590个同/不同玩家对用于监督学习；（3）训练成对判断模型并设计历史聚合评估机制。

Result: 最终成对模型达到平均ROC AUC 0.931，在95%精确率下实现0.722的不同玩家召回率。特征分析表明最强的身份信号来自低级操作（准星控制、射击节奏、移动-急停-射击协调），表明稳定的低级别机械习惯比单场比赛表现结果更具判别价值。在账号历史聚合评估中，增加历史深度使AUC从K=1基线的0.931提升至K=10时的0.986。

Conclusion: CS2 demo行为数据可有效支持监督式同一玩家验证任务，通过多demo历史聚合可实现账号级身份一致性建模，为游戏账号完整性审查提供了可行的自动化技术路径。

Abstract: In competitive first-person shooter (FPS) games such as Counter-Strike 2 (CS2), account-integrity review often asks whether an account's recent behavior remains consistent with its historical operator. This consistency question arises in cases such as temporary substitution, rank boosting, and high-skill players using lower-ranked accounts, where manual review requires comparing a current match against multiple historical matches. We formulate this review task as same-player verification: we encode the behavioral trajectory of a single player in a match replay (demo) as a demo-player behavioral fingerprint, and train a model to judge whether two behavioral observations come from the same real player. Grounded in CS2 game understanding, the fingerprints cover crosshair control, movement-stop-fire coordination, economy/buy, combat/engagement, and temporal rhythm. From 1,330 CS2 demos we extract 13,300 demo-player observations, and sample 663,590 same/different pairs from an 88.4M candidate-pair space for supervised training and evaluation. The final pairwise model reaches an average ROC AUC of 0.931 and achieves 0.722 different-player recall at 95% precision. Feature analysis shows that the strongest identity signals come from low-level operations, especially crosshair control, firing rhythm, and movement-stop-fire coordination, indicating that stable low-level mechanical habits are more informative for this verification task than single-match performance outcomes. In the account-history aggregation evaluation, increasing history depth raises AUC from the K=1 single-pair baseline of 0.931 to 0.986 at K=10. These results show that CS2 demo behavior can support supervised same-player verification and account-level identity-consistency modeling through multi-demo history aggregation.

</details>


### [121] [Federation Is Nearly Free, Reasoning Is Not: Tradeoffs for AI Co-Scientists in Protein Characterization Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25215)
*Maia Kapur,Timothy Boe,Abby Jerger,Paul Rigor*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LLM选择对预测质量的影响远超其他因素（Opus准确率92%-94% vs o4-mini 40%-50%），PPO策略以零成本达到88%准确率但无推理痕迹，联合拓扑对性能影响可忽略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言驱动的自主科学工作流存在灵活性与推理能力同确定性、可重现性、可观察性之间的根本权衡；智能体跨机构边界的通信中联合拓扑会影响延迟和成本，需要系统评估这些权衡。

Method: 在生产的科学智能体平台上进行控制性消融实验，使用可验证任务：给定蛋白质序列，要求智能体跨工具路由来可靠表征其功能。对比联合拓扑、经典强化学习与LLM驱动框架、语言模型、提示词专业知识等因素，并按蛋白质新颖性分层分析结果。

Result: LLM选择主导预测质量（Opus 92%-94% vs o4-mini 40%-50%）；PPO策略以零token成本、最快延迟和完美一致性达到88%准确率，但不产生推理痕迹；专家提示的LLM准确率最高但成本高且一致性较低；任务最难时提示词依赖性最大；联合拓扑对性能的惩罚可忽略不计。

Conclusion: 为科学工作流部署智能体提供可操作指导：对于常规、可验证的任务，廉价确定性策略能以前沿水平准确率和完整可重现性交付结果；灵活的LLM推理最适合开放式探索任务。

Abstract: Natural language driven autonomous co-scientist workflows involve a fundamental trade-off between flexibility and reasoning at the expense of determinism, reproducibility, and observability. Such agents increasingly must communicate across institutional boundaries, where federation topology can shape latency and cost. We systematically evaluated these tradeoffs using a controlled ablation on a production agentic platform for science. We use a verifiable task: given a protein sequence, we ask an agent to confidently characterize its function by routing across common tools. We compare federation topology, classic RL vs LLM-driven harnesses, language model, and prompt expertise. We also stratify results by protein novelty. We find that the choice of LLM dominated prediction quality far more than topology or prompting (Opus ~92%-94% vs o4-mini ~40%-50%). The PPO policy was nearly as accurate as the best LLM (88%) at zero token cost, fastest latency, and perfect consistency, but yields no reasoning trace. Expert prompted LLMs reached the highest accuracy but were high-cost and less consistent; prompt dependence was largest when the task was hardest. Federation imposed a negligible penalty on performance. These results offer actionable guidance for deploying agents for scientific workflows: for routine, verifiable tasks, a cheap deterministic policy delivers near-frontier accuracy with complete reproducibility, while flexible LLM reasoning is best reserved for open-ended discovery.

</details>


### [122] [Semantic Graph Unification for Industrial Digital Threads: Bridging 11 Heterogeneous Manufacturing Systems Through Ontology-Driven Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24918)
*Grama Chethan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出基于本体驱动的RDF知识图谱框架，通过五阶段ETL管道统一11个数据源，实现工业数字线程的语义融合。实验表明69%的可发现信号需要跨系统图连接，验证集F1达1.0。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代制造企业运行ERP、MES、PLM等多个异构系统，形成数据孤岛，阻碍整体分析、根因调查和工业4.0可追溯性。点对点集成的复杂度呈O(n^2)增长且依赖脆弱，亟需语义层面的统一解决方案。

Method: 构建本体驱动的RDF知识图谱，融合ISA-95、OPC UA、RAMI 4.0等标准，定义78个RDFS类、108个对象属性和243个数据属性。采用五阶段ETL管道实现11个模拟数据源的语义统一，建立97个owl:sameAs身份链接。开发自动发现引擎，应用跨站点关联、告警覆盖、ECN影响分析、CUSUM/EWMA漂移检测等9类策略发现跨系统洞察。

Result: 阻塞24个跨系统工具后，召回率从1.00降至0.31（F1从1.00降至0.48），证明69%的可发现信号依赖跨系统图连接。留一法消融实验确认9个策略中有6个贡献独特信号。在65信号清单上验证F1=1.00（95% Clopper-Pearson置信区间[0.79, 1.00]）。

Conclusion: 语义知识图谱能有效打破制造系统数据孤岛，跨系统图连接对洞察发现至关重要。框架通过287个MCP工具暴露SPARQL原生语义层供LLM代理使用，五个行业模板验证了跨制造垂直领域的模式稳定性。

Abstract: Modern manufacturing enterprises operate heterogeneous systems -- ERP, MES, PLM, SCADA, QMS, SCM -- each with its own data model and API. The resulting silos prevent holistic analysis, delay root-cause investigation, and obstruct Industry 4.0 traceability. Point-to-point integration scales as O(n^2) and accumulates brittle dependencies. This paper presents an open framework for semantic graph unification of industrial digital threads. An ontology-driven RDF knowledge graph unifies data from 11 simulated sources across nine domains through a five-stage ETL pipeline with automated entity resolution spanning 97 owl:sameAs identity links. The ontology encompasses 78 RDFS classes, 108 object properties, and 243 data properties, drawing on ISA-95, OPC UA, eClass, the Asset Administration Shell, RAMI 4.0, and additional standards. An automated discovery engine applies nine strategy categories -- cross-station correlation, alarm coverage, ECN impact, CUSUM/EWMA drift detection -- to surface insights spanning system boundaries. The primary empirical result: blocking 24 cross-system tools reduces recall from 1.00 to 0.31 (F1 from 1.00 to 0.48), showing that 69% of discoverable signals require cross-system graph joins. Leave-one-out ablation confirms six of nine strategies contribute unique signals. Verification against a 65-signal manifest (16 positive, 49 null) yields F1 = 1.00 (95% Clopper-Pearson CI [0.79, 1.00]); as the manifest was author-constructed, this constitutes verification not independent validation. The graph is exposed to LLM agents via 287 Model Context Protocol tools as a SPARQL-native semantic layer. Five industry templates (aerospace, CPG, pharma, medical devices, turbine blades) demonstrate schema stability across manufacturing verticals.

</details>


### [123] [post-graph-rag: A PostgreSQL-Native Graph RAG Engine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24921)
*Chandan Rajah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了post-graph-rag，一个开源的图检索增强生成引擎，通过在单一PostgreSQL数据库中集成文本块、实体图和社区摘要，并在提取时执行严格的质量约束和时序管理，解决了现有系统在基础设施复杂性、图质量和时序演进方面的三大问题。实验表明，该方法构建了更密集、更可查询的知识图谱，支持时序查询，同时降低了查询延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于图的检索增强生成系统存在三大缺陷：(1)基础设施复杂，需要维护向量存储、图数据库和文档存储的一致性；(2)图质量低下，提取管道从不拒绝输出，导致图中充斥着无意义的边；(3)缺乏时序管理，图只累积不更新，无法区分过时和当前事实。这些局限性严重影响了系统的实用性和可维护性。

Method: post-graph-rag采用统一的PostgreSQL数据库架构，利用pgvector进行向量搜索和边表进行图遍历。核心创新包括：(1)提取时不变性约束——拒绝模糊谓词、代词名称和裸数量，规范化谓词到可选词汇表，通过模型提供的别名将实体解析到单一顶点；(2)时序层设计——关系携带从文本提取的有效期，后续文档可自动取代早期不兼容的断言，支持as-of查询；(3)否定关系处理——被拒绝的关系保留正向谓词并标记否定标志。

Result: 在三个语料库上与LightRAG对比，使用相同的提取和嵌入模型：post-graph-rag构建了更密集的图，每实体关系数高达2.4倍；边标签更明确，每关系不同标签数为0.46-0.58（控制词汇下0.11），而LightRAG为0.77-1.33；查询性能相当但延迟更低；在时序演进方面显著优于基线：在小说序列和十年财务报告上分别检测到13和8个被取代的关系，而基线为零。

Conclusion: post-graph-rag证明了通过工程化设计可以在单一数据库中实现高质量的图RAG系统。提取时的质量约束和时序管理机制显著提升了图的密度、可查询性和实用性。该方法为构建可维护、可演进的图RAG系统提供了可行方案，代码已开源，具有重要的工程实践价值。

Abstract: Graph-based retrieval-augmented generation connects facts that no single passage states, but current implementations pay for that three times: in infrastructure, requiring a vector store, graph database and document store to be kept consistent; in graph quality, because an extraction pipeline that never refuses output fills the graph with edges that assert nothing; and over time, because a graph that only accumulates treats superseded and current facts alike.
  post-graph-rag is an open-source engine addressing all three. Text chunks with embeddings, a canonical entity graph and community summaries live in one PostgreSQL database, with pgvector for search and edge tables for traversal. Extraction-time invariants run before anything is written: vague predicates, pronominal names and bare quantities are rejected; predicates are normalised onto an optional vocabulary; entities resolve to one vertex per canonical name via model-supplied aliases; and denied relations keep the positive predicate under a negation flag. A temporal layer lets relations carry a validity period from the prose, lets a later document supersede an earlier incompatible assertion from document order alone, and answers as-of queries.
  Against LightRAG on three corpora under identical extraction and embedding models, post-graph-rag builds a denser graph everywhere, up to $2.4\times$ the relations per entity, and a more queryable one: distinct edge labels run at 0.46 to 0.58 per relation, 0.11 under a controlled vocabulary, against 0.77 to 1.33. It answers comparably with lower query latency, and supports temporal evolution the baseline lacks: 13 and 8 relationships superseded on a novel sequence and a decade of filings, against zero. These are engineering measurements, not a benchmark result.
  Code: https://github.com/crajah/post-graph-rag, https://github.com/crajah/post-graph

</details>


### [124] [Candidate supply and answer selection shape the value of LLM judging in multi-agent systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25937)
*Jia-Hao Ji,Sijie Li,Jiabei Cheng,Zixi She,Jin-Tai Yu,Zhiyuan Yuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 多智能体系统常因共识机制缺陷导致正确答案被错误答案覆盖；通过引入LLM评估器并结合答案频率改进最终选择规则，可将准确率从63.82%提升至约70.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体系统（MAS）中存在一个关键问题：系统已生成正确答案，却最终报告错误答案。由于候选生成、智能体通信和最终答案选择规则通常同时变化，这一现象难以解释。本研究旨在理解这一现象的根本原因，并探索如何通过评估器选择压力来改善系统表现。

Method: 研究将多智能体推理概念化为候选生成、同行通信和终端选择的演化管道。通过分析MMLU-Pro、GPQA、MedXpertQA和MuSR等基准的15,336个问题，并重放来自五个基准的81,390个固定候选池（涵盖16,278个问题），系统性地测试了LLM评估器作为选择信号的有效性。研究还提出了将答案频率与评估器评价相结合的新选择规则。

Result: 三个核心发现：(1) 正确答案常已存在于候选池中，但系统仍可能收敛并报告错误答案；(2) 评估器可靠性并非模型固定属性，而随任务、生成器和正确答案稀缺度变化；(3) 将答案频率与评估器评价结合仅改变最终选择规则，即可将准确率从63.82%提升至70.82-70.95%，主要挽救了被流行错误答案数量压制的正确答案。

Conclusion: 研究表明，生成更多候选样本的价值取决于这些样本是否能使正确答案存在、频繁或可识别。通过隔离生成、识别和选择三个阶段，本研究为设计能够保护已生成正确答案不被丢失的多智能体架构建立了诊断基础，为未来多智能体系统的优化提供了理论框架。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems (MAS) sometimes already have the potential to answer correctly, but still report a wrong answer. Explaining this outcome is difficult because generation, communication and final answer-selection rules usually change simultaneously. We conceptualize multi-agent reasoning as an evolutionary pipeline of candidate generation, peer communication and terminal selection, wherein consensus without quality control can exhibit patterns of memetic drift. We study two questions: (1) when an LLM judge provides effective selection pressure by supplying a signal of answer correctness for candidates generated in a multi-agent system, and (2) when using that signal improves the reported answer. To map judge reliability, we analysed 15,336 questions from MMLU-Pro, GPQA, MedXpertQA and MuSR, with Humanity's Last Exam analysed separately. To test these rules, we replayed 81,390 fixed candidate pools drawn from 16,278 questions across five benchmarks. We report three findings. (1) A correct answer is often already present among the generated candidates, but the system can still converge on and report a wrong answer. (2) Judge reliability is not a fixed trait of the model, but varies with the task, the generator and how rare the correct answer is. (3) Combining answer frequency with the judge's evaluation changed only the final answer-selection rule and raised accuracy from 63.82% to 70.82-70.95%, primarily by rescuing correct answers that were outnumbered by popular errors. In the systems studied here, the value of generating more candidates depends on whether those extra samples make correct answers present, frequent or recognisable. By isolating generation, recognition and selection, these findings establish a diagnostic basis for designing multi-agent architectures that protect generated correct answers from being lost.

</details>


### [125] [FrontierChallenge: Evaluating Scientific Workflow Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24979)
*Liangcai Su,Zhaopeng Feng,Zhuo Chen,Zhen Zhang,Xiang Lin,Ruilin Li,Handuo Zhang,Ning Wang,Kailong Wen,Yueqi Guo,Feng Xing,Yiling Guo,Chenxiong Qian,Simon Shaolei Du,Lidong Bing,Xinyu Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出了FrontierChallenge跨领域科学工作流基准测试，包含300个端到端科学任务，评估了97个任务。最佳模型配置仅完成20.6%的任务，部分进度与最终完成率严重不匹配，75.5%的失败任务仍声称已完成，表明需评估完整科学交付物。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有科学智能体基准测试主要关注最终答案、孤立程序或单一领域，缺乏对跨领域端到端科学工作流的评估。科学智能体在实际应用中需要分析数据、执行代码并产出完整的科学成果，因此需要新的基准来全面评估工作流执行和科学交付物的完整性。

Method: 构建FrontierChallenge跨领域基准测试，包含300个端到端科学工作流，发布并评估97个任务，涵盖量子化学、分子动力学、材料表征、分析化学、生命科学和电化学/环境六个领域。每个任务提供固定输入并指定所需科学交付物集合。使用三种智能体框架评估12个前沿模型，采用Pass Rate衡量完全完成任务的比例，Avg. Score捕捉部分进度。

Result: 最佳配置仅完成97个任务中的20个，Pass Rate为20.6%。分析化学和电化学/环境领域部分进度与完成率严重脱节：Avg. Score分别达到87.6和94.9，但最高Pass Rate仅4%和0%。在未通过的Claude Code轨迹中，75.5%仍以声称完成的表述结束。高部分分数或自信的完成声明都不能可靠指示科学任务已被完整交付。

Conclusion: 当前前沿模型在跨领域端到端科学工作流任务上表现有限，部分进度无法有效转化为完整交付。自信的完成声明与实际完成情况严重不符。评估科学智能体时需要同时考察端到端工作流执行和科学交付物的完整性，而非仅依赖部分指标或模型自评。

Abstract: Scientific agents increasingly analyze data, execute code, and produce research artifacts, yet most benchmarks emphasize final answers, isolated programs, or a single domain. We introduce FrontierChallenge, a cross-domain benchmark comprising 300 end-to-end scientific workflows. In this paper, we release and evaluate 97 of these tasks, spanning quantum chemistry, molecular dynamics, materials characterization, analytical chemistry, life science, and electrochemistry/environment. Each task provides fixed inputs and specifies a bundle of required scientific deliverables. We evaluate twelve frontier models with three agent scaffolds. Pass Rate measures the fraction of tasks satisfying the full-completion criterion, while Avg. Score captures partial progress. Each of the best-performing configurations completed only 20 of the 97 released tasks, yielding a Pass Rate of 20.6%. Partial progress translated especially poorly into complete delivery in analytical chemistry and electrochemistry/environment: Avg. Scores reached 87.6 and 94.9, but the highest Pass Rates were only 4% and 0%. Among non-passing Claude Code trajectories, 75.5% still ended with language claiming completion. These findings show that neither high partial scores nor confident claims of completion reliably indicate that a scientific task has been fully delivered, highlighting the need to evaluate end-to-end workflow execution and the completeness of scientific deliverables together.

</details>


### [126] [ProgRouter: Online Progress-Guided Orchestration for Multi-Agent LLM Workflows under Quality-Cost Tradeoffs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25992)
*Songyuan Li,Ahmed M. Abdelmoniem,Shiqiang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ProgRouter框架，通过在线进度引导的自适应路由选择LLM代理，在保证任务质量的同时显著降低多智能体LLM工作流的运营成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM工作流虽然能解决复杂开放性任务，但由于反复的LLM调用和长程上下文累积导致运营成本高昂。现有的级联路由方法采用一次性查询级决策，无法适应多步工作流的动态、状态依赖特性，即每一步选择合适的LLM取决于任务进展、剩余难度和成本效率要求的演变。

Method: 提出ProgRouter在线进度引导路由框架，包含三个核心组件：(1)多视角任务进度评分器，结合粗粒度工作流结果与细粒度子任务完成、进度趋势和工作流状态质量信号；(2)双路径任务进度预测器和自适应元门控机制，估算每个候选路由LLM的进度增益；(3)在线逐步路由决策机制，平衡进度增益、任务时间预算和长期运营成本效率。

Result: 在HumanEval Plus、MBPP、MATH-500和ASQA数据集上进行实验，涵盖智能体代码生成、数学推理和检索增强长形式问答任务，结果表明ProgRouter相比关键基线方法降低了运营成本，同时保持了强任务求解性能。

Conclusion: ProgRouter成功解决了多智能体LLM工作流中的动态路由问题，通过进度引导的自适应决策机制实现了质量与成本的有效平衡，为复杂任务的LLM工作流优化提供了新思路。

Abstract: Multi-agent large language model (LLM) workflows have emerged as a powerful paradigm for solving complex, open-ended tasks through collaborative reasoning among specialized LLM agents, but they incur substantial operating costs due to repeated LLM invocations and long-horizon context accumulation. Existing cascade routing methods make one-shot, query-level decisions and cannot adapt to the dynamic, state-dependent nature of multi-step workflows, in which the right LLM at each step depends on evolving task progress, remaining task difficulty, and cost-efficiency requirements. We present ProgRouter, an online progress-guided routing framework that adaptively selects LLM agents across workflow steps to preserve task-solving quality while adhering to time and cost budgets. ProgRouter introduces a multi-view task progress scorer that combines coarse workflow outcome regimes with fine-grained signals on subtask completion, progress trends, and workflow state quality. Then, a dual-path task progress predictor and an adaptive meta-gating mechanism estimate the progress gain for each candidate routed LLM. ProgRouter makes online step-wise routing decisions that balance progress gain, task time budgets, and long-term operating cost efficiency. Experiments on HumanEval Plus, MBPP, MATH-500, and ASQA, spanning agentic code generation, mathematical reasoning, and retrieval-augmented long-form question answering, demonstrate that ProgRouter reduces the operating cost relative to key baselines while maintaining strong task-solving performance.

</details>


### [127] [Solving Robust POMDPs with Omega-regular Objectives via Partially Observable Stochastic Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24986)
*Durgam Latha,Dion Reji,S. Akshay,Djordje Zikelic,Shankaranarayanan Krishna*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文证明了(s,a)-矩形多面体不确定性集的鲁棒部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程(RPOMDP)与部分可观测随机博弈(POSG)在omega-正则目标下的语义等价性，并推导出求解RPOMDP的计算复杂性上下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典POMDP假设转移概率已知，但实际应用中往往只能确定转移概率属于某个不确定性集合。为了解决这一现实问题，需要研究转移概率不确定的鲁棒POMDP(RPOMDP)，特别是在涵盖可达性、安全性和线性时序逻辑等广泛目标的omega-正则目标下的求解问题。

Method: 针对(s,a)-矩形RPOMDP与多面体不确定性集，本文构建了从RPOMDP到部分可观测随机博弈(POSG)的归约，并首次证明了双向归约的可行性，从而建立了两者在omega-正则目标下的语义等价性。

Result: 证明了(s,a)-矩形多面体不确定性集的RPOMDP与POSG在omega-正则目标下语义等价；推导出了求解不同omega-正则目标下RPOMDP的一系列新的计算复杂性结果，包括上界和下界；作为推论，也获得了RMDP的新计算复杂性结果。

Conclusion: 通过建立RPOMDP与POSG之间的等价性，本文为求解不确定性环境下的决策问题提供了理论基础，并为计算复杂性分析提供了统一的框架，推动了鲁棒决策理论的发展。

Abstract: Robust POMDPs (RPOMDPs) generalize classical POMDPs to the setting where exact transition probabilities are not known -- rather, they are only known to belong to some uncertainty set of values. In this work, we study the problem of solving RPOMDPs with general omega-regular objectives, which subsume a broad class of objectives such as reachability, safety, and linear temporal logic (LTL) objectives. We show that, for (s,a)-rectangular RPOMDPs with polytopic uncertainty sets, the problem of solving RPOMDPs under omega-regular objectives can be reduced to solving partially observable stochastic games (POSGs) under omega-regular objectives. Moreover, we show for the first time that reductions can be constructed in both directions, establishing the semantic equivalence between (s,a)-rectangular RPOMDPs with polytopic uncertainty sets and POSGs. This allows us to derive a range of new computational complexity results, including both upper and lower complexity bounds, on solving RPOMDPs with different omega-regular objectives. As a corollary, we also derive new computational complexity results for RMDPs.

</details>


### [128] [CVE-SAI: Counterfactual Visual Evidence-Guided Selective Attribute Indexing for Risk-Controlled E-commerce Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25023)
*Xiaolong Sun,Qichao Wang,Hangyu Li,Liang Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CVE-SAI方法，通过反事实视觉证据引导的选择性属性索引，解决电商多模态产品模型中缺乏视觉支持验证、混淆预测与索引、缺乏风险控制的问题，在保证安全性的前提下提升检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态电商属性补全方法存在三大缺陷：(1)仅优化属性答案准确率而不验证视觉支持；(2)混淆瞬时预测与持久索引准入；(3)缺乏对事实错误或视觉不支持数值的显式风险控制，可能导致索引污染。

Method: 提出反事实视觉证据引导的选择性属性索引(CVE-SAI)框架：首先从主图像和属性问题推断并冻结本体约束候选值(不依赖目录文本)；引入焦点区域失真(FZD)构建属性特定的视觉依赖代理，使用证据引导注意力重分配(EGAR)优化评分；在候选冻结后执行证据必要性、证据保留、干扰变换稳定性和目录文本冲突审计；目录文本仅用于收紧准入决策而非修改候选值；采用独立家族级校准，在5%不安全准入预算下选择策略。

Result: 在Amazon Berkeley Objects的五个视觉属性上验证，CVE-SAI在属性推理和证据定位方面表现优异，在共享风险协议下达到最高的认证准入覆盖率，在自动准入系统中实现最强受控检索性能和最低的不安全自动诱导曝光。

Conclusion: 将推理与准入决策解耦，能够使视觉支持的属性补全在改善检索效果的同时限制持久索引污染，为电商场景下的可靠多模态属性索引提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Multimodal product models can complete missing e-commerce attributes, yet current methods still optimize attribute-answer accuracy without verifying visual support, conflate transient prediction with persistent index admission, and lack explicit risk control over factually incorrect or visually unsupported values. We address these gaps with Counterfactual Visual Evidence-Guided Selective Attribute Indexing (CVE-SAI), which first infers and freezes an ontology-constrained candidate from the primary image and attribute question without catalog text, and then decides whether that candidate should enter the index. Focus-Zone Distortion (FZD) constructs an attribute-specific visual-dependence proxy through a controlled counterfactual intervention, and Evidence-Guided Attention Redistribution (EGAR) uses the proxy to refine ontology-constrained scoring. The canonical candidate is frozen before evidence necessity, evidence retention, nuisance-transformation stability, and candidate-specific catalog-text conflict audits; catalog text can only tighten admission and cannot revise the candidate. Independent family-level calibration selects one policy with a simultaneous one-sided finite-sample bound under a 5% unsafe-admission budget. Experiments on five visual attributes derived from Amazon Berkeley Objects show that CVE-SAI improves attribute inference and evidence localization, achieves the highest certified admission coverage under the shared risk protocol, and yields the strongest controlled retrieval performance with the lowest unsafe auto-induced exposure among automatic-admission systems. Separating inference from admission therefore enables visually supported attribute completion to improve retrieval while limiting persistent index contamination.

</details>


### [129] [Retrieve, Match, Escalate: Accurate and Scalable Product Linking with VLM-Distilled Cross-Encoders and Agentic VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25037)
*Jian Wang,Steven Xu,Sanjyot Thete,Maryam Barouti,Tom Tang,Elaine Wu,Charu Sareen,Kyle MacDonald*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种用于大规模电商产品链接的检索-匹配级联系统，通过轻量级文本模型和代理式多模态VLM分阶段处理，以98%的精度自动链接68%的产品，总覆盖率达到77%，同时显著降低了计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在电商平台规模下，需要将数十亿条噪声高、多类目的商家产品记录映射到数千万条标准目录产品。传统单模型方法面临两难：要么对困难案例精度不足，要么对简单案例计算成本过高。因此需要一种能根据案例难度动态分配计算资源的解决方案。

Method: 提出了检索-匹配级联架构：1) 检索阶段筛选候选匹配；2) 轻量级文本交叉编码器自动解决高置信度的大多数简单案例（从数百万双VLM共识标签中蒸馏训练，无需人工标注，在98%精度标准下自动接受）；3) 代理式多模态视觉语言模型（VLM）处理模糊案例，通过检查产品图像并执行网络搜索来收集证据。

Result: 交叉编码器以98%精度自动链接68%的产品。开源自托管代理模型以约七分之一的成本达到了封闭前沿VLM 92%召回率中的88%（仅损失4个点）。将困难案例升级到代理处理后，端到端链接覆盖率从68%提升至77%，而每对计算成本跨越近五个数量级。

Conclusion: 该级联系统成功实现了计算资源与案例难度的动态匹配，在保持高精度的同时显著降低了总体成本，验证了开源模型在工业级产品链接任务中的可行性和成本效益，为大规模电商实体消解提供了实用的生产级解决方案。

Abstract: Product linking, the entity-resolution task of mapping merchant product records to canonical catalog products, consolidates fragmented listings so downstream search, recommendation, and advertising see one clean entry per product. At marketplace scale, billions of noisy, multi-category records must be resolved against tens of millions of canonical products, where scoring every candidate with a single model is either too weak for the hard cases or too costly for the easy ones. We present a production retrieve-then-match cascade that spends computation in proportion to difficulty: retrieval surfaces plausible matches, a lightweight text cross-encoder auto-resolves the high-confidence majority, and an agentic multimodal vision-language model settles the ambiguous remainder by inspecting product images and issuing web searches for evidence that is in neither record. The cross-encoder is distilled from millions of dual-VLM-consensus labels, retiring human annotation from the training set, and is calibrated to auto-accept links at a 98% precision bar validated against a smaller operator-certified audit. The agent is a self-hosted open-weight model that reaches a closed frontier VLM's precision at a four-point recall cost (88% versus 92%) for roughly one-seventh the per-pair cost, with no fine-tuning. Per-pair cost spans nearly five orders of magnitude from the cheap cross-encoder to the frontier VLM, so escalating only the hard tail to the agent raises end-to-end link coverage from the cheap stage's 68% to 77%.

</details>


### [130] [LifePlanner: Evaluating LLM Agents for Geo-spatial Planning with Social Media Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25039)
*Zhen Dong,Yuning Peng,Yutao Shi,Lei Zhong,Yongsen Mao,Yuan Liu,Haiping Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了LifePlanner基准测试，通过结合地图数据和社交媒体信号来评估LLM在地理空间规划任务中的表现，发现前沿模型在复杂规划任务中表现显著下降，主要归因于证据获取不完整、工具使用不精确和约束整合能力不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的地理空间规划基准测试仅提供干净的数据和工具，缺乏人们在日常规划中使用的开放式社交信号。真实的地理空间规划（如行程设计）需要基于现实的工具使用、嘈杂的证据检索和多约束推理，因此需要一个更贴近现实的测试基准。

Method: 引入LifePlanner基准，将大规模本地社交媒体数据与地图数据相结合，通过MCP工具集提供访问接口。该基准包含四个任务类别和三个难度级别的评估套件，用于全面测试LLM的地理空间规划能力。

Result: 实验表明前沿LLM在简单检索任务上表现良好，但在复杂规划任务上性能急剧下降，通过率降至40.2%。失败主要源于从大型多模态数据库中证据获取不完整、工具使用不精确和约束整合能力弱，而非模型规模或推理长度的问题。

Conclusion: 未来进展需要有效的基于现实的规划能力而非单纯的模型扩展。结果表明当前LLM在处理真实世界复杂规划任务时面临证据获取、工具使用和约束整合三大核心挑战，为后续研究指明了方向。

Abstract: Geo-spatial planning, like trip design, is a realistic testbed for LLM agents because it requires grounded tool use, noisy evidence retrieval, and multi-constraint reasoning. Most benchmarks, however, only provide clean geospatial data and tools, missing the open-ended social signals that people use in daily planning. We introduce LifePlanner, a benchmark that enriches map data with large-scale local social media posts and provides access through an MCP toolset. LifePlanner provides an evaluation suite spanning four task categories and three difficulty levels. Experiments show frontier LLMs perform well on simple retrieval but degrade sharply on complex planning, with the Pass Rate dropping to 40.2%. Results show that failures mainly stem from incomplete evidence acquisition from such a large multimodal database, imprecise tool use, and weak constraint integration rather than model size or reasoning length, suggesting that future progress requires effective grounded planning instead of scaling alone.

</details>


### [131] [SimVerity: When Does Simulated Agent Success Survive Physical Deployment?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25067)
*Zhonghao Zhan,Yefan Zhang,Krinos Li,Hamed Haddadi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了SimVerity框架，通过在真实智能家居环境中重放仿真场景并交叉验证，系统量化了仿真通过提供的物理部署证据；发现仿真与实际部署存在显著差异，仿真通过不代表物理部署成功。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仿真评估被广泛用于基准测试AI代理，但仿真通过提供的物理部署证据尚未被系统量化；需要建立仿真结果与实际部署之间的可信验证机制。

Method: 提出SimVerity裁决转移保障框架：在目标智能家居部署中重放匹配场景，并通过独立的物理验证器对代理执行进行交叉验证；采用风险画像预测方法，并测量代理的可审计性。

Result: 高级仿真器通过所有240次灯光试验，但摄像机捕捉到42次设定状态检查无法发现的亚秒级故障；风险画像预测成功预测未测量路径的故障，在两个队列的11个保留会话中均优于基准；改变代理配置可将场景匹配率从52-88%提升至100%；第二个仿真器无独立交叉检查作用。

Conclusion: 部署成功是真实世界过程而非仿真中的静态属性；仅物理测量能暴露仿真器的共同盲点；SimVerity将裁决转移转化为明确决策：部署前清除、弃权或升级。

Abstract: Simulated evaluation is widely used to benchmark AI agents, yet how much evidence a simulated pass provides about physical deployment has not been systematically quantified. We present SimVerity, a verdict-transfer assurance framework: it replays matched scenarios on target smart home deployments and cross-validates agent execution against independently qualified physical witnesses. Our evaluation highlights that deployment success is a real-world process, not a static property in simulation: completion, reported state, observable effect, and settled outcome diverged within the same execution. Although an advanced simulator cleared all 240 light trials, a camera caught 42 sub-second failures invisible to settled-state checks. False clearance was predictable: a risk profile learned from measured trials and locked before evaluation predicted failures on a path it never physically measured, beating a property-blind baseline in all eleven held-out sessions across two cohorts. Agent auditability was also measurable: switching one agent loop's model-client/serving configuration raised its scenario-matching share from 52-88% to 100%. Finally, a second qualified simulator added no independent cross-check: it never disagreed on any overlapping case, and only physical measurement exposed their shared blind spots. SimVerity turns verdict transfer into an explicit decision: clear, abstain, or escalate before deployment.

</details>


### [132] [Auto-Policy, not Auto-Skill: Compiled Agent Skills for the Physical World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25091)
*Zhonghao Zhan,Hamed Haddadi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示了一种名为"Borrowed Authority"的新型安全漏洞，即恶意代理技能可绕过权限验证导致物理伤害，并提出Edge Skillguard解决方案，在技能工件内部嵌入类型化权限层，成功拦截所有攻击请求而不影响正常操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自进化技能系统（如AutoSkills、Hermes Agent）仅关注效率提升而忽视安全性，存在关键缺陷：技能描述代理应如何行为，但策略决定哪些行为被允许执行，当前格式用markdown和脚本覆盖前者，却将后者留给模型判断。随着技能生成规模扩大，安全缺口也在扩大，特别是在错误调用可能导致开门或转账等高风险场景中。已有研究记录了恶意技能攻击云软件和越狱LLM控制机器人造成物理伤害，但两者交集（恶意代理技能造成物理伤害）尚未被报告。

Method: 提出Edge Skillguard，一种类型化权限层，不同于传统工作流引擎在工具间设置防护，而是将防护机制嵌入技能工件内部，包含世界状态和传感器证据的守护机制。该系统在实时边缘控制平面测试床上进行验证，覆盖五种攻击变体，并在Tailscale网格跨主机环境中测试了5倍规模的扩展性。

Result: 在实时边缘控制平面测试床上，防护机制成功拦截了五种攻击变体的全部60个借权请求（60/60），且未阻止任何良性请求。该结果在5倍规模和跨主机的Tailscale网格环境中保持稳定。

Conclusion: 高风险技能应将类型化调用策略与过程知识共同封装，使物理动作依赖于机器可验证的证据而非对等代理声明，从而有效防范借权攻击。

Abstract: Self-evolving Skill harnesses (AutoSkills, Hermes Agent) generate more advisory orchestration automatically; their reported gains are efficiency, not safety. This misses the actual gap: a Skill describes how an agent should behave; a Policy decides which behavior is allowed to become an action. Today's format covers the first with markdown and scripts; the second is left to the model. Generating more Skills scales the gap, not the safety, especially when a wrong invocation can unlock a door or move money. Two adjacent attacks are documented: malicious skills compromising cloud software, and jailbroken LLM-controlled robots causing physical harm. Their intersection, malicious agent skills causing physical harm, follows directly but has not been reported. We name this class Borrowed Authority: Skills format gives the receiving agent no typed way to reject an inter-agent permission claim, so a malicious or misused Skill can drive actuation by attaching one. We propose Edge Skillguard, a typed authority layer that lives inside the Skill artifact rather than between tools as workflow engines do, with guards over world state and sensor evidence. On a live edge control-plane testbed, the guards reject 60/60 borrowed-authority requests across five attack variants without blocking benign requests, and the result holds at 5x scale and across hosts over a Tailscale mesh. These results suggest that high-risk Skills should co-package typed invocation policy with procedural knowledge, so that physical actions depend on machine-checkable evidence rather than peer-agent claims.

</details>


### [133] [PhysElite: How Far Are LLMs from Solving Olympiad-Level Physics Problems?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25097)
*Ruoran Xu,Wending Gao,Liyunfeng Chen,Aixin Shi,Haoyu Cheng,Zixiang Fang,Yiqiang Zou,Qiufeng Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了PhysElite，一个大规模双语多模态物理奥林匹克级推理基准，包含11,586道题目，旨在解决现有物理基准难度不足和覆盖不全的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物理基准存在两大局限：(1)缺乏高难度数据集，(2)在视觉形式、知识点和分步解题过程的覆盖不全面，导致模型在当前数据集上的表现不能完全代表其解决复杂物理问题的能力。

Method: 构建PhysElite基准数据集，包含11,586道奥林匹克级别物理题目，每题配备相应的视觉图表、中英双语分步解答推导和最终答案。对18个开源和闭源多模态大语言模型进行基准测试，并进行了步骤级别的过程评估以诊断模型在推理链中的失败环节。

Result: 对18个模型进行基准测试，发现即使是表现最好的模型也仅达到33.7%的答案准确率，步骤级评估揭示了模型在推理链中的失败位置。

Conclusion: 当前多模态大语言模型在专家级物理推理任务上仍有很大提升空间，PhysElite为评估和改进模型的物理推理能力提供了有价值的工具，数据集已公开发布。

Abstract: Understanding how (multimodal) large language models perform on physics problems requires benchmarks that reflect the difficulty and breadth of expert-level physical reasoning. Existing physics benchmarks remain limited in the following two important ways: (1) short of high-difficulty datasets, and (2) lack of comprehensive coverage of visual forms, knowledge points, and step-by-step solution processes. As a result, model performance on current datasets may not be fully representative of their ability to solve complex physics problems. To address these issues, we present PhysElite, a large-scale bilingual multimodal benchmark for Olympiad-level physics reasoning. PhysElite contains 11,586 Olympiad-tier problems. For each problem, we provide corresponding visual diagrams, step-by-step bilingual Chinese-English solution derivations, and the final answer. We benchmark 18 open-source and closed-source MLLMs, and find that even the strongest model reaches only 33.7% answer accuracy. We additionally conduct step-level process evaluation to diagnose where models fail in the reasoning chain. Our datasets are released at https://huggingface.co/datasets/physelite/PhysElite.

</details>


### [134] [Towards Reliable, Generalizable, and Specific In-Context Knowledge Editing via Multi-Objective Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25100)
*Xuzhong Wang,Maiqi Jiang,Tejal Nair,Girija Bhusal,Yanfu Zhang,Haipeng Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MO-IKE方法，通过多目标强化学习优化上下文知识编辑的提示构建，在可靠性、泛化性和特异性三个目标间实现更好平衡，在Llama-3.2上显著提升了编辑性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有上下文知识编辑方法将提示构建视为单一目标优化问题，仅在部分过程中做决策，忽略了可靠性、泛化性和特异性之间的竞争关系以及提示的全局组织，导致编辑效果不够均衡和一致。

Method: 提出多目标上下文知识编辑(MO-IKE)算法，将提示构建建模为约束马尔可夫决策过程，训练动态检索器以优化多个竞争目标，实现更加平衡和全局一致的提示构建。

Result: 在Llama-3.2模型上，MO-IKE将编辑成功率(可靠性)从85.0%提升至92.0%，转述一致性(泛化性)从77%提升至79%，同时相比先前的RL方法将保留率(特异性)提高23.0%。

Conclusion: MO-IKE通过多目标强化学习有效解决了知识编辑中多目标优化问题，实现了可靠性、泛化性和特异性的更好平衡，显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are powerful but limited by static parametric knowledge that becomes outdated once pretraining ends. Knowledge editing addresses this problem by updating model behavior on target facts without full retraining. In particular, in-context knowledge editing has gained attention because it is training-free and readily applicable to black-box LLMs. Recent reinforcement learning (RL)-based approaches improve over fixed retrieval strategies by adapting prompt construction to the quantity-quality trade-off. Despite initial success, they fail to model the prompt as a structured entity under the distinct and often competing objectives of reliability, generality, and specificity. Previous methods largely optimize a single objective and make decisions over only part of the prompt construction process, thereby overlooking both the balance of different objectives and the global organization of demonstrations. We propose Multi-Objective In-context Knowledge Editing (MO-IKE), a multi-objective RL algorithm that formulates prompt construction for in-context knowledge editing as a Constrained Markov Decision Process. MO-IKE trains a dynamic retriever to optimize competing objectives in knowledge editing, enabling more balanced and globally coherent prompt construction. On Llama-3.2, MO-IKE improves edit success (reliability) from 85.0% to 92.0%, paraphrase consistency (generality) from 77% to 79%, while increasing retention rate (specificity) by 23.0% compared to prior RL-based methods.

</details>


### [135] [FuzzingBrain-Bench V1: Evaluating Open-Ended Bug Discovery by LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25158)
*Ze Sheng,Aleksandar Kezic,Zhicheng Chen,Jeff Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了FuzzingBrain-Bench基准测试，用于评估AI模型发现开源软件漏洞的能力。该基准包含77个挑战，评估了三个Claude模型，其中Claude Opus 4.8表现最佳，在60个挑战中触发崩溃，得分196/579。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试通过要求模型生成触发预定义目标漏洞的概念验证输入来评估LLM发现软件漏洞的能力。然而，这种设置会忽略模型发现的未匹配预定义目标的有效崩溃，导致评估结果无法反映模型的真实能力。

Method: 构建了FuzzingBrain-Bench基准测试：将开源项目和经过sanitizer插桩的harness封装在独立的Docker镜像中；模型的目标是生成能够通过harness触发尽可能多不同崩溃的输入；性能评分基于产生的不同崩溃签名数量，设置上限并乘以难度系数。V1版本包含77个挑战，涵盖43个开源项目，包括36个C、32个C++和9个Java/JVM挑战。

Result: 在完整基准上评估了Claude Haiku 4.5、Claude Sonnet 4.6和Claude Opus 4.8。Claude Opus 4.8表现最佳，在77个挑战中的60个触发崩溃，得分196/579。三个模型均未在13个挑战中触发任何崩溃。

Conclusion: FuzzingBrain-Bench提供了一个更全面、更真实的评估框架，能够准确衡量AI模型发现软件漏洞的实际能力。基准语料库和harness已公开发布，支持后续研究和模型改进。

Abstract: Evaluating the ability of large language models (LLMs) to discover software bugs is increasingly important. Existing benchmarks typically evaluate this capability by asking the model to generate a proof-of-concept input that triggers a predefined target vulnerability. However, this setup may overlook valid crashes discovered by the model when they do not match the predefined target. As a result, the evaluation may not reflect the model's real capability.
  We present FuzzingBrain-Bench, a benchmark for assessing AI models' ability to discover bugs in open-source software. Models are given an open-source project and a sanitizer-instrumented harness in a self-contained Docker image. Their goal is to generate inputs that trigger as many distinct crashes as possible through the harness.
