BVR Sim supports multiple JSBSim aircraft models, including the F-15, F-16, F/A-18, and F-22, with configurable weapons, sensors, controllers, and opponents. A unified tactical action interface specifies desired heading, altitude, speed, and weapon release above aircraft-specific inner-loop controllers, enabling policies to operate across heterogeneous platforms. The environment provides interchangeable Python and accelerated C++ backends, entity-oriented observations, compositional rewards, scripted opponents, replay and visualization, and adapters for multi-agent learning frameworks. At a 0.4-s decision interval, the C++ backend achieves 104 simulated seconds per wall-clock second in 1-vs-1 and remains practical through 10-vs-10 scenarios. A policy trained only on the F-16 transfers without retraining to four unseen aircraft, reaching a 45.5% mean win rate with aircraft-specific controller adaptation. MAPPO and HAPPO experiments further verify end-to-end compatibility with standard multi-agent reinforcement-learning pipelines.

</details>


### [170] [Trust-Aware Sequential Decision Making and Rollout Planning for Resilient Multi-Robot Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25690)
*Roee M. Francos,Daniel Garces,Orhan Eren Akgün,Nathaniel D. Bastian,Stephanie Gil*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 针对多机器人路由中的定位欺骗攻击，提出了分层二分匹配攻击策略和信任感知监控方法，有效检测 adversaries 并恢复决策性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有顺序决策方法假设规划信息可靠且智能体按预期执行动作，但被攻破的智能体会违反这两个假设，导致规划模型与物理执行不匹配，威胁多机器人系统安全。

Method: 1) 提出距离约束欺骗模型和分层二分匹配策略最大化攻击影响；2) 开发信任感知监控器，结合基于真实GPS欺骗数据校准的概率定位信任与任务执行行为证据进行智能体分类并移除检测到的 adversaries。

Result: 使用真实GPS欺骗数据集和旧金山出租车需求数据的实验表明，该方法在各种欺骗能力、对抗舰队规模、自适应攻击、监控配置和rollout horizon下均能有效检测攻击并实现弹性路由，恢复了rollout相比基础策略的经验优势。

Conclusion: 信任感知移除策略在检测后恢复了规划器-执行一致性，使rollout重新获得预期的成本改进行为，证明了该方法对多机器人系统中定位欺骗攻击的有效防御能力。

Abstract: Sequential decision-making in multi-robot systems typically assumes that planning information is reliable and that agents execute the actions anticipated by the planner. Compromised agents can violate both assumptions, creating a mismatch between the planning model and physical execution. We study this problem in online multi-robot routing under localization spoofing. We introduce a distance-constrained spoofing model for monitor-aware adversaries, together with a tiered bipartite matching strategy that maximizes assignment influence while limiting spoofing magnitude. To mitigate such attacks, we develop a trust-aware monitor that combines probabilistic localization trust, calibrated using real GPS spoofing data, with behavioral evidence from task execution to classify agents and remove detected adversaries from subsequent planning. We further show that undetected adversaries can cause rollout to lose its expected cost-improvement behavior by violating planner-execution consistency. Trust-aware removal restores this consistency after detection, enabling stable routing and recovery of rollout's empirical advantage over the base policy. Experiments using real GPS spoofing datasets and San Francisco taxicab demand demonstrate effective detection and resilient routing across varying spoofing capabilities, adversarial fleet sizes, adaptive attacks, monitoring configurations, and rollout horizons.

</details>


### [171] [HypoForge: A Self-Improving Multi-Agent Framework for Automated Hypothesis Generation and Testing via Scientific Skill Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25770)
*Ziqing Qian,Jiaying Lei,Yifang Wang,Nan Cao*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: HypoForge是一个经验引导的多智能体框架，通过针对假设生成和假设检验两个阶段设计不同的技能学习策略，实现了AI科学家系统的持续改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的AI科学家系统大多依赖静态提示或固定工作流程，无法积累经验以实现持续改进。作者观察到假设生成和假设检验两个阶段涉及不同的监督信号，需要针对性的学习方法。

Method: 提出HypoForge框架，采用阶段特定的技能学习策略：1）假设生成阶段采用对抗性生成器-判别器机制，通过比较性批评改进推理；2）假设检验阶段从执行结果和真实结果中学习测试技能。两种策略与各自的监督信号相匹配，无需微调基础模型即可实现持续改进。

Result: 在假设生成和假设检验基准测试中，HypoForge持续优于现有AI科学家框架和技能级别变体。进一步分析证明了所提出的阶段特定技能学习范式的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过将技能学习策略与阶段特定监督信号相匹配，HypoForge成功实现了AI科学家系统的持续改进能力，为自动化科学发现提供了新的研究方向。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have enabled AI scientist systems to automate scientific discovery, yet existing approaches most rely on static prompting or fixed workflows and fail to accumulate experience for continual improvement. We propose HypoForge, an experience-guided multi-agent framework that learns reusable scientific skills for automated hypothesis generation and hypothesis testing. HypoForge is built on the observation that these two stages involve different supervision signals. For hypothesis generation, where explicit feedback is unavailable, HypoForge adopts an adversarial generator--discriminator mechanism to improve reasoning through comparative critique. For hypothesis testing, where empirical feedback is available, HypoForge learns testing skills from execution outcomes and ground-truth results. By matching skill learning strategies with stage-specific supervision, HypoForge enables continual improvement without fine-tuning foundation models. Experiments on hypothesis generation and testing benchmarks show that HypoForge consistently outperforms existing AI scientist frameworks and skill-level variants. Further analysis demonstrates the effectiveness of the proposed stage-specific skill learning paradigms.

</details>


### [172] [AI Agentic Selective Laser Sintering Process Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25928)
*Peter Pak,Victor Alvarado,Amir Barati Farimani*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本研究开发了一种基于AI的智能代理系统，用于选择性激光烧结(SLS)工艺参数优化，仅需少量迭代即可使PA12 GF、PA11 Onyx和PA12 Blend三种材料的拉伸和弯曲性能达到技术规格要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 增材制造中的工艺参数优化是一项复杂任务，传统方法需要大量人工干预和试错。为了实现复杂工作流程的智能自动化，特别是在选择性激光烧结中优化机械性能，需要开发能够自主学习并迭代优化的智能代理系统。

Method: 构建AI驱动的智能代理系统，应用于Inova Mk1设备上的选择性激光烧结工艺。系统利用历史构建数据作为知识基础，在最小用户指导下迭代优化三种材料（PA12 GF、PA11 Onyx和PA12 Blend）的工艺参数，以提升拉伸和弯曲性能。

Result: 智能代理系统在少量迭代次数内成功优化了工艺参数，使三种材料的机械性能达到与TDS（技术数据表）规定值相当的水平。系统展现出从更新数据中持续学习的能力，验证了智能自动化在SLS工艺参数优化中的有效性。

Conclusion: 智能代理系统能够实现复杂任务（如SLS工艺参数优化）的智能自动化，通过持续学习和迭代优化机制，为增材制造领域提供了一种高效的工艺优化解决方案。

Abstract: Agentic systems enable the intelligent automation of complex workflows, specific to additive manufacturing this is applicable for complex tasks such as process parameter optimization for mechanical properties. This work investigates the AI enabled agentic process optimization within Selective Laser Sintering (SLS) to iteratively improve the tensile and flexural properties of 3 different materials on the Inova Mk1. These materials include PA12 GF, PA11 Onyx, and PA12 Blend (volume mixture of 25% PA12 GF and 75% PA12 White) and with using knowledge from previous builds and minimal guidance from the user, the agentic system was able to optimize process parameters over a small number of iterations to achieve comparable TDS specified mechanical properties. This work showcases the ability for an agentic system to continually learn from updated data, enabling the intelligent automation of complex tasks such as process parameter optimization for selective laser sintering.

</details>


### [173] [Praxist: From Experimental Artifacts to Solution Lineages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25955)
*Jin Li,Ahmed Murtadha,Zhiyu Wang,Qiwen Chen,William Chen,Yifei Wu,Guan Wang,Andy L. Siy,Jiayi Yang,Mengsha Huang,Wenhao Li,Yixuan Liu,Shuailin Pan,Mingli Yuan,Sen Song,Yuhao Sun*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了Praxist，一种以血缘为核心的代际研发系统，通过构建可追溯的证据图谱，使AI研发代理能够继承经过验证的机制，在MLE-bench基准测试中以约1/12的成本达到80%获奖率（49枚金牌），解决了现有系统无法追踪改进原因和重复学习的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自主研发代理主要作为实验室工具存在，存在三个关键缺陷：在精选基准上演示、难以追踪改进原因、成本远超可持续工程实践。结构性问题在于大多数系统将每次尝试视为近乎独立的事件，日志和搜索树记录了发生的事件，但未建立哪个设计元素产生了改进、其证据是否通过验证，或如何与其他元素重组。因此长期运行的活动会反复学习相同的教训。

Method: 提出Praxist系统，采用以血缘为中心的代际方法。将可复现的工件和评估器结果转换为类型化的证据图谱，包含发现、车道结构前沿和议程。分离本地工件构建与队列级证据合成，使后续尝试能够继承经过验证的机制、未解决的声明和有用的约束，并将结果附加到可审查的血缘关系上。

Result: 在标准化的75任务MLE-bench套件上，Praxist获得60枚奖牌（80.0%），其中49枚金牌，而Claude Code基线在Claude Opus 4.8上获得55枚奖牌（73.3%）和34枚金牌。模型支出为3054美元对比38370美元，约为基线的1/12。在四个案例研究（量化交易、LiDAR-惯性-视觉SLAM、托卡马克磁控制、火箭着陆）中，均改善了任务原生基线的主要精度、存活率或资源成本。

Conclusion: 本研究首次将更强的人工制品、低一个数量级的成本和可审计的血缘关系三者结合在一起，达到了生产性研究所需的操作配置文件，而不仅仅是基准演示建立的操作配置文件。该方法为可持续的AI辅助研发提供了可行的路径。

Abstract: Autonomous R\&D agents now write, run, and improve executable artifacts under automated evaluation---but largely as laboratory instruments: shown on curated benchmarks, with gains that are hard to trace to a cause and costs well above what sustained engineering practice absorbs. The limitation is structural. Most systems treat each attempt as nearly self-contained, so logs, memories, and search trees record what happened without establishing which design element produced an improvement, whether its evidence survived validation, or how it recombines with others. Long campaigns therefore keep re-learning the same lessons. We introduce Praxist, a lineage-centered generational system that converts reproducible artifacts and evaluator outcomes into a typed evidence graph of findings, lane-structured frontiers, and agendas. Separating local artifact construction from cohort-level evidence synthesis lets later attempts inherit validated mechanisms, unresolved claims, and useful constraints, and leaves results attached to an inspectable lineage. On the standardized 75-task MLE-bench suite, the finalized official-grader results give Praxist 60 medals (80.0\%), 49 of them gold, against 55 medals (73.3\%) and 34 gold for a Claude Code baseline on Claude Opus 4.8---at a recorded model spend of US\$3,054 versus US\$38,370, roughly a twelfth of the cost. Four case studies---quantitative trading, LiDAR-inertial-visual SLAM, tokamak magnetic control, and rocket landing---carry the same process into open-ended engineering problems, improving on each task-native baseline in headline accuracy, survival, or resource cost, with the discovery path on record. Stronger artifacts at an order of magnitude less spend, each backed by an auditable lineage, are, to our knowledge, first brought together here: the operating profile production research requires, not the one a benchmark demonstration establishes.

</details>


<div id='cs.NI'></div>

# cs.NI [[Back]](#toc)

### [174] [Rethinking Battery-free Sensing Communication via Wake-up Radios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25292)
*Gaosheng Liu,Kasim Sinan Yildirim,Sajal K. Das,Yingxin Song*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: MagPie是一种面向无电池传感器的通信协议，通过结合微安级唤醒无线电和独立供电的低功耗实时时钟，解决了间歇性供电环境下的时序状态丢失和通信窗口受限问题，实现了无需控制锚点的静态单跳数据收集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无电池传感器面临两大挑战：通信窗口短暂且随机，以及主能源域断电时丢失时序状态。现有方法无法在缺乏持续供电控制节点的情况下保证可靠的首次接触和后续通信。

Method: MagPie将微安级唤醒无线电（WuR）与独立备用电源的低功耗实时时钟（LP-RTC）结合：WuR扩展首次接触窗口，LP-RTC保存已获取的相位供后续交换使用。采用能量门控、纪元版本调度和幂等时隙分配机制，实现无需供电控制锚点的静态单跳All-to-One数据收集。方法还考虑了角色选择、周期性监听，并在显式条件分位数覆盖下界定调度重试尾部。

Result: 在五种能量采集场景的仿真试验中，MagPie在100/100次首次会合事件中全部成功，而对比方法Find仅完成36-100/100。单碰撞域分槽Aloha研究表明，高竞争密度下需要自适应K值，并报告了120个组件的均值、P95和置信区间。STM32WL33实验验证了对齐、时钟持久性和六设备时隙执行，包括11.05小时的功能运行。

Conclusion: MagPie在无电池传感器环境中实现了可靠的时序状态保持和首次接触，无需依赖持续供电的控制节点。然而，研究未宣称测量端到端能耗、环境采集性能或多跳可扩展性；高度可变的能量采集仍限制数据收集能力，因为协调无法创造缺失的能量。

Abstract: Battery-free sensors expose short, stochastic communication windows and may lose timing state whenever their main energy domain browns out. MagPie combines a microampere wake-up radio (WuR) with a separately backed low-power real-time clock (LP-RTC): the WuR widens the first-contact window, while the LP-RTC preserves the acquired phase for later exchanges. Energy gates, epoch-versioned schedules, and idempotent slot allocation extend this mechanism to static, single-hop All-to-One collection without a powered control anchor. The analysis accounts for role selection and duty-cycled listening, and bounds scheduled-retry tails only under explicit conditional quantile coverage. Evaluation separates three scopes. In independent, administratively censored simulation trials, MagPie completes 100/100 first rendezvous events in each of five trace-parameterized harvesting scenarios; Find completes 36-100/100. A single-collision-domain slotted-Aloha study shows that adaptive K is necessary at high contender density and reports mean, P95, and confidence intervals through 120 components. Finally, controlled STM32WL33 experiments validate alignment, clock persistence, and six-device slot execution, including an 11.05-hour functional run. The study does not claim measured end-to-end energy, ambient-harvesting performance, or multi-hop scalability; highly variable harvesting still limits collection because coordination cannot create missing energy.

</details>


### [175] [Designing Core Layer in Campus Network Using Software-Defined Networking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25373)
*Iwan Setiawan,Azis Wisnu Widhi Nugraha,Sauqi Asrorul Zaza*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文基于软件定义网络（SDN）范式，利用RouteFlow平台和OSPF路由协议，设计并验证了大学校园网核心层的高可用性网络拓扑与路由方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 校园网核心层（骨干网络）需为内外部网络提供互联与路由服务，设计高可用性是该层的首要任务。传统网络架构在灵活性与可扩展性方面存在局限，引入SDN范式有望提升核心层的设计效率与运维能力。

Method: 采用RouteFlow作为SDN平台，在OpenFlow基础设施上实现OSPF传统路由协议。实验环境包含两台虚拟机：第一台作为SDN/OF数据平面，通过Mininet仿真器部署Open vSwitch；第二台作为SDN控制平面，集成RouteFlow与POX控制器。使用ping测试互联性，通过tcpdump监控各交换机网络接口流量以追踪数据包路径，验证OSPF是否实现了最短路径路由。

Result: 在虚拟化SDN测试平台上成功搭建了校园网核心层原型，并通过互联性测试与流量监控，验证了数据包能够按照OSPF计算的最短路径进行路由。

Conclusion: 本研究表明，在SDN环境中集成传统路由协议（如OSPF）是可行的，能够为校园网核心层的设计与部署提供一种灵活、可验证的解决方案。

Abstract: As one of the layers in campus network, core layer or backbone network should provide interconnectivity and routing between internal and external networks, including other campus networks or the Internet. With that requirement, designing this layer to be highly available to interconnect and route traffic is a high priority. By using software-defined networking (SDN) paradigm, we attempt to design core layer in campus network employing best practices of network topology and routing protocol at the layer with a use case of core layer in a university campus network. We use RouteFlow as the SDN platform supporting a traditional routing protocol, i.e. Open Shortest Path First (OSPF), over OpenFlow (OF) network infrastructure. The experimental testbed/environment consists of two virtual machines (VMs). The first VM is used as the SDN/OF data plane with Open vSwitch in Mininet network emulator, and the second one representing SDN control plane comprising RouteFlow with POX controller. We evaluated the design by testing the interconnectivity using ping for the OF switches in the topology and hosts that are connected to the switches. We also tracked the route of the packets by monitoring traffic passed through all network interfaces of the switches using tcpdump. This case was evaluated since we need to make sure that packets were routed with the shortest path from source to destination using OSPF that was implemented in the virtual network at the top of the SDN platform.

</details>


### [176] [Traffic-Adaptive Per-Hop Multipath Routing in Multi-Hop UAV Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25383)
*Zhenyu Zhao,Tiankui Zhang,Xiaoxia Xu,Yuanpeng Zheng,Junjie Li,Wenjuan Xing*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向多跳无人机网络的流量自适应逐跳多路径路由方法MAPPO-DM，通过多智能体强化学习动态分配流量，有效提升了时延敏感任务的按时投递率并降低了丢包率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在无人机中继移动边缘计算网络中，计算任务产生的流量具有多样的时延要求和数据大小。传统多路径路由通常在预定义的端到端路径上分割流量，难以快速响应无人机网络中的链路波动和拓扑变化，无法很好地处理异构流量。

Method: 提出流量自适应逐跳多路径路由方法，每个无人机动态地在多个候选下一跳之间分配流量。将路由问题建模为分散式部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程（Dec-POMDP），开发了基于集中训练-分散执行框架的多智能体强化学习算法MAPPO-DM，使用Dirichlet分布建模连续的流量分割动作。

Result: 仿真结果表明，MAPPO-DM在提升数据包按时投递率的同时降低了丢包率，在多种网络条件下均优于基准方法，并保持鲁棒的性能表现。

Conclusion: 所提出的逐跳多路径路由方法相比传统端到端多路径路由更能适应无人机网络的动态特性，MAPPO-DM算法能够有效处理异构流量并应对网络条件变化，为无人机中继MEC网络提供了可靠的路由解决方案。

Abstract: In uncrewed aerial vehicle (UAV)-relayed mobile edge computing (MEC) networks, computation tasks generate traffic with diverse latency requirements and data sizes. Routing decisions therefore need to adapt to both traffic characteristics and changing network conditions. Compared with single-path routing, multipath routing is better suited to such heterogeneous traffic because it provides multiple forwarding options and enables flexible traffic splitting. However, conventional multipath routing usually splits traffic over predefined end-to-end paths, making it difficult to respond quickly to link fluctuations and topology changes in UAV networks. To address this issue, we propose a traffic-adaptive per-hop multipath routing method for multi-hop UAV networks, in which each UAV dynamically distributes traffic among multiple candidate next hops. We formulate the routing problem to improve the on-time packet delivery ratio while reducing the packet loss ratio, and model it as a decentralized partially observable Markov decision process (Dec-POMDP). To solve this problem, we develop a multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) algorithm, termed Multi-Agent Proximal Policy Optimization with Dirichlet Modeling (MAPPO-DM). MAPPO-DM follows the centralized-training-and-decentralized-execution framework and models continuous traffic-splitting actions using a Dirichlet distribution. Simulation results show that MAPPO-DM outperforms the baseline methods and maintains robust performance under various network conditions.

</details>


### [177] [SPFR: Semantic Potential Field Routing for the Distributed Internet of Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25396)
*Yeguang Qin,Liangqi Peng,Fengxiao Tang,Ming Zhao*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了语义势场路由算法（SPFR），一种针对去中心化智能体物联网（IoA）的分布式路由方法。通过将执行器发现与重选集成到逐跳转发过程中，SPFR解决了传统"先发现后转发"方法无法支持下游重选的问题，实现了接近全局最优的效用性能，同时显著降低了消息开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在缺乏集中式路由控制的分布式智能体物联网（IoA）环境中，将任务路由到能力匹配的执行器面临两大挑战：目的地非预定性以及智能体仅具有有限的本地服务视图。传统的"先发现后转发"方法在执行网络转发前就选定执行器，因此无法在下游发现更优候选时支持重新选择，这促使研究者设计一种能够将执行器发现与重选集成到转发过程中的新型路由机制。

Method: 本文提出语义势场路由（SPFR）算法，将每个本地语义转发信息库（FIB）中可见的执行器表示为任务条件的语义势能源。该算法引入两个关键机制：1）效用值设定势场强度，跳数距离引起指数衰减；2）在每一跳，转发智能体重新计算势场、重选主导执行器并向其转发任务一跳。研究者还在任务一致的冻结FIB条件下，证明了环路自由性和有限跳终止性，并推导了在有限可见性下相对于全可见性目标的显式加性误差界限。

Result: 在真实世界拓扑上的大量仿真表明，SPFR在实现效用方面接近分布式效用贪婪路由和请求触发的全局发现方法的性能水平，同时使用更少的转发跳数和大幅减少的请求触发消息数量。此外，算法在网络和服务动态变化下保持鲁棒性。理论分析方面，在特定条件下成功证明了算法的环路自由性和有限跳终止性，并给出了误差界限的理论保证。

Conclusion: SPFR成功解决了分布式IoA环境下的任务路由挑战，通过将执行器发现与重选集成到逐跳转发中，避免了传统方法在下游无法重选的限制。该方法在保证理论性质（环路自由、有限终止、误差界限）的同时，实现了接近最优效用的实际性能，并显著降低了通信开销，为分布式智能体网络中的任务路由提供了高效可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: In a distributed Internet of Agents (IoA) without centralized routing control, routing tasks to capability-matched executors is challenging because destinations are not predetermined and agents have bounded local service views. Discover-then-forward approaches, by contrast, select an executor before network forwarding and therefore do not directly support reselection when additional candidates become visible downstream. We introduce Semantic Potential Field Routing (SPFR), a distributed IoA routing algorithm that integrates executor discovery and reselection into hop-by-hop forwarding. SPFR represents each executor visible in a local semantic forwarding information base (FIB) as a task-conditioned semantic potential source, with utility setting its strength and hop distance inducing exponential attenuation. At each hop, the forwarding agent recomputes these potentials, reselects the dominant executor, and forwards the task one hop toward it. Under task-consistent frozen-FIB conditions, we prove loop freedom and finite-hop termination and derive an explicit additive error bound under bounded visibility relative to the full-visibility objective. Extensive simulations on real-world topologies show that SPFR approaches the realized utility of distributed utility-greedy routing and request-triggered global discovery while using fewer forwarding hops and substantially fewer request-triggered messages, and remains robust under network and service dynamics.

</details>


### [178] [Lightweight AI for UAV-Mounted RIS: An Overview](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25402)
*Sherief Hashima,Kohei Hatano,Eiji Takimoto,Mohamed Rihan,Basem. M. Elhalawany,Hamada Rizk*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文综述了适用于无人机搭载可重构智能表面（UAV-RIS）系统的轻量级AI技术，包括强化学习、元学习、联邦学习、多臂赌博机和能耗感知优化等方法，以解决联合优化中的高计算复杂度问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UAV-RIS系统在6G网络中具有重要应用前景，但联合优化无人机轨迹、RIS相位配置和资源分配会带来高计算复杂度，这与无人机平台严格的能耗和机载处理约束相矛盾，因此需要研究轻量级AI技术来解决这一挑战。

Method: 本文对适用于UAV-RIS系统的轻量级AI技术进行了全面综述，包括强化学习（RL）、元学习、联邦学习（FL）、多臂赌博机（MAB）和能耗感知优化等方法，并提出了详细的分类体系和对比分析。

Result: 提供了现有工作的详细分类法和比较分析，突出了计算-能耗权衡，识别了可扩展、节能的空载智能表面的开放研究挑战，并通过案例研究展示了MAB方案对UAV-RIS系统吞吐量和能效的影响。

Conclusion: 轻量级AI技术为UAV-RIS系统提供了实用的解决方案，能够平衡计算复杂度与能耗约束，为未来可扩展、节能的空载智能表面系统指明了研究方向。

Abstract: Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAV)-mounted Reconfigurable Intelligent Surfaces (RIS) have emerged as a promising architecture for enhancing wireless coverage, spectral efficiency, and energy performance in 6G networks. By combining programmable electromagnetic wave manipulation with aerial mobility, UAV-RIS systems enable dynamic blockage mitigation, adaptive beamforming, and flexible deployment across terrestrial, maritime, and satellite-integrated environments. However, joint optimization of UAV trajectory, RIS phase configuration, and resource allocation incurs high computational complexity, which is incompatible with the strict energy and onboard processing constraints of UAV platforms. Lightweight AI techniques offer practical solutions to this challenge. Hence, this paper provides a comprehensive overview of lightweight AI techniques for UAV-mounted RIS systems, including Reinforcement Learning (RL), meta-learning, Federated Learning (FL), Multi-Armed Bandits (MAB), and energy-aware optimization. We present a detailed taxonomy and comparative analysis of existing work, highlight computational-energy trade-offs, and identify open research challenges for scalable, energy-efficient airborne intelligent surfaces. Furthermore, we present a case study demonstrating the effect of MAB schemes on throughput and energy efficiency in UAV-mounted RIS.

</details>


### [179] [E2-Conditioned Finite-Horizon Effective Capacity for Public-Safety MCX over Shared O-RAN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25442)
*Jingqing Wang,Wenchi Cheng*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个有限时域有效容量(FH-EC)框架，用于解决共享O-RAN网络中公共安全关键任务服务的服务保障问题。该框架考虑了E2观测状态、控制执行延迟和相关性感知的连接多样性，在有限任务时间窗口内提供可执行的服务能力表征，并通过自适应连接选择提升了MCX服务的支持能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在共享O-RAN网络中支持公共安全关键任务服务面临三大挑战：(1)需要在有限的突发事件时间窗口内保证服务质量，同时普通移动业务竞争相同资源；(2)异构E2域暴露不同的观测、控制动作和执行延迟；(3)现有RAN关键性能指标是回顾性的，而传统有效容量理论只适用于渐进稳态，忽略了初始E2观测条件和有限选择-执行瞬态过程。

Method: 首先建立了一个有限时域O-RAN MCX服务模型，整合了E2观测网络状态、控制执行延迟和相关性感知的连接多样性。基于该模型推导了FH-EC公式，用于表征有限任务时间窗口内的可执行服务能力。进一步将FH-EC转换为置信度校准的能力配置文件，并开发了包含合约认证、共享资源保护和FH-EC驱动配置文件选择的MCX编排框架，部署在近实时RAN智能控制器(RIC)上。

Result: 通过MATLAB/ns-3联合仿真评估验证了：(1)预测的短时域状态和执行效应；(2)自适应连接选择在O-DU降级条件下能够改善MCX支持能力；(3)同时满足配置的多QoS要求和非MCX业务保护要求。

Conclusion: 所提出的FH-EC框架成功解决了共享O-RAN中公共安全MCX服务的有限时域服务保障问题。通过考虑E2初始条件、执行延迟和相关感知的连接多样性，该框架能够提供置信度校准的服务能力表征，并实现了自适应连接选择优化，为共享网络环境下的关键任务服务编排提供了有效的技术方案。

Abstract: Supporting public-safety Mission Critical Services (MCX) over a shared Open radio access network (O-RAN) requires service assurance over finite incident horizons, while ordinary mobile traffic competes for the same resources and heterogeneous E2 domains expose different observations, control actions, and actuation latencies. Existing RAN key performance indicators are retrospective, whereas conventional effective capacity characterizes an asymptotic stationary regime and therefore suppresses both the initial E2-observed condition and the finite selection-to-actuation transient. To solve this problem, in this paper we develop an E2-conditioned finite-horizon effective capacity (FH-EC) framework for public-safety MCX over shared O-RAN. Specifically, we first establish a finite-horizon O-RAN MCX-based service model that incorporates E2-observed network states, control actuation latency, and correlation-aware connectivity diversity. Based on this model, we derive an FH-EC formulation that characterizes the executable service capability within a finite mission horizon. Furthermore, we transform FH-EC into confidence-calibrated capability profiles and develop an MCX orchestration framework with contract certification, shared-resource protection, and FH-EC-driven profile selection at the Near-RT RAN Intelligent Controller (RIC). Coupled MATLAB/ns-3 evaluations demonstrate the predicted short-horizon state and actuation effects and show that adaptive connectivity selection improves MCX supportability under O-DU degradation while satisfying the configured multi-QoS and non-MCX protection requirements.

</details>


### [180] [AERIS: Offline Policy Improvement for Multi-UAV Integrated Sensing and Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25477)
*Ziyuan Wang,Yifan Sui,Wei Wei,Wenjie Xin,Zekai Zhang,Xiangwang Hou,Xiao-Ping,Zhang*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出AERIS框架和STAR-CRDT算法，实现多无人机ISAC系统的离线策略优化，在无需在线试错的情况下显著提升通信、感知和安全性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有UAV-ISAC控制方法存在两大局限：传统优化方法需要反复求解全局非凸问题，计算开销大；在线强化学习依赖高风险的试错飞行，可能导致感知丢失或碰撞事故。需要一种能从历史飞行数据中学习、避免在线风险的方法。

Method: 提出AERIS离线策略改进框架，采用集中训练-分散执行架构：训练阶段利用全局历史信息评估团队效果，执行阶段各无人机仅基于局部历史决策。设计STAR-CRDT算法，实现支持集感知的局部动作修正，仅将可信改进提炼到分散执行器中。

Result: STAR-CRDT相比最强基线将ISAC目标回报提升29.3%；通信总速率提升3.4%，感知通过率提升4.8%，感知裕度提升69.1%，碰撞风险事件减少54.2%；在OpenStreetMap构建的未见真实道路地图上仍获得最佳回报。

Conclusion: AERIS框架和STAR-CRDT算法成功实现了多无人机ISAC的离线安全学习，在保证飞行安全的前提下显著提升了通信和感知性能，具有良好的泛化能力，为动态多UAV-ISAC控制提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV)-enabled integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) is a promising 6G paradigm, but dynamic multi-UAV ISAC control must jointly balance communication quality, sensing reliability, and flight safety under stochastic mobility. Existing optimization methods often require repeated global non-convex solving, while online reinforcement learning (RL) depends on risky trial-and-error flights that may cause sensing loss or collision-risk events.
  This paper proposes AERIS, an offline policy improvement framework for multi-UAV ISAC. AERIS learns from fixed flight logs under centralized training and decentralized execution, so each UAV acts from local histories while training uses logged global information to assess team-level effects. We further design STAR-CRDT, an offline multi-agent RL algorithm that performs support-aware local action rectification and distills only trusted improvements into the decentralized actor. We prove an offline-support policy improvement guarantee. Experiments show that STAR-CRDT improves the main ISAC objective return by 29.3% over the strongest baseline. It further improves communication sum rate, sensing pass rate, and sensing margin by 3.4%, 4.8%, and 69.1%, while reducing collision-risk events by 54.2%. On unseen real-road maps built from OpenStreetMap data, STAR-CRDT still obtains the best return.

</details>


### [181] [Stochastic End-to-End Latency Modeling of the IoT-Edge-Cloud Continuum: Impact of Jitter and Traffic Variability on Deterministic Service Provisioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25658)
*Keyvan Aghababaiyan,Javier Gozalvez,Baldomero Coll-Perales*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文研究了时间敏感性变动（到达时间抖动和流量变动）对IoT-边缘-云连续体端到端确定服务级别的影响。通过队列理论模型分析，发现：严格时延服务宜本地执行；边缘卸载适用于良好网络和本地高负载；云执行对流量变动最敏感；卸载服务对流量变动比抖动更敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 6G将整合通信与计算能力形成IoT-边缘-云连续体，支持跨连续体的工作负载分布。为支持时间敏感服务，需同时控制通信与计算时延。到达时间抖动和流量变动是两个关键的时间变动源，会影响数据生成、传输和处理的时序，导致时延不确定性增加。因此需要研究这些随机时间变动如何影响端到端确定性服务级别的保障能力。

Method: 提出了一种新颖的基于队列论的端到端时延模型，该模型开源发布。模型联合捕获计算与通信时延，刻画完整的端到端时延分布（包括尾部时延），适用于IoT-边缘-云连续体架构。

Result: 主要发现：（1）严格时延期限和大计算需求的服务对时间变动更敏感，本地执行为首选；（2）宽松期限的服务在本地或边缘执行更具韧性；（3）边缘卸载在良好蜂窝连接和增加的本地处理负载下有益；（4）云执行因额外通信时延对流量变动更敏感；（5）卸载服务因更高通信时延对流量变动比对抖动更敏感。

Conclusion: 有效的服务卸载必须联合考虑服务需求和时间变动源才能保障确定性服务级别。研究强调了不同服务特性和变动源对卸载决策的影响，为6G连续体中确定性服务的部署提供了理论指导。

Abstract: 6G will integrate communication and computing capabilities in a IoT-edge-cloud continuum, enabling nodes to distribute workloads across the continuum. To support time-sensitive services, both communications and computing latencies must be controlled. Two key sources of temporal variability are arrival-time jitter and traffic variability. They can both impact the timing at which data is generated, transmitted and processed, and the resulting fluctuations can propagate throughout the continuum, increasing latency uncertainty. This paper studies the impact of stochastic temporal variability on the ability to support end-to-end deterministic service levels across the continuum. To this end, we present a novel queueing-based end-to-end latency model for the continuum, which we openly release. The model jointly captures computing and communication latency, and characterizes the complete end-to-end latency distribution, including tail latency. Our analysis shows that services with stringent latency deadlines and larger computing demands are more sensitive to temporal variabilities, making local execution the preferred option. In contrast, services with more relaxed deadlines are more resilient to temporal variabilities when executed locally or at the edge despite higher average and tail latencies. Edge offloading is beneficial under good cellular connectivity and increasing local processing workloads, whereas cloud execution is more sensitive to traffic variabilities because of the additional communication latency. Our analysis also shows that services offloaded are more sensitive to traffic variability than jitter due to higher communication latencies. These findings highlight that effective service offloading must jointly consider service requirements and sources of temporal variability to guarantee deterministic service levels.

</details>


### [182] [Generative AI-Enabled Mission-Aware Radio Orchestration for RIS-Assisted LEO Satellite ISAC Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25803)
*Fitsum Debebe Tilahun,Chung G. Kang*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于生成式AI的LEO卫星网络无线资源编排框架，利用大语言模型将任务需求映射为结构化策略，结合传统物理层优化实现任务自适应的资源重配置，在保持可行性的同时提升了语义理解能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具有集成感知与通信（ISAC）能力的低地球轨道（LEO）卫星网络需要根据运营商目标的变化重新调整无线资源，而现有系统缺乏从自然语言任务描述到可执行策略的高效映射机制，亟需一种能够理解语义并快速响应任务变化的编排框架。

Method: 提出混合时间尺度的编排框架：在任务时间尺度上，利用大语言模型（LLM）将任务语义映射为包含通信、感知和公平性权重、QoS阈值、功率分配指导及求解器初始化的结构化策略；在信道时间尺度上，通过确定性验证和物理层优化（波束、功率、RIS配置）确保可行性并实现策略落地。对比了零样本（LLM-ZS）和上下文学习（LLM-ICL）两种操作模式。

Result: 在未见过的组合任务上，LLM-ZS和LLM-ICL分别达到91.7%和94.4%的优先级排序准确率，ICL主要改善数值校准；两者在下游无线性能上无统计显著差异，因为都能恢复决定可行动作的硬约束；直接优化活跃波束和RIS相位时，交替优化保持了定性排序。

Conclusion: LLM-ZS适合作为低上下文需求的默认方案，LLM-ICL适用于需要精细校准的语义复杂任务；生成式AI能够增强下一代无线编排而无需替代确保可行性的物理层优化，二者互补形成有效解决方案。

Abstract: Mission-adaptive low-Earth-orbit (LEO) satellite networks with integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) must retarget radio resources as operator goals change. To enable this adaptation from flexible operator language, we develop a generative-AI-enabled radio-orchestration framework in which a large language model (LLM) maps each mission into a structured policy comprising communication, sensing, and fairness weights, mandatory quality-of-service thresholds, power-allocation guidance, and solver initialization. Deterministic validation and physical-layer optimization then enforce feasibility and realize the policy through beam, power, and reconfigurable intelligent surface (RIS) configuration. This mixed-timescale design uses generative AI for semantic adaptation at the mission timescale while retaining conventional wireless optimization at the faster channel timescale. We compare zero-shot (LLM-ZS) and in-context (LLM-ICL) operation on familiar and held-out compositional missions. On held-out instructions, LLM-ZS and LLM-ICL achieve $91.7\%$ and $94.4\%$ priority-order accuracy, respectively, with ICL mainly improving numerical calibration. Their downstream radio-performance difference is statistically unresolved because both usually recover the hard constraints that determine admissible actions. Accordingly, LLM-ZS is the low-context default, while LLM-ICL is useful for semantically difficult missions requiring finer calibration. Explicit alternating optimization preserves the qualitative ordering when active beams and RIS phases are optimized directly. The results show how generative AI can enhance next-generation radio orchestration without replacing feasibility-critical physical-layer optimization.

</details>


### [183] [Open-Source 5G RAN Platforms: A Dual Perspective on Performance and Capabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25820)
*Maria Katarine Santana Barbosa,Iasmin Gomes,Vinícius Melo,Kelvin Lopes Dias*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文对两大开源5G RAN平台(OAI和srsRAN)进行了全面的比较评估，涵盖功能特性、部署灵活性以及多种实际应用场景下的性能表现，填补了现有研究中缺乏多维度对比评估的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对开源5G RAN平台(OAI和srsRAN)的评估研究存在明显局限性：大多局限于单独的测试平台评估、端到端研究或部分对比，缺乏考虑不同软件定义无线电(SDR)、RAN配置、用户应用和真实UE数量的综合评估。为了解决这一研究空白，需要开展更全面的比较评估。

Method: 采用定性与定量相结合的评估方法。首先进行支持功能和部署灵活性的定性评估；随后开展定量性能分析，包括：无线电资源控制(RRC)建立过程、理论数据速率合规性测试，以及具有不同服务质量(QoS)需求的实际应用工作负载测试。具体测试场景包括：视频点播(VoD)用于数据速率评估，直播流媒体(LS)和云游戏(CG)作为延迟敏感型应用测试。

Result: 通过全面的评估框架，研究为两个开源5G RAN平台的能力提供了综合视角，包括功能支持情况、部署灵活性表现，以及在无线电资源控制、数据速率合规性和实际应用工作负载方面的性能数据。

Conclusion: 该研究为业界和学术界选择合适的开源5G RAN平台提供了有价值的参考依据，有助于快速原型开发、低成本私有5G网络部署，以及未来平台改进方向的确定。

Abstract: Open-source implementations of the fifth generation (5G) Radio Access Network (RAN), such as OpenAirInterface (OAI) and srsRAN, have become increasingly relevant for industry and academic research, rapid prototyping, and low-cost private 5G deployments. The state of the art presents different evaluation scenarios for both platforms; however, these are often limited to individual testbed evaluations, end-to-end studies, or partial comparisons between them. In particular, there is a lack of evaluations considering different Software-Defined Radios (SDRs), RAN configurations, user applications, and numbers of real UEs. To address this gap, this paper presents a comparative evaluation of OAI and srsRAN. The study includes a qualitative assessment of supported features and deployment flexibility, followed by a quantitative performance analysis covering radio resource control setup procedures, compliance with theoretical data rates, and real application workloads with diverse quality of service requirements. Specifically, we consider Video on Demand (VoD) for data rate evaluation, Live Streaming (LS) and Cloud Gaming (CG) as latency-sensitive applications. These workloads provide a comprehensive view of each platform capability.

</details>


### [184] [SlimTCP: It's fast, but not because it's slim](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25834)
*Mihai Drosi Caju,Costin Raiciu*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文研究在数据中心网络环境中，通过简化TCP/IP协议栈代码库来提升其性能的可能性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据中心网络对网络协议栈性能有更高要求。现有TCP/IP协议栈代码复杂度高，可能限制了性能表现，因此需要探索代码简化是否能带来性能提升。

Method: 聚焦于简化TCP/IP协议栈的代码库，研究代码简化与性能之间的关系。通过精简代码结构和逻辑，验证简化是否能提高协议栈的整体性能。

Result: 研究了代码简化对TCP/IP协议栈性能的影响，验证了简化代码库能够提升协议栈在数据中心网络环境中的性能表现。

Conclusion: 简化TCP/IP协议栈的代码库是一种有效的性能优化方法，可以为数据中心网络环境下的高性能网络通信提供支持。这一发现为未来协议栈设计和优化提供了重要参考。

Abstract: In this paper, the authors explore the possibility of improving the performance of TCP/IP stacks in the context of data-center networks. This paper will focus particularly on the claim that simplifying the code-base of the stack increases its performance.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [185] [Probabilistic Performance Analysis of Parallel Signature Search Strategies in Multi-Level Tree Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25087)
*Jingwei Li,Thomas G. Robertazzi*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了一个概率框架，能在文件读取前预测五种搜索策略的完成时间，涵盖从顺序扫描到全树并行，为分层分布式搜索提供快速准确的性能预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分层分布式搜索（如分布式索引遍历、深度包检测和序列比对）需要决定采用何种并行策略：顺序扫描、子树并行或全树并行。现有分析仅部分回答此问题，且对于多签名文件需要运行时信息，无法提前预测性能。

Method: 构建概率预测框架：将节点扫描时间建模为签名存在的混合分布，层时间建模为顺序统计量，并行子树扫描使用极值理论；当签名计数已知时，采用生成函数处理容量约束下的占用问题。每个性能公式附带精确性标签（精确、插件式、渐近或界限），并通过蒙特卡洛模拟量化近似误差。

Result: 框架能在文件读取前提供先验完成时间预测，计算成本可忽略（设计示例在毫秒内评估完成），预测具有明确的精度范围。多核原型验证了全树、层和子树级并行之间的粗粒度分离，但同步开销可能消除相近策略间的预测差异。

Conclusion: 所提框架实现了低成本、高精度的先验完成时间预测，可支持后续资源成本优化，为分层分布式搜索的并行策略选择提供理论指导。

Abstract: Hierarchical distributed search, locating a data pattern, or signature, across a tree-structured collection of files, underlies distributed index traversal, deep packet inspection and sequence alignment. A practitioner must decide how much parallelism to employ: scan each layer sequentially, fan out within subtrees, or launch the whole tree at once. Existing analyses answer this only partially: they characterize every node by the statistics of a signature-holding file and, for multi-signature files, need quantities revealed only at run time. We develop a probabilistic framework predicting the completion time of five search strategies, spanning sequential to full-tree parallelism, before any file is read. Node scan times are modeled as a mixture over signature presence, layer times as order statistics, and parallel subtree scans by extreme-value arguments; when signature counts are known, occupancy under capacity constraints is treated by generating functions. Each performance formula carries an exactness label: exact (or exact-in-regime), plug-in, asymptotic or bound, with each approximation quantified against Monte Carlo simulation and its regime identified. A multicore prototype reproduces the coarse separation between full-tree, layer- and subtree-level parallelism, but shows that synchronization overhead can erase the predicted separation between close strategies. The framework delivers a priori completion-time predictions with explicit accuracy regimes and negligible computational cost, the design example evaluated in under a millisecond; these timing models can support subsequent resource-cost optimization.

</details>


### [186] [Hierarchical Shared Memory-Aware Optimization for TRSM on GPU Platforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25469)
*Xinzhe Chen,Haowei Li,Lijuan Hu,Wenjing Ma,Fangfang Liu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出HSMA-TRSM优化框架，通过分层共享内存感知策略解决GPU上左下三角TRSM的实现挑战，在NVIDIA A100/H800和海光DCU Z100上实现最高2.05x（对比cuBLAS）和2.06x（对比rocBLAS）的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 左下三角TRSM的GPU高效实现面临两大挑战：(1)前向代入引入严格的行依赖关系；(2)共享内存容量不足以容纳双精度复数等宽数据类型的两个操作矩阵。现有实现仍存在优化空间，需要针对不同规模问题设计专门优化策略。

Method: 针对小规模问题(m,n≤64)：设计流水线计算-内存重叠机制，采用循环展开和指令重排序；提出双线程组七阶段流水线策略解决双精度复数类型的共享内存约束。针对大规模问题：引入对角块解耦优化，将对角块求逆的共享内存占用降至O(IB)，支持基于矩阵规模和硬件特性的自适应块大小选择。建立基于离线性能分析和在线查找的编译时配置选择框架，零运行时开销选择最优块大小。

Result: 在NVIDIA A100、H800和海光DCU Z100上评估，HSMA-TRSM相比cuBLAS和rocBLAS分别实现最高2.05x和2.06x加速。双精度复数小规模问题（共享内存受限场景）和大实数型问题（自适应分块改善GEMM主导更新）中收益最显著。

Conclusion: HSMA-TRSM框架成功解决了GPU上TRSM的共享内存约束和行依赖问题，针对不同规模问题采用差异化优化策略。成熟厂商内核在某些场景下优化余地有限，而在共享内存受限和GEMM更新密集场景中本方法优势明显。

Abstract: Triangular Solve with Multiple Right-hand Sides (TRSM) is a fundamental BLAS Level-3 operation that underpins LU/Cholesky decomposition, sparse direct solvers, and matrix inversion. In the left-side lower-triangular case studied in this paper, efficient GPU implementation remains challenging because forward substitution introduces strict row-wise dependencies, and shared memory is too scarce to hold both operand matrices for wide data types such as double complex. This paper presents HSMA-TRSM, a hierarchical shared memory-aware optimization framework for left-side lower-triangular TRSM on NVIDIA A100, NVIDIA H800, and Hygon DCU Z100 accelerators. For the small-scale regime (m,n<=64), we design a pipelined compute-memory overlap mechanism through loop unrolling and instruction reordering, and propose a dual thread-group seven-stage pipeline strategy to address shared memory constraints for double complex types. For large-scale problems, we introduce a diagonal block decoupling optimization with an O(IB)shared-memory footprint for diagonal block inversion, enabling adaptive block size selection based on matrix scale and hardware characteristics. A compile-time configuration selection framework based on offline profiling and online lookup selects the optimal block size per platform with zero runtime overhead. Evaluated on NVIDIA A100, H800, and Hygon DCU Z100, HSMA-TRSM achieves peak speedups of 2.05xover cuBLAS and 2.06xover rocBLAS. The gains are strongest in shared-memory-constrained double-complex small cases and in large real-type cases where adaptive blocking improves GEMM-dominated updates, while mature vendor kernels leave less optimization headroom in some regimes.

</details>


### [187] [psRL: Efficient Training for Agentic AI via Training-Time Prefix Sharing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25683)
*Mianjie Yu,Zizhao Mo,Huanyu Qu,Zhirong Qian,Huanle Xu,Cen Li,Zifeng Zhao,Zhi Zhou,Jinhua Zhou,Jun Xie,Chengzhong Xu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 针对智能体AI训练中更新阶段成为瓶颈的问题，提出psRL系统，通过利用训练样本间的前缀冗余，实现了最高5.2倍的吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代智能体AI训练中，由于树结构和逐步强化学习等采样策略大幅增加了训练样本量，系统瓶颈从rollout阶段转移到update阶段。生产环境轨迹分析显示训练样本间存在大量前缀冗余，这提供了一个新的优化机会。

Method: 提出psRL（prefix sharing for RL）训练系统，利用更新阶段的全局可见性和数据不可变性特性。引入两种新颖的前缀共享机制，实现跨GPU worker的灵活、细粒度工作负载分配，同时优化前缀重用和负载均衡。实现新型KV缓存管理器，支持可适应的块大小分配和动态KV缓存。

Result: 在生产环境轨迹数据上的评估表明，psRL在吞吐量方面相比现有系统提升高达5.2倍，同时最大化内存利用率并保持高前缀命中率。

Conclusion: 通过有效利用训练样本间的前缀冗余，psRL成功解决了智能体AI训练中更新阶段的性能瓶颈问题，显著提升了系统吞吐量，为大规模智能体AI训练提供了高效的解决方案。

Abstract: In modern agentic AI training, the system bottleneck is shifting from rollout to update. Emerging sampling strategies such as tree-structured and step-wise RL greatly increase training sample volume while incurring relatively low marginal rollout cost, causing the update phase to dominate the end-to-end execution time. Crucially, this shift exposes a new optimization opportunity, as production traces reveal substantial prefix redundancy across training samples. In this paper, we propose psRL (prefix sharing for RL), a new training system for agentic AI designed to exploit prefix redundancy among training samples. Leveraging the global visibility and data immutability inherent to the update phase, psRL achieves efficient workload scheduling and memory management for distributed training. Specifically, psRL introduces two novel prefix-sharing mechanisms that enable flexible, fine-grained workload distribution across GPU workers, simultaneously optimizing prefix reuse and achieving load balancing. Moreover, psRL implements a new underlying KV cache manager that facilitates adaptable block-size allocation and dynamic KV caching, maximizing memory utilization while maintaining a high prefix hit rate. Evaluations using production traces demonstrate that psRL outperforms existing systems by up to 5.2x in throughput. The source code will be publicly available soon.

</details>


### [188] [An Oversubscription and Service Pricing Exploitation-Based Profit Maximization Framework for Industry Cloud Resource Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25712)
*Deepika Saxena,Ashutosh Kumar Singh*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于资源超售和差异化定价的工业云资源管理框架，通过机器学习预测和聚类分组，显著降低了电力消耗和成本，同时提高了资源利用率和利润。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业云提供商面临资源利用率低、运营成本高、利润受限等挑战。由于用户VM资源利用率往往低于分配量，导致资源浪费和电力成本增加。需要一种能够预测资源需求、优化VM部署、并通过差异化定价模型提高盈利能力的管理框架。

Method: 提出自适应集成机器学习预测模型，基于用户VM历史资源利用率进行主动预测；采用模糊C均值聚类将具有相似预测资源使用模式的VM分组；引入两类云服务定价模型（延迟敏感模型和尽力而为模型），根据用户请求类型选择合适的VM部署策略；通过提前确定所需VM数量和配置来优化资源分配。

Result: 在两个基准VM追踪数据集上的实验表明：电力费用降低55.56%；功耗降低高达60.7%；活跃服务器数量减少51%；资源利用率提升高达60%；利润提高51.18%。相比现有方法表现显著优越。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架成功实现了工业云提供商的盈利能力和运营效率最大化目标。通过资源超售策略与差异化定价模型的结合，有效解决了资源浪费和电力成本问题，为工业云资源管理提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: This article proposed a novel industry cloud resource management framework that exploits resource oversubscription and heterogeneous service pricing models to maximize profitability and operational efficiency for industry cloud providers. The framework proposes an adaptive ensemble machine learning driven prediction model for proactive estimation of resource utilization of Virtual Machines (VM)s based on previous resource utilization of respective users' VMs to minimize resource wastage due to oversubscription by them. Accordingly, the VMs having similar predicted resource usage are grouped using Fuzzy C means clustering. This helps to determine the required number of VMs with specific configuration to be deployed before executing user requests. Concurrently, the framework incorporates two distinct categories of cloud service pricing models, namely the Delay Sensitive Model and the Best-Effort Model. Accordingly, the user requests are classified and executed by selecting the most suitable VMs, with the goal of maximizing revenue and reducing electricity costs in cloud data centers (CDCs). Experimental simulation and comparison against state-of-the-art methods, using two benchmark VM traces, validates the performance of proposed framework. It significantly reduces electricity bills by 55.56 percentage, power consumption and active servers by up to 60.7 percentage and 51 percentage, respectively, while improving resource utilization and profits by up to 60 percentage and 51.18 percentage, respectively

</details>


### [189] [A Spatially-Aware Publish-Subscribe Middleware for IoT Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25728)
*Philipp Ungrund,Kurt Rothermel,Sukanya Bhowmik*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种扩展主题发布-订阅模型的方法，通过集成空间过滤和路由功能，为CPS和IoT提供空间感知支持。该方法基于MQTT5协议实现，无需修改现有客户端，实验验证了系统的高效性和实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间和邻近感知是网络物理系统(CPS)和物联网(IoT)中高效通信的关键使能因素。然而，缺乏适合的中间件支持和标准化的空间感知机制，显著限制了位置相关应用的普及性。

Method: 扩展基于主题的发布-订阅模型，集成空间过滤和路由能力。采用灵活的几何方法描述空间过滤器，通过几何世界模型与增强的主题代理协同工作，实现空间约束的消息传播。该方法基于MQTT5协议实现，不违反标准且无需修改现有客户端实现。

Result: 基于合成和真实数据的实验结果表明：系统具有强效率，代理端计算开销极小，在现有IoT设备上部署该方案具有实际可行性。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法成功解决了CPS和IoT中空间感知中间件支持不足的问题，在不破坏协议标准和兼容性的前提下，实现了高效的空间过滤和路由功能，为位置相关应用的普及提供了可行的技术方案。

Abstract: Spatial and proximity awareness are critical enablers for efficient communication in Cyber-Physical Systems (CPS) and the Internet of Things (IoT). However, the lack of suitable middleware support and standardized mechanisms for spatial awareness significantly limits the pervasiveness of location-dependent applications. In this paper, we present a novel approach that extends topic-based publish-subscribe, a dominant messaging model in this domain, with integrated spatial filtering and routing capabilities. Our approach leverages a highly flexible geometric method for describing spatial filters, enabling expressive and efficient spatial subscriptions. A geometric world model operates together with an enhanced topic-based broker to realize spatially constrained message dissemination. Importantly, this functionality is achieved using the MQTT5 protocol without violating the standard or requiring any modifications to existing client implementations. Experimental results based on both synthetic and real-world data demonstrate strong system efficiency, minimal computational overhead at the broker, and the practical feasibility of deploying the approach on existing IoT devices.

</details>


### [190] [REE-TM: Reliable and Energy-Efficient Traffic Management Model for Diverse Cloud Workloads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25747)
*Ashutosh Kumar Singh,Deepika Saxena,Volker Lindenstruth*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出REE-TM方法处理异构工作负载，结合TG-QNN工作量估计器和QBHO优化算法，在云环境中实现可靠性和能效提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云服务工作负载需求的多样性严重影响资源分配效率，现有文献忽视或弱化处理来自互联网服务用户的异构作业请求特征。

Method: REE-TM方法包含三个核心组件：(1)异构作业请求分类与执行节点选择机制；(2)Toffoli门量子神经网络(TG-QNN)工作量估计器，采用量子黑洞(QBHO)算法优化权重；(3)基于熵的流量状态分析和资源拥塞检测。通过预测工作量实现主动式资源管理。

Result: 在基准数据集上的仿真实验表明，与未采用REE-TM相比，该方法可靠性提升高达30.25%，能效提升高达23%，在资源竞争和性能退化场景下表现优异。

Conclusion: REE-TM通过结合量子神经网络和异构流量分类，有效解决了云环境中异构工作负载的资源管理问题，显著提升了可靠性和能效。

Abstract: Diversity of workload demands lays a critical impact on efficient resource allocation and management of cloud services. The existing literature has either weakly considered or overlooked the heterogeneous feature of job requests received from wide range of internet services users. To address this context, the proposed approach named Reliable and Energy Efficient Traffic Management (REE-TM) has exploited the diversity of internet traffic in terms of variation in resource demands and expected complexity. Specifically, REE-TM incorporates categorization of heterogeneous job requests and executes them by selecting the most admissible virtual node (a software-defined instance such as a virtual machine or container) and physical node (an actual hardware server or compute host) within the cloud infrastructure. To deal with resource-contention-based resource failures and performance degradation, a novel workload estimator 'Toffoli Gate-based Quantum Neural Network' (TG-QNN) is proposed, wherein learning process or interconnection weights optimization is achieved using Quantum version of BlackHole (QBHO) algorithm. The proactively estimated workload is used to compute entropy of the upcoming internet traffic with various traffic states analysis for detection of probable resource-congestion. REE-TM is extensively evaluated through simulations using a benchmark dataset and compared with optimal and without REE-TM versions. The performance evaluation and comparison of REE-TM with measured significant metrics reveal its effectiveness in assuring higher reliability by up to 30.25% and energy-efficiency by up to 23% as compared without REE-TM.

</details>


### [191] [Quantum Blackhole Learning-Optimized Hadamard Neural Network Model for Dynamic Resource Reservation in Industry Clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25754)
*Deepika Saxena,Hari Mohan Gaur,Ashutosh Kumar Singh,Anand Mohan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于量子黑洞优化的哈达玛神经网络（QB-HNN）用于云环境下的工作负载预测，相比现有LSTM和EQNN方法，预测误差分别降低了36.36%和22.83%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器学习模型学习能力有限，难以准确预测具有突发资源需求变化的高维多样化工作负载，导致工业云中出现过度功耗和资源管理问题，亟需更强大的预测模型。

Method: 提出QB-HNN模型，将工作负载信息转换为量子比特，通过包含哈达玛门激活函数的深度量子比特神经元网络进行传播以实现叠加态学习；引入量子黑洞双相优化（QB-BiO）算法训练和优化量子比特神经权重。

Result: 在三类异构云工作负载的六个基准数据集上进行了综合评估，相比现有LSTM和EQNN方法，预测误差分别降低了36.36%和22.83%，验证了该模型在各种工作负载下的优越预测性能。

Conclusion: 所提出的QB-HNN模型结合量子力学的计算效率和神经网络的学习能力，显著提升了工作负载预测准确性，为工业云的主动资源预留提供了可靠的技术支撑。

Abstract: Accurate workload prediction and proactive resource reservation are crucial for industry clouds. However, the conventional machine learning (CML) models with limited learning capabilities often fail to predict diverse, high-dimensional workloads with sudden changes in resource demand, leading to excessive power consumption and resource management issues. In this context, this article proposes a novel Hadamard neural network with quantum blackhole (QB-HNN) optimization. This model combines the computational efficiency of quantum mechanics with the persuasive learning capability of neural networks (NNs). The workload information is transformed into qubits and propagated via a deep network of qubit neurons comprising a Hadamard-gated activation function to fetch superposition within the QB-HNN model for intuitive pattern learning. Furthermore, a novel quantum blackhole biphase optimization (QB-BiO) algorithm is introduced to train and optimize qubit neural weights. The performance of the proposed model is comprehensively evaluated and compared with five state-of-the-art approaches using six benchmark datasets of three heterogeneous varieties of cloud workloads. The prediction accuracy achieved for an extensive range of workloads confirms its influential performance by minimizing the prediction error up to 36.36% and 22.83% over existing LSTM- and EQNN-based prediction approaches, respectively.

</details>


### [192] [Scalable Multi-GPU Simulation of 3D Multicellular Growth with RNN-Based Workload Balancing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25890)
*Matvey Moisseyev,Huijing Du,Dandan Zheng,Chi Zhang,Hongfeng Yu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种面向3D多细胞生长模拟的可扩展多GPU框架，通过GPU加速和RNN负载均衡控制器，将交互计算加速约1000倍，并将全局负载不均衡度从11.3%降至3.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于亚细胞单元模型的多细胞生长模拟能够捕捉复杂的组织发育过程，但其单元级别的相互作用带来了巨大的计算开销。同时，细胞的运动、生长和分裂会持续重塑空间工作负载分布，导致初始平衡的分区随时间推移变得低效。

Method: 提出了一种可扩展的多GPU框架，结合了GPU加速、空间分箱、区域分解和工作负载感知分区技术。核心创新是引入了一个基于RNN的负载均衡控制器，该控制器观察最近的每秩执行时间和分区状态，学习对反应式边界调整规则的残差修正。控制器在负载均衡环的可微分代理中离线训练，使用随机化的工作负载动态，无需测量执行轨迹进行训练。

Result: GPU加速结合空间分箱将交互计算比串行CPU基线加速约三个数量级。RNN引导的负载均衡将平均全局不均衡度从静态分区的11.3%降低至3.5%，相比静态分区降低端到端运行时间9.0%，相比反应式基线减少切片迁移7.7倍。

Conclusion: 历史感知控制可以在改善工作负载平衡的同时避免不必要的重新分区，证明了该框架在处理空间和时间上演化的工作负载方面的有效性，为复杂的多细胞生长模拟提供了高效的计算解决方案。

Abstract: Detailed multicellular growth simulations based on subcellular element models (SEMs) can capture complex tissue development, but their element-level interactions impose substantial computational cost. This work presents a scalable multi-GPU framework for 3D multicellular growth simulation that combines GPU acceleration, spatial binning, domain decomposition, and workload-aware partitioning. Cell movement, growth, and division continuously reshape the spatial workload distribution, causing initially balanced partitions to become inefficient over time. To address this, we introduce an RNN-based load-balancing controller that observes recent per-rank execution times and partition states and learns residual corrections to a reactive boundary-adjustment rule. The controller is trained offline in a differentiable surrogate of the load-balancing loop with randomized workload dynamics, requiring no measured execution traces for training. We evaluate the framework in terms of single-GPU acceleration, multi-GPU computation scaling, controller-level load-balancing behavior, and end-to-end simulation performance, with comparisons against static partitioning, reactive load balancing, and conventional time-series prediction baselines. A representative embryonic epidermal development use case further demonstrates the type of spatially and temporally evolving workload targeted by the framework. In our evaluation, GPU acceleration with spatial binning accelerates the interaction computation by roughly three orders of magnitude over a serial CPU baseline. RNN-guided load balancing reduces the mean global imbalance from 11.3% under static partitioning to 3.5%, lowers end-to-end runtime by 9.0% relative to static partitioning, and reduces slice migration by 7.7x compared with the reactive baseline, showing that history-aware control can improve workload balance while avoiding unnecessary repartitioning.

</details>


### [193] [Slasher: Power Flexibility for Cloud Datacenters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26021)
*Liuzixuan Lin,Fiodar Kazhamiaka,Alok Gautam Kumbhare,Chaojie Zhang,Jaylen Wang,Hassan Khan,Rodrigo L. Assis,Mariana Rodrigues,Kyle Woolcock,Nithish Mahalingam,Brijesh Warrier,Rodrigo Fonseca,Ricardo Bianchini*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了Slasher系统，这是一个用于Azure数据中心功率调节的通用系统，能够处理从单个机架到区域性电网事件的各种功率调制场景，在满足功率目标的同时最小化对托管工作负载的负面影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据中心消耗数兆瓦功率，经常遇到需要调节功率消耗的场景，包括数据中心基础设施故障、电网故障、电网服务等，这些场景在功率幅度、缩减范围、通知时间等方面需求各异，亟需一个统一的功率调节系统。

Method: 1) 分析主要功率调节场景，利用生产云数据中心数据表征功率缩减杠杆；2) 描述Slasher系统架构，将云数据中心功率调节控制问题形式化；3) 开发高保真数据中心模拟器；4) 提出工作负载影响模型；5) 基于模拟器和影响模型设计和评估功率控制算法。

Result: 成功构建并部署了Slasher系统，该系统能够协调数据中心资源，满足功率目标的同时最小化对托管工作负载的负面影响，适用于从单个机架到区域性多数据中心电网事件的广泛场景。

Conclusion: 通过系统性地分析功率调制场景、构建高保真模拟器和工作负载影响模型，Slasher为云数据中心功率调节提供了一个通用的解决方案框架，证明了在实际生产环境中有效处理多样化功率调节需求的可行性。

Abstract: Datacenters consume many megawatts of power, and regularly encounter scenarios that require modulating their power draw. These scenarios include datacenter infrastructure failures, power grid failures, grid services, and more, spanning a diverse range of requirements in terms of the power magnitude, the scope of the reduction, the notice time, and other dimensions. To address these scenarios, we have built Slasher, a general system for modulating the power of \azure datacenters to handle scenarios ranging from individual racks to regional multi-datacenter grid events. Slasher coordinates datacenter resources with the goal of meeting power targets while minimizing negative impact on hosted workloads.
  In this paper, we review the main power modulation scenarios, characterize the power reduction levers using data from production cloud datacenters, describe Slasher's system architecture, and formulate the cloud datacenter power modulation control problem. We also develop a high-fidelity datacenter simulator and propose a workload impact model, using them to design and evaluate power control algorithms.

</details>
